"""Step-1 v2 count implementation.

This preserves the old strict-pair/single activity ledger for auditability,
then adds a transparent propagation-count range over unresolved/one-to-many
cross-wall relationships. No threshold, filter, propagation classifier, or
physiological definition is changed.
"""
from itertools import combinations
import numpy as np
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.direction_identity_trial import compare_identity_without_direction
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.measurement.radial_events import _describe_arrival, _arrival_evidence, RadialWaveConfig


def retained_phase_fits(node,block,s,dt):
    """Fit signed phase peaks only where each section has one unambiguous peak."""
    fits=[];availability=[]
    for sign in (1,-1):
        peaks=[[p['peak_s'] for p in node['profile'][int(c)] if p['sign']==sign]
               for c in block]
        complete=all(len(values)==1 for values in peaks)
        availability.append(dict(sign=sign,complete=complete,
                                 peak_counts=[len(values) for values in peaks]))
        if complete:
            fits.append(dict(_arrival_evidence(np.asarray(s)[block],
                             np.array([values[0] for values in peaks]),dt),sign=sign))
    return fits,availability


def _node_evidence(node, s, dt):
    evidence=[]
    ids=np.asarray(sorted(node['sections']),int)
    for block in np.split(ids,np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ids)!=1)+1):
        if len(block)<3:
            continue
        arrival=np.array([node['arrival'][int(c)] for c in block])
        phase_fits,availability=retained_phase_fits(node,block,s,dt)
        measurement=_describe_arrival(block,arrival,np.asarray(s),dt,phase_fits,RadialWaveConfig())
        evidence.append(dict(node_id=node['node_id'],sections=block.tolist(),measurement=measurement,
            phase_fit_availability=availability,
            propagation_supported=measurement['classification'] in ('C2F','F2C')))
    return evidence


def _maximum_bipartite_matching_count(left_nodes, adjacency):
    """Deterministic maximum cardinality matching; returns number of pairs."""
    owner={}
    def augment(left, seen):
        for right in sorted(adjacency.get(left, ())):
            if right in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(right)
            if right not in owner or augment(owner[right], seen):
                owner[right]=left
                return True
        return False
    return sum(augment(left,set()) for left in sorted(left_nodes))


def count_activities(nodes,s,span,dt,observation_seconds):
    edges=[compare_identity_without_direction(a,b,span,dt)
           for a,b in combinations(nodes,2) if a['wall']!=b['wall']]
    choices={n['node_id']:[] for n in nodes};uncertain={n['node_id']:[] for n in nodes}
    for edge in edges:
        target=choices if edge['relation']=='shared_identity_supported' else uncertain if edge['relation']=='unresolved' else None
        if target is not None:
            a,b=edge['node_a'],edge['node_b'];target[a].append(b);target[b].append(a)

    # Preserve v1 strict one-to-one/no-uncertainty grouping exactly.
    pairs={tuple(sorted((a,bs[0]))) for a,bs in choices.items() if len(bs)==1 and choices[bs[0]]==[a]
           and not uncertain[a] and not uncertain[bs[0]]}
    lookup={n['node_id']:n for n in nodes};used=set();groups=[]
    for a,b in sorted(pairs):
        groups.append([lookup[a],lookup[b]]);used.update((a,b))
    groups.extend([[n] for n in nodes if n['node_id'] not in used])

    # Compute propagation support once per original wall node.  This lets the
    # uncertainty graph exclude non-propagation nodes, preventing them from
    # consuming a matching slot that matters to the propagation count bound.
    node_evidence={node['node_id']:_node_evidence(node,s,dt) for node in nodes}
    node_propagates={node_id:any(row['propagation_supported'] for row in evidence)
                     for node_id,evidence in node_evidence.items()}

    activities=[]
    for i,group in enumerate(groups,1):
        evidence=[row for node in group for row in node_evidence[node['node_id']]]
        members=[n['node_id'] for n in group]
        phases=[p for n in group for ps in n['profile'].values() for p in ps]
        activities.append(dict(activity_id=f'activity_{i:03d}',source_nodes=members,
            walls=sorted({n['wall'] for n in group}),
            propagation_supported=any(node_propagates[node_id] for node_id in members),
            block_evidence=evidence,direction_used_for_counting=False,
            direction_conflict_QC=len({n['direction'] for n in group if n['direction']})>1,
            unresolved_related_nodes=sorted({k for a in members for k in choices[a]+uncertain[a] if k not in members}),
            evidence_start_s=min(p['start_s'] for p in phases),evidence_end_s=max(p['end_s'] for p in phases)))

    # ----- Legacy v1 count: strict pairs + every unmerged propagation node -----
    legacy_count=sum(a['propagation_supported'] for a in activities)

    # Strict merged pair contributes one propagation activity if either member
    # has supported propagation under the unchanged classifier.
    confirmed_pair_members={member for pair in pairs for member in pair}
    confirmed_propagation_pairs=sum(
        any(node_propagates[node_id] for node_id in pair) for pair in pairs
    )

    # Only unmerged propagation-supported nodes enter the ambiguity graph.
    unmerged_prop={node_id for node_id,prop in node_propagates.items()
                   if prop and node_id not in confirmed_pair_members}

    relevant_relations={'shared_identity_supported','unresolved'}
    adjacency={node_id:[] for node_id in unmerged_prop if lookup[node_id]['wall']==0}
    ambiguous_nodes=set()
    for edge in edges:
        if edge['relation'] not in relevant_relations:
            continue
        a,b=edge['node_a'],edge['node_b']
        if a not in unmerged_prop or b not in unmerged_prop:
            continue
        if lookup[a]['wall']==1:
            a,b=b,a
        adjacency.setdefault(a,[]).append(b)
        ambiguous_nodes.update((a,b))

    max_matching=_maximum_bipartite_matching_count(adjacency.keys(),adjacency)
    unmerged_prop_count=len(unmerged_prop)
    min_count=int(confirmed_propagation_pairs + unmerged_prop_count - max_matching)
    max_count=int(confirmed_propagation_pairs + unmerged_prop_count)
    clean_unmerged=sum(node_id not in ambiguous_nodes for node_id in unmerged_prop)
    resolved_count=int(confirmed_propagation_pairs + clean_unmerged)

    if max_count != legacy_count:
        raise RuntimeError('v2 upper count must equal the frozen v1 strict-pair/single count')

    if observation_seconds>0:
        frequency_range=[min_count*60/observation_seconds,max_count*60/observation_seconds]
        legacy_frequency=legacy_count*60/observation_seconds
        exact_frequency=(frequency_range[0] if min_count==max_count else None)
    else:
        frequency_range=[None,None];legacy_frequency=None;exact_frequency=None

    return dict(
        activities=activities,
        identity_comparisons=edges,
        # Historical fields retained unchanged for backward audit compatibility.
        significant_propagation_activity_count=legacy_count,
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm=legacy_frequency,
        significant_propagation_activity_count_v1_legacy=legacy_count,
        legacy_significant_propagation_activity_count=legacy_count,
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_v1_legacy=legacy_frequency,
        # New v2 transparent identity-uncertainty outputs.
        significant_propagation_activity_count_range=[min_count,max_count],
        significant_propagation_activity_count_min=min_count,
        significant_propagation_activity_count_max=max_count,
        significant_propagation_activity_count_v2_exact=(min_count if min_count==max_count else None),
        significant_propagation_activity_count_exact=(min_count if min_count==max_count else None),
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_range=frequency_range,
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_min=frequency_range[0],
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_max=frequency_range[1],
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_v2_exact=exact_frequency,
        significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_exact=exact_frequency,
        count_range_semantics="identity ambiguity range, not statistical confidence interval",
        resolved_propagation_activity_count=resolved_count,
        ambiguous_cross_wall_propagation_pairs=int(max_matching),
        ambiguous_cross_wall_propagation_node_count=int(len(ambiguous_nodes)),
        ambiguous_propagation_node_count=int(len(ambiguous_nodes)),
        ambiguous_propagation_fraction=(len(ambiguous_nodes)/sum(node_propagates.values()) if any(node_propagates.values()) else None),
        ambiguous_cross_wall_propagation_fraction=(len(ambiguous_nodes)/sum(node_propagates.values()) if any(node_propagates.values()) else None),
        other_significant_activity_count=len(activities)-legacy_count,
        effective_observation_seconds=observation_seconds,
        direction_used_for_counting=False,cutoff=.6,
        count_algorithm_version='activity_count_v2_logic_fix_only',
        reviewed=False,frozen_count_algorithm=False,
        interpretation=('algorithm-defined significant propagation activity; '
                        'identity uncertainty is represented by a count range; weak activity may be missed'))
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/activity_counting.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/activity_strength.py | git_blob_sha=db0331848cc88d72f0f3aac169f6ec770f7d61fd ================
"""Whole-activity strength after ownership; no new detection or wave counts.

Median per-native-section phase-footprint RMS is compared with the maximum
matched-footprint RMS in calibration rigid residuals. Ratio > 1 is an
engineering prominence decision, not a physiological truth test.
"""
import numpy as np
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.measurement.amplitude import band_limited_signal


def filtered_runs(signal, valid, time_s):
    y = np.asarray(signal, float); m = np.asarray(valid, bool)
    t = np.asarray(time_s, float)
    if y.shape != m.shape or y.shape[0] != len(t) or len(t) < 3 or np.any(np.diff(t) <= 0):
        raise ValueError('Expected native time-first signals and matching validity')
    result = np.full_like(y, np.nan)
    minimum = max(3, int(np.ceil(12/np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    for col in np.ndindex(y.shape[1:]):
        key = (slice(None),)+col
        supported = m[key] & np.isfinite(y[key])
        for a, b in np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False, supported, False])).reshape(-1, 2):
            if b-a >= minimum:
                result[(slice(a,b),)+col] = band_limited_signal(y[(slice(a,b),)+col], t[a:b])
    return result


class RigidReference:
    """Translate a candidate footprint over complete native noise supports.

    Exact section offsets are retained, including gaps. Temporal offsets and
    inclusive durations are rounded to the reference's native frames, never
    interpolated. Windows crossing a missing reference sample are excluded.
    """
    def __init__(self, banks):
        self.banks = []
        for name, y, t in banks:
            y = np.asarray(y, float); t = np.asarray(t, float)
            if y.ndim != 3 or len(y) != len(t) or len(t) < 3:
                raise ValueError('Expected rigid time x wall x section arrays')
            dt = float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
            if not np.allclose(np.diff(t), dt, rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-9):
                raise ValueError('Reference time must be uniform')
            good = np.isfinite(y)
            energy = np.concatenate([np.zeros((1,)+y.shape[1:]),
                                     np.cumsum(np.where(good, y*y, 0), axis=0)])
            missing = np.concatenate([np.zeros((1,)+y.shape[1:], int),
                                      np.cumsum(~good, axis=0)])
            self.banks.append((name, energy, missing, dt))

    def maximum(self, sections, starts_s, ends_s):
        sections = np.asarray(sections, int)
        offsets = sections-sections[0]
        origin = float(np.min(starts_s))
        maximum = None; count = 0; by_bank = {}
        for name, energy, missing, dt in self.banks:
            lo = np.rint((np.asarray(starts_s)-origin)/dt).astype(int)
            hi = np.rint((np.asarray(ends_s)-origin)/dt).astype(int)+1
            duration = hi-lo
            time_count = len(energy)-int(hi.max())
            spatial_count = energy.shape[2]-int(offsets.max())
            if min(time_count, spatial_count) < 1:
                continue
            start = np.arange(time_count)[:, None, None]
            column = np.arange(spatial_count)[None, :, None]+offsets[None, None, :]
            values = []
            for wall in range(energy.shape[1]):
                sums = energy[start+hi, wall, column]-energy[start+lo, wall, column]
                complete = (missing[start+hi, wall, column]-missing[start+lo, wall, column] == 0).all(2)
                rms = np.sqrt(np.maximum(sums, 0)/duration)
                statistic = np.median(rms, axis=2)[complete]
                if len(statistic):
                    values.append(statistic)
            if values:
                pooled = np.concatenate(values)
                value = float(pooled.max()); count += len(pooled)
                by_bank[name] = dict(maximum=value, matched_windows=len(pooled))
                maximum = value if maximum is None else max(maximum, value)
        return dict(maximum_rms=maximum, matched_windows=count, calibration_banks=by_bank)


def score_activity(event, source_evidence, filtered, valid, time_s, reference):
    """Use canonical source identity so a small split cannot replace full support."""
    entity = source_evidence[event['canonical_source_entity_id']]
    ids = np.array(sorted(int(c) for c in entity['profile']), int)
    if not len(ids) or np.any(np.diff(ids) <= 0):
        raise ValueError('Expected ordered nonempty canonical activity sections')
    t = np.asarray(time_s, float); y = np.asarray(filtered, float); m = np.asarray(valid, bool)
    if y.shape != m.shape or y.shape[0] != len(t) or y.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError('Expected one wall and matching time/section validity')
    rms = []; starts = []; ends = []
    for col in ids:
        phases = entity['profile'].get(str(col), entity['profile'].get(int(col)))
        start = min(p['start_s'] for p in phases); end = max(p['end_s'] for p in phases)
        a = int(np.searchsorted(t, start-1e-8)); b = int(np.searchsorted(t, end+1e-8, side='right'))
        if b <= a or not m[a:b, col].all() or not np.isfinite(y[a:b, col]).all():
            return dict(event_proposal_id=event['event_proposal_id'], decision='unscored_missing_support',
                        activity_rms=None, strength_ratio=None, event_count=None, event_frequency=None)
        rms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.mean(y[a:b, col]**2))))
        starts.append(float(t[a])); ends.append(float(t[b-1]))
    activity = float(np.median(rms))
    null = reference.maximum(ids, starts, ends)
    floor = null['maximum_rms']
    ratio = activity/floor if floor is not None and floor > 0 else None
    decision = ('unscored_no_matched_reference' if floor is None else
                'above_rigid_reference' if activity > floor else 'not_above_rigid_reference')
    return dict(event_proposal_id=event['event_proposal_id'], canonical_source=entity['entity_id'],
        decision=decision, activity_rms=activity, per_section_rms=rms, sections=ids.tolist(),
        phase_start_s=starts, phase_end_s=ends, strength_ratio=ratio,
        matched_rigid_reference=null, direction=event['direction'],
        ownership_status=event['ownership_status'],
        prominent_propagation_proposal=decision=='above_rigid_reference' and event['direction'] in ('C2F','F2C'),
        event_count=None, event_frequency=None)

"""Apply the existing significant-activity counter to saved A0 maps.

No outcome access, tracking rerun, new detector, or threshold selection. These
remain algorithm-defined significant propagation counts, not adjudicated waves.
"""
import argparse
from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time
import traceback

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.activity_counting import count_activities
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.activity_strength import filtered_runs
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.candidate_split_verifier import prepare_parent, decide_split
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.global_event_ownership import reconcile
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.normalized_activity import normalize_magnitude, score_salience

from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.measurement.io import jsonable

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
COUNT = 'significant_propagation_activity_count'
RATE = 'significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm'
VERSION = 'activity_count_v2_logic_fix_only'
V2_FIELDS = [
    'legacy_significant_propagation_activity_count',
    'significant_propagation_activity_count_v1_legacy',
    'significant_propagation_activity_frequency_cpm_v1_legacy',
    *[COUNT + suffix for suffix in ('_min', '_max', '_range', '_exact', '_v2_exact')],
    *[RATE + suffix for suffix in ('_min', '_max', '_range', '_exact', '_v2_exact')],
    'resolved_propagation_activity_count', 'ambiguous_cross_wall_propagation_pairs',
    'ambiguous_propagation_node_count', 'ambiguous_propagation_fraction',
    'ambiguous_cross_wall_propagation_node_count', 'ambiguous_cross_wall_propagation_fraction',
    'count_range_semantics', 'raw_observation_seconds', 'filtered_observation_seconds',
    'filtered_observation_fraction', 'count_algorithm_version', 'reviewed', 'frozen_count_algorithm',
]
CODE = ['analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/batch_wave_counts.py', 'analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/activity_counting.py',
        'analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/activity_strength.py', 'analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/candidate_split_verifier.py',

def digest(path):
    return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()


def save(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    text = json.dumps(jsonable(value), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
    temporary = path.with_suffix('.tmp')
    temporary.write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
    temporary.replace(path)


def count_map(maps, noise_floor):
    a = np.asarray(maps['A'], float)
    valid = np.asarray(maps['valid_mask'], bool) & np.isfinite(a)
    t = np.asarray(maps['interval_center_s'], float)
    s = np.asarray(maps['s'], float)
    if a.shape != (len(t), 2, len(s)) or valid.shape != a.shape:
        raise ValueError('Expected native time x two walls x section maps')
    span = float(np.median(maps['interval_dt_s']))
    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
    filtered = filtered_runs(a, valid, t)
    filtered_valid = np.isfinite(filtered)
    observable = (filtered_valid[:, :, :-2] & filtered_valid[:, :, 1:-1] &
                  filtered_valid[:, :, 2:]).any() if len(s) >= 3 else False
    exposure = observation_union(dict(maps, valid_mask=valid), min_run_s=12.0)
    if bool(observable) != bool(exposure['seconds'] > 0):
        raise RuntimeError('Detector support and frequency denominator diverged')
    support_metadata = {key: exposure[key] for key in
                        ('raw_observation_seconds', 'filtered_observation_seconds',
                         'filtered_observation_fraction')}
    if not observable or exposure['seconds'] <= 0:
        unavailable = {key: None for key in V2_FIELDS}
        unavailable.update(support_metadata, count_algorithm_version=VERSION,
                           reviewed=False, frozen_count_algorithm=False,
                           count_range_semantics='identity ambiguity range, not statistical confidence interval')
        return dict(status='no_analyzable_event_support', **{COUNT: None, RATE: None},
                    **unavailable,
                    effective_observation_seconds=exposure['seconds'],
                    failure_reason='No three adjacent native sections with finite filtered support',
                    observation=exposure)
    normalized, metadata = normalize_magnitude(filtered, valid)
    ownership = dict(walls=[])
    selected = dict(walls=[])
    evidence = []
    for w in (0, 1):
        report = detect_partial(a[:, w], valid[:, w], t, s, noise_floor=noise_floor)
        report.pop('filtered_A')  # Arrays remain in the source NPZ, not JSON.
        proposals = [decide_split(prepare_parent(a[:, w], valid[:, w], t, s, parent,
                     noise_floor=noise_floor, measurement_span_s=span),
                     minimum_adjacent_support=3) for parent in report['events']]
        result = reconcile(report, proposals, t, valid[:, w], measurement_span_s=span)
        scores = [score_salience(event, result['source_evidence'], normalized[:, w],
                  valid[:, w], t) for event in result['event_proposals']]
        ownership['walls'].append(dict(reconciliation=result))
        selected['walls'].append(dict(wall=w, scores=scores))
        evidence.append(dict(wall=w, parent_report=report, split_proposals=proposals,
                             reconciliation=result, salience=scores))
    nodes = candidate_nodes(selected, ownership)
    result = count_activities(nodes, s, span, dt, exposure['seconds'])
    result.update(support_metadata)
    result.update(status='completed', failure_reason=None, normalization=metadata,
                  observation=exposure, measurement_span_s=span, sample_dt_s=dt,
                  native_section_count=len(s), sampled_s_range=[float(s[0]), float(s[-1])],
                  requested_s_range=[0., 1.], walls=evidence,
                  raw_valid_fraction=float(valid.mean()),
                  unresolved_activity_rows=sum(bool(e['unresolved_related_nodes'])
                                               for e in result['activities']),
                  reviewed=False, frozen_count_algorithm=False)
    return result


def process_case(task):
    case_id, source, destination, floor = task
    begin = time.monotonic()
    try:
        before = digest(source)
        with np.load(source, allow_pickle=False) as z:
            maps = dict(z)
        result = count_map(maps, floor)
        if digest(source) != before:
            raise RuntimeError('Source spacetime changed during counting')
        result.update(case_id=case_id, source_spacetime=str(source),
                      source_sha256=before, elapsed_s=time.monotonic()-begin)
        save(destination, result)
        keys = [COUNT, RATE, 'other_significant_activity_count',
                'effective_observation_seconds', 'unresolved_activity_rows',
                'raw_valid_fraction', 'sampled_s_range', 'native_section_count'] + V2_FIELDS
        return dict(case_id=case_id, processing_status=result['status'],
                    failure_reason=result.get('failure_reason'), result_path=str(destination),
                    **{k: result.get(k) for k in keys})
    except Exception as error:
        # Per-case batch boundary only: retain the actual error, never zero.
        failed = dict(case_id=case_id, processing_status='failed',
                      failure_reason=f'{type(error).__name__}: {error}',
                      traceback=traceback.format_exc(), **{COUNT: None, RATE: None})
        save(destination, failed)
        return dict({key: None for key in V2_FIELDS},
                    **{k: v for k, v in failed.items() if k != 'traceback'})


def run(args):
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    inputs = pd.read_csv(args.features/'patient_results.csv', usecols=['case_id'])
    if args.case_id:
        inputs = inputs.loc[inputs['case_id'].isin(args.case_id)]
    if not len(inputs) or inputs['case_id'].duplicated().any():
        raise ValueError('Expected unique, nonempty examination IDs')
    protocol_source = json.loads(args.noise_protocol.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    floor = float(protocol_source['noise_floor_rate'])
    protocol = dict(noise_floor_rate=floor, noise_protocol=str(args.noise_protocol),
        noise_protocol_sha256=digest(args.noise_protocol), cutoff=.6, minimum_native_support=3,
        parents='periodic FFT + observable partial phase',
        split='existing measurement_span_mean, support=3, within parents only',
        ownership='existing global ownership + direction-independent cross-wall identity',
        propagation='existing arrival classifier with restored phase fits',
        observation='union of detector-eligible measurement intervals: per-section continuous support >=12 s, then >=3 adjacent native sections in either wall',
        count_algorithm_version=VERSION,
        spatial_scope='all existing native sections, no resampling, no best-region selection',
        direction_used_for_counting=False, outcomes_read=False, thresholds_changed=False,
        algorithm_frozen=False, count_interpretation='significant propagation activity, not adjudicated total waves',
        known_limitations=['local_once false positives', 'adjacent-event duplicate identity',
                           'fixed .6 salience misses weak events'],
        code_sha256={p: digest(ROOT/p) for p in CODE}, requested_cases=len(inputs))
    save(args.output/'protocol_before_counting.json', protocol)
    tasks = [(row.case_id, args.features/row.case_id/'spacetime_2s.npz',
              args.output/(row.case_id+'.json'), floor) for row in inputs.itertuples()]
    rows = []
    if args.workers == 1:
        rows = [process_case(task) for task in tasks]
    else:
        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:
            futures = [pool.submit(process_case, task) for task in tasks]
            for future in as_completed(futures):
                rows.append(future.result())
                save(args.output/'progress.json', dict(finished=len(rows), total=len(tasks)))
    table = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index('case_id').reindex(inputs['case_id']).reset_index()
    table.to_csv(args.output/'wave_counts.csv', index=False, encoding='utf-8-sig')
    table.to_excel(args.output/'wave_counts.xlsx', index=False)
    if any(digest(ROOT/p) != h for p, h in protocol['code_sha256'].items()):
        raise RuntimeError('Counting implementation changed during the batch')
    summary = dict(total=len(table), status_counts=table['processing_status'].value_counts().to_dict(),
        total_algorithm_defined_activities=int(table[COUNT].sum()),
        legacy_total_significant_propagation_activity_count=int(table[COUNT].sum()),
        total_significant_propagation_activity_count_range=[
            int(table[COUNT + '_min'].sum()), int(table[COUNT + '_max'].sum())]
            if table[COUNT].notna().any() else None,
        exact_count_exams=int(table[COUNT + '_exact'].notna().sum()),
        ambiguous_count_exams=int((table[COUNT + '_min'] < table[COUNT + '_max']).sum()),
        count_algorithm_version=VERSION,
        count_range_semantics='identity ambiguity range, not statistical confidence interval',
        nonzero_exams=int((table[COUNT] > 0).sum()),
        unavailable_exams=int(table[COUNT].isna().sum()),
        frozen_algorithms_changed=False, thresholds_changed=False)
    save(args.output/'summary.json', summary)
    print(json.dumps(summary), flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--features', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--noise-protocol', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument('--case-id', action='append')
    run(parser.parse_args())

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/candidate_split_verifier.py | git_blob_sha=b7d4a5f038eca7f89c0c5b61b9ee2af729e82a51 ================
"""Sandbox: split evidence strictly inside existing periodic/partial parents.

No parent detection, global auxiliary scan, truth access or patient counting.
Local phase nodes are passed to the unchanged ridge/pair measurement code.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from itertools import combinations
import numpy as np
from uterine_wave import radial_events as core
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.event_input_smoothing import measurement_span_mean


def _blocks(ids):
    ids=np.asarray(ids,int)
    return [x for x in np.split(ids,np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ids)!=1)+1) if len(x)]


def _local_nodes(signal,valid,t,s,parent,span,floor):
    context=np.flatnonzero((t>=parent['start_s']-span)&(t<=parent['end_s']+span))
    times=t[context]
    values=np.full((len(times),len(s)),np.nan)
    columns=[[] for _ in s]
    for col in parent['sections']:
        supported=valid[context,col]&np.isfinite(signal[context,col])
        for run_id,(begin,end) in enumerate(core._runs(supported)):
            if end-begin<3:
                continue
            samples=int(2*np.floor(span/np.median(np.diff(times))/2)+1)
            if end-begin<samples:
                continue
            # Only this parent's buffered slice is read by the auxiliary filter.
            y=measurement_span_mean(signal[context[begin:end],col],times[begin:end],span_s=span)
            values[begin:end,col]=y
            edges=np.r_[0,np.flatnonzero(np.diff(y>=0))+1,len(y)]
            for sequence,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(edges[:-1],edges[1:])):
                peak=a+int(np.argmax(abs(y[a:b])))
                amplitude=float(abs(y[peak]))
                if amplitude<=max(float(floor[col]),1e-12) or peak in (a,b-1):
                    continue
                columns[col].append(dict(column=col,run_id=run_id,sequence=sequence,
                    sign=1 if y[peak]>0 else -1,peak_index=int(begin+peak),
                    peak_time_s=float(times[begin+peak]),amplitude=amplitude,
                    first=int(begin+a),last=int(begin+b-1),
                    start_s=float(times[begin+a]),end_s=float(times[begin+b-1]),
                    left_censored=bool(a==0),right_censored=bool(b==len(y))))
    return context,times,values,columns


def prepare_parent(signal,valid,time_s,space,parent,*,noise_floor,measurement_span_s):
    """Compute local evidence once; support=3/4/5 uses the SAME observations.

    The parent uses its original time/section bounding box. Context is one
    existing measurement span; child ARRIVALS must remain in the unexpanded
    box. No neighboring sections or new parent envelopes are introduced.
    """
    y=np.asarray(signal,float);m=np.asarray(valid,bool)
    t=np.asarray(time_s,float);s=np.asarray(space,float)
    ids=np.asarray(parent['sections'],int)
    if (y.shape!=m.shape or y.shape!=(len(t),len(s)) or len(t)<3 or
        np.any(np.diff(t)<=0) or not np.allclose(np.diff(t),np.median(np.diff(t)),rtol=1e-5,atol=1e-9) or
        len(ids)<1 or np.any(np.diff(ids)<=0) or ids.min()<0 or ids.max()>=len(s) or
        not np.isfinite(measurement_span_s) or measurement_span_s<=0):
        raise ValueError('Expected native uniform maps, ordered parent sections and positive measurement span')
    floor=np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(noise_floor,float),(len(s),))
    if not np.isfinite(floor).all() or np.any(floor<0) or parent['start_s']>parent['end_s']:
        raise ValueError('Invalid frozen noise reference or parent time bounds')
    context,times,values,columns=_local_nodes(y,m,t,s,parent,measurement_span_s,floor)
    ridges=core._ridges(columns,times,s) if len(times)>=3 else []
    edges=core._phase_edges(ridges)
    config=core.RadialWaveConfig()
    measured=[]
    # Keep both physiological polarities available in the observations. This
    # trial verifies two positive->negative maxima, the already frozen raw
    # length-bump reference. It does not declare negative-first activity false.
    for key,edge in edges.items():
        if ridges[edge['first']]['sign']!=1:
            continue
        event=core._measure_pair(dict(edge),ridges,values,times,s,config)
        kept=[col for col,arrival in zip(event['sections'],event['arrival_time_s'])
              if parent['start_s']<=arrival<=parent['end_s']]
        for block in _blocks(kept):
            if len(block)<3:
                continue
            local_edge=dict(edge,columns=block.tolist())
            item=core._measure_pair(local_edge,ridges,values,times,s,config)
            item.update(_edge=local_edge,_key=key)
            measured.append(item)
    # Local event rank is derived from actual eligible successive phases, not
    # the injected truth or an optimized lag. It exposes identity switching.
    ordinals={};turn_counts={}
    for col,column in enumerate(columns):
        turns=[]
        for first,second in zip(column[:-1],column[1:]):
            if (first['sign']!=1 or second['sign']!=-1 or first['run_id']!=second['run_id'] or
                second['sequence']-first['sequence']!=1):
                continue
            k,k2=first['last'],second['first']
            crossing=times[k]-values[k,col]/(values[k2,col]-values[k,col])*(times[k2]-times[k])
            if parent['start_s']<=crossing<=parent['end_s']:
                turns.append((first,second))
        turn_counts[col]=len(turns)
        for rank,(first,second) in enumerate(turns):
            ordinals[(col,first['run_id'],first['sequence'],second['sequence'])]=rank
    node_maps={r['id']:{n['column']:n for n in r['nodes']} for r in ridges}
    options=[]
    for left,right in combinations(measured,2):
        if set(left['phase_ridge_ids'])&set(right['phase_ridge_ids']):
            continue
        common=sorted(set(left['sections'])&set(right['sections']))
        for block in _blocks(common):
            if len(block)<3:
                continue
            a,b=left,right
            arrivals_a=dict(zip(a['sections'],a['arrival_time_s']))
            arrivals_b=dict(zip(b['sections'],b['arrival_time_s']))
            differences=np.array([arrivals_b[c]-arrivals_a[c] for c in block])
            if np.all(differences<0):
                a,b=b,a
                differences=-differences
            conflicts=[]
            if not np.all(differences>0):
                conflicts.append('children_cross_in_time')
            ranks=[[],[]]
            for col in block:
                nodes=[node_maps[rid][int(col)] for item in (a,b) for rid in item['phase_ridge_ids']]
                if len({n['run_id'] for n in nodes})!=1:
                    conflicts.append('different_continuous_runs')
                    continue
                if not nodes[1]['sequence']<nodes[2]['sequence']:
                    conflicts.append('interleaved_phase_sequences')
                for index in (0,1):
                    first,second=nodes[2*index:2*index+2]
                    rank=ordinals.get((int(col),first['run_id'],first['sequence'],second['sequence']))
                    if rank is None:
                        conflicts.append('unresolved_local_turn_identity')
                    else:
                        ranks[index].append(rank)
            if any(len(set(r))!=1 for r in ranks):
                conflicts.append('local_event_rank_switch')
            if all(ranks) and any(y!=x+1 for x,y in zip(*ranks)):
                conflicts.append('additional_intervening_activity')
            children=[]
            for item in (a,b):
                edge=dict(item['_edge'],columns=block.tolist())
                child=core._measure_pair(edge,ridges,values,times,s,config)
                if any(flag in child['quality_flags'] for flag in ('nonmonotone_arrivals','phase_direction_conflict')):
                    conflicts.append('inconsistent_child_arrival_order')
                selected,_=core._pair_phases(ridges,{item['_key']:edge})
                propagation=bool(selected and child['classification'] in ('C2F','F2C') and
                    'phase_direction_conflict' not in child['quality_flags'] and
                    'nonmonotone_arrivals' not in child['quality_flags'])
                child.update(parent_candidate_id=parent['event_id'],activity_split_supported=True,
                    propagation_supported=propagation,
                    fitted_classification=child['classification'],
                    classification=child['classification'] if propagation else 'activity_only',
                    direction=child['classification'] if propagation else None,
                    fitted_velocity_paths_per_s=child['velocity_paths_per_s'],
                    velocity_paths_per_s=child['velocity_paths_per_s'] if propagation else None,
                    speed_candidate_paths_per_s=child['velocity_paths_per_s'] if propagation else None,
                    supporting_phase_evidence=[dict(section=int(c),
                        phases=[dict(sign=node_maps[r][int(c)]['sign'],
                            peak_s=node_maps[r][int(c)]['peak_time_s'],
                            observed_start_s=node_maps[r][int(c)]['start_s'],
                            observed_end_s=node_maps[r][int(c)]['end_s'],
                            run_id=node_maps[r][int(c)]['run_id'],
                            amplitude=node_maps[r][int(c)]['amplitude']) for r in child['phase_ridge_ids']]) for c in block],
                    propagation_source='Local nodes through unchanged ridge/pair measurement and pairing',
                    reviewed=False)
                children.append(child)
            options.append(dict(identity=tuple(a['phase_ridge_ids']+b['phase_ridge_ids']),
                                common_sections=block.tolist(),children=children,
                                conflicts=sorted(set(conflicts))))
    return dict(parent=deepcopy(parent),context_time_range_s=[float(times[0]),float(times[-1])] if len(times) else None,
        context_expansion_s=float(measurement_span_s),turn_counts=turn_counts,options=options,
        local_phase_ridges=[dict(ridge_id=r['id'],sign=r['sign'],sections=[n['column'] for n in r['nodes']],
                                peak_time_s=[n['peak_time_s'] for n in r['nodes']]) for r in ridges],
        local_observations=dict(time_s=times,sections=ids,smoothed_A=values[:,ids],
                                valid_mask=m[context][:,ids],auxiliary_valid_mask=np.isfinite(values[:,ids]),
                                raw_A=y[context][:,ids]))


def decide_split(prepared,*,minimum_adjacent_support=3,include_signals=False):
    if minimum_adjacent_support not in (3,4,5):
        raise ValueError('This fixed experiment permits adjacent support 3, 4 or 5')
    parent=prepared['parent']
    eligible=[p for p in prepared['options'] if len(p['common_sections'])>=minimum_adjacent_support]
    identities={p['identity'] for p in eligible if not p['conflicts']}
    if len(identities)==1 and not any(p['conflicts'] for p in eligible):
        selected=max((p for p in eligible if not p['conflicts']),key=lambda p:len(p['common_sections']))
        status='split_two';reason='two ordered complete local phase sequences on adjacent native sections'
        children=deepcopy(selected['children'])
        for index,child in enumerate(children,1):
            child['event_id']=f"{parent['event_id']}_child_{index}"
    else:
        children=[]
        tentative=any(n>=2 for n in prepared['turn_counts'].values())
        status='split_ambiguous' if tentative or eligible else 'keep_single'
        reason=('competing identities or inconsistent local order' if eligible else
                'second-turn evidence lacks shared adjacent support' if tentative else 'no supported internal second activity')
    result=dict(parent_candidate_id=parent['event_id'],parent=deepcopy(parent),
        split_status=status,reason=reason,minimum_adjacent_support=minimum_adjacent_support,
        context_time_range_s=prepared['context_time_range_s'],
        context_expansion_s=prepared['context_expansion_s'],
        child_1=children[0] if children else None,child_2=children[1] if children else None,
        tentative_pair_count=len(eligible),
        conflict_reasons=sorted({flag for p in eligible for flag in p['conflicts']}),
        local_turn_counts=prepared['turn_counts'],event_count=None,event_frequency=None)
    if include_signals:
        result['local_observations']=prepared['local_observations']
    return result

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/direction_identity_trial.py | git_blob_sha=4420d5235266f822b89ac2fb4a714e5fdeadcc85 ================
"""Revalidate retained-direction evidence before cross-wall direction reporting.

This is an experimental reconciliation layer.  It does not create candidates,
change the fixed salience cutoff, or replace formal event outputs.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from itertools import combinations

import numpy as np

from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.measurement.radial_events import RadialWaveConfig, _describe_arrival


DIRECTIONS = ('C2F', 'F2C')


def _longest_adjacent(ids):
    ids = np.asarray(sorted(set(ids)), int)
    return max((len(block) for block in np.split(
        ids, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ids) != 1) + 1)), default=0)


def _adjacent_blocks(ids):
    ids = np.asarray(sorted(set(ids)), int)
    if not len(ids):
        return []
    return [block for block in np.split(
        ids, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ids) != 1) + 1) if len(block)]


def _phase_subset_matches(left, right, tolerance_s):
    """Allow extra local phases while matching the smaller signed sequence."""
    smaller, larger = (left, right) if len(left) <= len(right) else (right, left)
    smaller = sorted(smaller, key=lambda phase: phase['peak_s'])
    larger = sorted(larger, key=lambda phase: phase['peak_s'])
    first_available = 0
    matches = []
    for phase in smaller:
        choices = [(abs(phase['peak_s'] - larger[j]['peak_s']), j)
                   for j in range(first_available, len(larger))
                   if phase['sign'] == larger[j]['sign']]
        if not choices:
            return False, matches
        distance, index = min(choices)
        if distance > tolerance_s:
            return False, matches
        first_available = index + 1
        matches.append((phase['peak_s'], larger[index]['peak_s']))
    return bool(matches), matches


def compare_identity_without_direction(a, b, span_s, sample_dt_s):
    """Compare activity identity before using either wall's direction label."""
    if a['wall'] == b['wall']:
        raise ValueError('Identity comparison requires different walls')
    tolerance = float(span_s + sample_dt_s)
    shared = sorted(set(a['sections']) & set(b['sections']))
    matched, near, conflicts = [], [], []
    for section in shared:
        arrival_near = (a['arrival_marker'] == b['arrival_marker'] and
                        abs(a['arrival'][section] - b['arrival'][section]) <= tolerance)
        phases_match, _ = _phase_subset_matches(
            a['profile'][section], b['profile'][section], tolerance)
        if arrival_near or phases_match:
            near.append(section)
        if arrival_near and phases_match:
            matched.append(section)
        elif arrival_near or phases_match:
            conflicts.append(section)
    supported = _longest_adjacent(matched) >= 3
    relation = ('shared_identity_supported' if supported else
                'unresolved' if near else 'no_shared_identity_evidence')
    return dict(node_a=a['node_id'], node_b=b['node_id'], relation=relation,
                shared_sections=shared, matched_sections=matched,
                near_sections=near, conflict_sections=conflicts,
                original_directions=[a['direction'], b['direction']],
                original_direction_conflict=(a['direction'] in DIRECTIONS and
                                             b['direction'] in DIRECTIONS and
                                             a['direction'] != b['direction']),
                tolerance_s=tolerance,
                rule='>=3 adjacent arrival-and-phase matches; direction not used for identity')


def revalidate_retained_direction(node, s, sample_dt_s, config=RadialWaveConfig()):
    """Refit each contiguous retained block; never bridge a salience gap."""
    sections = np.asarray(sorted(node['sections']), int)
    blocks = _adjacent_blocks(sections)
    measurements = []
    for block in blocks:
        arrival = np.asarray([node['arrival'][int(section)] for section in block], float)
        measurement = _describe_arrival(
            block, arrival, np.asarray(s, float), float(sample_dt_s), [], config)
        measurements.append(dict(sections=block.tolist(), arrival_time_s=arrival.tolist(),
                                 measurement=measurement))
    block_directions = [item['measurement']['classification'] for item in measurements
                        if item['measurement']['classification'] in DIRECTIONS]
    refit = block_directions[0] if len(set(block_directions)) == 1 else None
    original = node['direction'] if node['direction'] in DIRECTIONS else None
    if original is None:
        status, supported = 'original_direction_unresolved', None
    elif any(direction != original for direction in block_directions):
        status, supported = 'retained_support_conflicts_with_original', None
    elif any(direction == original for direction in block_directions):
        status, supported = 'retained_support_agrees', original
    else:
        status, supported = 'retained_support_unresolved', None
    return dict(node_id=node['node_id'], original_direction=original,
                retained_refit_direction=refit, supported_direction=supported,
                direction_status=status, retained_sections=sections.tolist(),
                retained_contiguous_blocks=[block.tolist() for block in blocks],
                block_direction_evidence=measurements,
                direction_rule=('each contiguous retained block refit separately; '
                                'opposing block makes direction unresolved'))


def reconcile_identity_then_direction(nodes, s, span_s, sample_dt_s):
    """Form cross-wall identities first, then combine revalidated directions."""
    comparisons = [compare_identity_without_direction(a, b, span_s, sample_dt_s)
                   for a, b in combinations(nodes, 2) if a['wall'] != b['wall']]
    choices = {node['node_id']: [] for node in nodes}
    uncertain = {node['node_id']: [] for node in nodes}
    for edge in comparisons:
        target = choices if edge['relation'] == 'shared_identity_supported' else (
            uncertain if edge['relation'] == 'unresolved' else None)
        if target is not None:
            target[edge['node_a']].append(edge['node_b'])
            target[edge['node_b']].append(edge['node_a'])
    pairs = {tuple(sorted((node_id, matches[0])))
             for node_id, matches in choices.items()
             if len(matches) == 1 and choices[matches[0]] == [node_id]
             and not uncertain[node_id] and not uncertain[matches[0]]}
    lookup = {node['node_id']: node for node in nodes}
    used, groups = set(), []
    for left, right in sorted(pairs):
        groups.append([lookup[left], lookup[right]])
        used.update((left, right))
    groups.extend([[node] for node in nodes if node['node_id'] not in used])
    groups.sort(key=lambda group: (min(np.median(list(node['arrival'].values()))
                                       for node in group), group[0]['node_id']))

    activities = []
    for index, group in enumerate(groups, 1):
        evidence = [revalidate_retained_direction(node, s, sample_dt_s) for node in group]
        supported = {item['supported_direction'] for item in evidence
                     if item['supported_direction'] in DIRECTIONS}
        original = {node['direction'] for node in group if node['direction'] in DIRECTIONS}
        if len(original) > 1:
            direction, direction_status = None, 'cross_wall_direction_conflict'
        elif len(supported) == 1:
            direction = next(iter(supported))
            direction_status = ('cross_wall_direction_agreement' if len(group) == 2 and
                                len([item for item in evidence if item['supported_direction']]) == 2
                                else 'single_wall_direction_support')
        elif len(supported) > 1:
            direction, direction_status = None, 'cross_wall_direction_conflict'
        else:
            direction, direction_status = None, 'direction_unresolved_after_retained_refit'
        members = [node['node_id'] for node in group]
        related = sorted({other for member in members
                          for other in uncertain[member] + choices[member]
                          if other not in members})
        phases = [phase for node in group for values in node['profile'].values()
                  for phase in values]
        activity_id=f'activity_{index:03d}'
        activities.append(dict(activity_id=activity_id,patient_candidate_id=activity_id,
            source_nodes=members, walls=sorted({node['wall'] for node in group}),
            cross_wall_identity=len(group) == 2, direction=direction,
            direction_status=direction_status, direction_evidence=evidence,
            classification=('propagation' if direction else 'direction_unresolved_activity'),
            ownership_status=('unresolved_cross_wall' if related else
                              'shared_identity' if len(group) == 2 else 'single_wall'),
            unresolved_related_nodes=related,
            evidence_start_s=min(phase['start_s'] for phase in phases),
            evidence_end_s=max(phase['end_s'] for phase in phases),
            source_measurements=deepcopy(group), reviewed=False))
    return dict(activities=activities, identity_comparisons=comparisons,
                input_candidate_rows=len(nodes), activity_rows=len(activities),
                cross_wall_identity_pairs=len(pairs),
                direction_counts={direction: sum(item['direction'] == direction
                                                  for item in activities)
                                  for direction in DIRECTIONS},
                direction_unresolved_rows=sum(item['direction'] is None for item in activities),
                formal_event_count=None, formal_event_frequency_cpm=None,
                experimental=True)
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/direction_identity_trial.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/event_input_smoothing.py | git_blob_sha=ec82144daf4d286c5f6c3716c6ca1c3bdfe14192 ================
"""Event-input ablation only: local averaging at the measurement time scale.

This is not the physiological-band amplitude/ER filter. The detector applies
it separately inside each continuous valid run; missing samples are not filled.
"""
import numpy as np


def measurement_span_mean(values, times, *, span_s):
    """Demean and apply an odd centered boxcar near one measurement interval.

    Reflection pads only the short kernel edge; padded values are not observed
    support. There is no spectral band selection, polynomial detrending or new
    duration parameter independent of the existing radial measurement span.
    """
    y=np.asarray(values,float);t=np.asarray(times,float)
    if y.ndim<1 or y.shape[0]!=len(t) or len(t)<3:
        raise ValueError('Expected time-first values on at least three timestamps')
    differences=np.diff(t)
    if (not np.isfinite(y).all() or not np.isfinite(t).all() or
        np.any(differences<=0) or not np.allclose(differences,np.median(differences),rtol=1e-5,atol=1e-9) or
        not np.isfinite(span_s) or span_s<=0):
        raise ValueError('Expected finite complete values, uniform time and positive measurement span')
    samples=int(2*np.floor(span_s/np.median(differences)/2)+1)
    if samples>len(t):
        raise ValueError('Smoothing span exceeds the continuous input run')
    half=samples//2
    centered=y-y.mean(axis=0,keepdims=True)
    padded=np.pad(centered,[(half,half)]+[(0,0)]*(y.ndim-1),mode='reflect')
    integral=np.concatenate([np.zeros_like(padded[:1]),np.cumsum(padded,axis=0)],axis=0)
    return (integral[samples:]-integral[:-samples])/samples
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/event_input_smoothing.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/global_event_ownership.py | git_blob_sha=8842bfe346b6a0c335abd99e2e5099903626d2b4 ================
"""Sandbox global ownership of frozen parents and already-local split children.

No image/signal detection, filter changes, injected truth or patient counts.
Global means all candidates in ONE wall. Cross-wall event pairing is separate.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from itertools import combinations
import numpy as np


def _runs(mask):
    return np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False,mask,False])).reshape(-1,2)


def build_entities(report,proposals,time_s,valid):
    t=np.asarray(time_s,float);m=np.asarray(valid,bool)
    if m.ndim!=2 or len(t)!=len(m) or len(t)<3 or np.any(np.diff(t)<=0):
        raise ValueError('Expected native time x section validity for one wall')
    epochs={c:_runs(m[:,c]) for c in range(m.shape[1])}
    def phase_node(col,p):
        index=int(np.argmin(abs(t-p['peak_s'])))
        epoch=next(((int(a),int(b)) for a,b in epochs[col] if a<=index<b),None)
        return dict(sign=int(p['sign']),peak_s=float(p['peak_s']),
            start_s=float(p['observed_start_s']),end_s=float(p['observed_end_s']),epoch=epoch)
    fragments={f['fragment_id']:f for f in report['fragments']}
    parents={e['event_id']:e for e in report['events']}
    entities={};parent_to_base={}
    for group in report['proposed_events']:
        eid='base:'+group['proposal_id'];profile={};arrival={}
        members=[parents[k] for k in group['fragment_ids']]
        for fid in group['fragment_ids']:
            f=fragments[fid];parent_to_base[fid]=eid
            for p in f['local_phase_support']:
                c=int(p['section']);profile.setdefault(c,[]).append(phase_node(c,p))
            arrival.update(zip(f['sections'],f['arrival_time_s']))
        for c in profile:
            unique={(p['sign'],p['peak_s']):p for p in profile[c]}
            profile[c]=sorted(unique.values(),key=lambda p:p['peak_s'])
        canonical=max(members,key=lambda e:(e['evidence_kind']=='paired_phase',len(e['sections'])))
        entities[eid]=dict(entity_id=eid,source_kind='existing',source_parents=list(group['fragment_ids']),
            complete=all(len(ps)==2 for ps in profile.values()),profile=profile,arrival=arrival,
            direction=canonical['classification'] if canonical['classification'] in ('C2F','F2C') else None,
            canonical_measurement=deepcopy(canonical),original_measurements=deepcopy(members),
            phase_ridge_provenance=[dict(parent_id=e['event_id'],phase_ridge_ids=e['phase_ridge_ids']) for e in members])
    splits=[]
    for proposal in proposals:
        if proposal['split_status']!='split_two':
            continue
        children=[]
        for child in (proposal['child_1'],proposal['child_2']):
            eid='child:'+child['event_id'];profile={}
            for row in child['supporting_phase_evidence']:
                c=int(row['section']);profile[c]=[phase_node(c,p) for p in row['phases']]
            entities[eid]=dict(entity_id=eid,source_kind='child',source_parents=[proposal['parent_candidate_id']],
                complete=True,profile=profile,arrival=dict(zip(child['sections'],child['arrival_time_s'])),
                direction=child['direction'],canonical_measurement=deepcopy(child),original_measurements=[],
                phase_ridge_provenance=[dict(parent_id=proposal['parent_candidate_id'],
                    source='parent_local_auxiliary',phase_ridge_ids=child['phase_ridge_ids'])])
            children.append(eid)
        splits.append(dict(parent_id=proposal['parent_candidate_id'],base_id=parent_to_base[proposal['parent_candidate_id']],
                           children=children))
    return entities,splits


def compare_identity(a,b,*,sample_dt_s,measurement_span_s):
    """Prioritized evidence, not a scalar score or fixed global-time radius.

    Existing 3-section connector support is reused. Both signs/sequence,
    physical continuity, local arrival and peak trajectories must agree.
    Uncomparable/partial evidence is retained rather than forcibly merged.
    """
    shared=sorted(set(a['profile'])&set(b['profile']))
    tolerance=measurement_span_s+sample_dt_s
    evidence=dict(shared_sections=shared,tolerance_s=tolerance,matched_sections=[],
                  exact_phase_nodes=0,phase_sequence_conflict=False,
                  direction_conflict=bool(a['direction'] and b['direction'] and a['direction']!=b['direction']))
    if not shared:
        return dict(relation='unrelated',priority=0,reason='no shared native support',**evidence)
    paired=a['complete'] and b['complete']
    exact=0;matched=[];near=[];contradictions=[]
    for c in shared:
        left,right=a['profile'][c],b['profile'][c]
        signs_a=[p['sign'] for p in left];signs_b=[p['sign'] for p in right]
        paired_here=len(left)==2 and len(right)==2
        if paired_here and signs_a!=signs_b:
            evidence['phase_sequence_conflict']=True
            continue
        pairs=[]
        for p in left:
            options=[q for q in right if q['sign']==p['sign']]
            if len(options)==1:
                pairs.append((p,options[0]))
        if not pairs:
            continue
        distances=[abs(p['peak_s']-q['peak_s']) for p,q in pairs]
        if min(distances)<=tolerance:
            near.append(c)
        epoch_ok=all(p['epoch'] is not None and p['epoch']==q['epoch'] for p,q in pairs)
        bounds_ok=all(q['start_s']-sample_dt_s<=p['peak_s']<=q['end_s']+sample_dt_s and
                      p['start_s']-sample_dt_s<=q['peak_s']<=p['end_s']+sample_dt_s for p,q in pairs)
        peaks_ok=max(distances)<=tolerance
        arrival_ok=not paired_here or abs(a['arrival'][c]-b['arrival'][c])<=tolerance
        if epoch_ok and bounds_ok and peaks_ok and arrival_ok:
            matched.append(c)
            exact+=sum(p['peak_s']==q['peak_s'] for p,q in pairs)
        elif min(distances)<=tolerance:
            contradictions.append(c)
    evidence.update(matched_sections=matched,exact_phase_nodes=exact)
    if evidence['phase_sequence_conflict']:
        return dict(relation='distinct',priority=0,reason='different ordered phase sequences',**evidence)
    blocks=np.split(np.asarray(matched,int),np.flatnonzero(np.diff(matched)!=1)+1)
    enough=max((len(x) for x in blocks),default=0)>=3
    if enough and not contradictions and len(matched)==len(shared):
        relation='duplicate' if paired else 'partial_duplicate'
        priority=3 if paired and exact==2*len(shared) else 2 if paired else 1
        return dict(relation=relation,priority=priority,reason='same local phase and arrival structure',**evidence)
    if near:
        return dict(relation='ambiguous',priority=0,reason='partial or conflicting local phase support',**evidence)
    return dict(relation='distinct',priority=0,reason='separate local phase timing',**evidence)


def _rank(e):
    # Complete evidence first, then larger native support; existing evidence
    # wins ties. A smaller local child cannot overwrite a wider complete parent.
    return (e['complete'],len(e['profile']),
            e['source_kind']=='existing',e['entity_id'])


def reconcile(report,proposals,time_s,valid,*,measurement_span_s):
    entities,splits=build_entities(report,proposals,time_s,valid)
    dt=float(np.median(np.diff(time_s)))
    relations={}
    def compare(x,y):
        key=tuple(sorted((x,y)))
        if key not in relations:
            relations[key]=compare_identity(entities[x],entities[y],sample_dt_s=dt,
                                            measurement_span_s=measurement_span_s)
        return relations[key]
    def duplicate(x,y):
        return compare(x,y)['relation'] in ('duplicate','partial_duplicate')
    bases=[k for k,e in entities.items() if e['source_kind']=='existing']
    base_ambiguity={}
    for x,y in combinations(bases,2):
        if compare(x,y)['relation']=='ambiguous':
            for k,other in ((x,y),(y,x)):
                base_ambiguity.setdefault(k,dict(reason='conflicting original ownership evidence',possible_owners=[]))['possible_owners'].append(other)
    for k in bases:
        choices=[p for p in bases if p!=k and entities[p]['complete'] and duplicate(k,p)]
        if any(not duplicate(a,b) for a,b in combinations(choices,2)):
            previous=base_ambiguity.get(k,{}).get('possible_owners',[])
            base_ambiguity[k]=dict(reason='original evidence matches conflicting complete events',possible_owners=sorted(set(previous+choices)))
    # Reconcile original evidence first. No child can serve as a transitive
    # bridge that merges otherwise conflicting original event identities.
    base_clusters={k:[k] for k in bases};base_owner={k:k for k in bases}
    for x,y in combinations(sorted(bases,key=lambda k:_rank(entities[k]),reverse=True),2):
        cx,cy=base_owner[x],base_owner[y]
        if cx==cy or x in base_ambiguity or y in base_ambiguity or not duplicate(x,y):
            continue
        union=base_clusters[cx]+base_clusters[cy]
        anchor=max(union,key=lambda k:_rank(entities[k]))
        if not all(k==anchor or duplicate(anchor,k) for k in union):
            continue
        if any(compare(p,q)['relation'] in ('ambiguous','distinct') for p,q in combinations(union,2)):
            continue
        base_clusters.pop(cx);base_clusters.pop(cy);base_clusters[anchor]=union
        for k in union:base_owner[k]=anchor
    owners={};uncertain={};attachments={k:[] for k in base_clusters}
    for k,e in entities.items():
        if e['source_kind']!='child':continue
        definite=[];possible=[]
        for anchor,members in base_clusters.items():
            relation=compare(k,anchor)
            if relation['relation'] in ('duplicate','partial_duplicate'):
                definite.append((anchor,relation['priority']))
            elif relation['relation']=='ambiguous':
                possible.append(anchor)
        highest=max((priority for _,priority in definite),default=0)
        strongest=[anchor for anchor,priority in definite if priority==highest]
        # Complete/exact evidence takes precedence over incomplete secondary
        # overlap. A partial match alone cannot resolve competing owners.
        if len(strongest)==1 and (highest>=2 or not any(p!=strongest[0] for p in possible)):
            owners[k]=strongest[0]
        elif definite or possible:
            uncertain[k]=dict(reason='multiple or incomplete existing ownership',
                              possible_owners=sorted(set([a for a,_ in definite]+possible)))
    must_separate={frozenset(s['children']) for s in splits}
    viable=[];actions=[]
    for split in splits:
        a,b=split['children']
        if a in uncertain or b in uncertain or (a in owners and b in owners and owners[a]==owners[b]):
            actions.append(dict(**split,status='ambiguous',reason='conflicting child ownership',child_owners=None))
        else:viable.append(split)
    unmatched=sorted({c for s in viable for c in s['children'] if c not in owners},
                     key=lambda k:_rank(entities[k]),reverse=True)
    new_clusters={};new_owner={}
    for child in unmatched:
        candidates=[anchor for anchor,members in new_clusters.items()
            if duplicate(child,anchor) and all(frozenset((child,k)) not in must_separate for k in members)
            and not any(compare(child,k)['relation'] in ('ambiguous','distinct') for k in members)]
        possible=[anchor for anchor in new_clusters if compare(child,anchor)['relation']=='ambiguous']
        if len(candidates)==1 and not possible:
            anchor=candidates[0];new_clusters[anchor].append(child);new_owner[child]=anchor
        elif candidates or possible:
            uncertain[child]=dict(reason='conflicting new-child ownership',possible_owners=sorted(set(candidates+possible)))
        else:
            new_clusters[child]=[child];new_owner[child]=child
    accepted=[]
    for split in viable:
        a,b=split['children']
        if a in uncertain or b in uncertain:
            actions.append(dict(**split,status='ambiguous',reason='new-child identity ambiguity',child_owners=None));continue
        assigned=[owners[c] if c in owners else new_owner[c] for c in (a,b)]
        if assigned[0]==assigned[1]:
            actions.append(dict(**split,status='ambiguous',reason='two children cannot share one identity',child_owners=None));continue
        action=dict(**split,status='reuse_existing' if all(c in owners for c in (a,b)) else 'accept_split',
                    reason='one identity per supported activity',child_owners=assigned)
        actions.append(action);accepted.append(action)
    # Resolve transaction dependencies monotonically: a rejected split cannot
    # erase an existing group or act as the sole bridge for another split.
    while True:
        active_children={c for a in accepted for c in a['children']}
        referenced={owner for a in accepted for owner in a['child_owners']}
        split_parents={a['parent_id'] for a in accepted}
        retired=set()
        for anchor,members in base_clusters.items():
            source_parents={p for k in members for p in entities[k]['source_parents']}
            complete_parents={m['event_id'] for k in members for m in entities[k]['original_measurements']
                              if m['evidence_kind']=='paired_phase'}
            # A remaining unpaired fragment is evidence, not an independently
            # complete event that can lock a decomposed container forever.
            # Preserve it explicitly as unresolved, never silently discard it.
            if (source_parents&split_parents and complete_parents<=split_parents and anchor not in referenced):
                retired.add(anchor)
        reject=set()
        for a in accepted:
            source=base_owner[a['base_id']]
            if source not in a['child_owners'] and source not in retired:
                reject.add(a['parent_id'])
                a['reason']='source group is still independently owned; cannot add two more identities'
        for anchor,members in new_clusters.items():
            active=[c for c in members if c in active_children]
            if not active:continue
            canonical=max(active,key=lambda k:_rank(entities[k]))
            if any(c!=canonical and not duplicate(c,canonical) for c in active):
                for a in accepted:
                    if anchor in a['child_owners']:
                        reject.add(a['parent_id']);a['reason']='new identity depends on rejected evidence bridge'
        if not reject:break
        for a in accepted:
            if a['parent_id'] in reject:a['status']='ambiguous';a['child_owners']=None
        accepted=[a for a in accepted if a['parent_id'] not in reject]
    finals=[];source_to_final={}
    def emit(anchor,members,kind):
        canonical=max(members,key=lambda k:_rank(entities[k]));e=entities[canonical]
        eid=f'event_proposal_{len(finals)+1:03d}'
        source_parents=sorted({p for k in members for p in entities[k]['source_parents']})
        finals.append(dict(event_proposal_id=eid,ownership_status='merged_evidence' if len(members)>1 else kind,
            canonical_source_entity_id=canonical,source_entity_ids=sorted(members),source_parent_ids=source_parents,
            from_split=any(entities[k]['source_kind']=='child' for k in members),
            supporting_sections=sorted(e['profile']),arrival_time_s=[e['arrival'][c] for c in sorted(e['profile'])],
            direction=e['direction'],canonical_measurement=deepcopy(e['canonical_measurement']),
            preserved_original_measurements=[deepcopy(m) for k in members for m in entities[k]['original_measurements']],
            phase_ridge_provenance=[deepcopy(p) for k in members for p in entities[k]['phase_ridge_provenance']],
            ownership_conflicts=[],event_count=None,event_frequency=None,reviewed=False))
        for k in members:source_to_final[k]=eid
    for anchor,members in sorted(base_clusters.items()):
        if anchor in retired:continue
        children=[c for c in active_children if owners.get(c)==anchor]
        emit(anchor,members+sorted(children),'retained_existing')
    for anchor,members in sorted(new_clusters.items()):
        active=[c for c in members if c in active_children]
        if active:emit(anchor,active,'retained_new_child')
    for action in actions:
        action['final_event_ids']=[source_to_final.get(c) for c in action['children']] if action['status']!='ambiguous' else []
    ambiguity=[dict(source_entity_id=k,**v) for k,v in sorted({**base_ambiguity,**uncertain}.items())]
    for event in finals:
        event['ownership_conflicts']=[a['source_entity_id'] for a in ambiguity
            if a['source_entity_id'] in event['source_entity_ids'] or any(owner in event['source_entity_ids'] for owner in a['possible_owners'])]
        event['ownership_conflicts'] += ['split:'+a['parent_id'] for a in actions if a['status']=='ambiguous'
                                        and a['parent_id'] in event['source_parent_ids']]
        if event['ownership_conflicts']:event['ownership_status']='ambiguous_preserved_existing'
    return dict(event_proposals=finals,split_actions=actions,ambiguous_ownership=ambiguity,
        source_to_final=source_to_final,retired_parent_groups=sorted(retired),
        unresolved_retired_fragments=[dict(parent_candidate_id=m['event_id'],source_group=anchor,
            original_measurement=deepcopy(m),status='unassigned_original_fragment',
            reason='Container split; partial fragment has no independently complete identity')
            for anchor in sorted(retired) for k in base_clusters[anchor] for m in entities[k]['original_measurements']
            if m['event_id'] not in split_parents],
        source_evidence=deepcopy(entities),
        pairwise_evidence=[dict(source_a=x,source_b=y,**value) for (x,y),value in sorted(relations.items())
                           if value['relation'] not in ('unrelated','distinct')],
        matching_rules=dict(native_sample_dt_s=dt,measurement_span_s=measurement_span_s,
            timing_tolerance_s=measurement_span_s+dt,minimum_shared_adjacent_sections=3,
            priority=['exact ordered phase nodes','complete phase and arrival structure','unique partial phase ownership'],
            no_transitive_bridge=True),event_count=None,event_frequency=None)
