================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/normalized_activity.py | git_blob_sha=1e051554c5ff57fb2a2493a9240aba8a036cec51 ================
"""Wang-inspired pointwise relative salience on unchanged radial maps.

The paper's magnitude is |curvature change|; here it is |filtered radial A|.
Min/max are fixed over the complete video and BOTH walls, not each candidate
or column. The paper does not specify this pooling scope. Cutoff=.6 and three
adjacent native supports reuse the published cutoff and current fit support.
"""
import numpy as np


def normalize_magnitude(filtered, valid):
    y = np.asarray(filtered, float); m = np.asarray(valid, bool)
    if y.shape != m.shape or y.ndim != 3 or y.shape[1] != 2:
        raise ValueError('Expected time x two walls x native sections with valid mask')
    supported = m & np.isfinite(y)
    magnitude = abs(y[supported]); normalized = np.full_like(y, np.nan)
    if not len(magnitude):
        return normalized, dict(status='no_filtered_support',minimum=None,maximum=None,
            physical_cutoff=None,cutoff=.6,normalization_scope='whole_video_both_walls')
    low = float(magnitude.min()); high = float(magnitude.max())
    normalized[supported] = (magnitude-low)/(high-low) if high > low else 0.
    return normalized, dict(status='normalized' if high>low else 'constant_magnitude',
        minimum=low,maximum=high,physical_cutoff=low+.6*(high-low),cutoff=.6,
        normalization_scope='whole_video_both_walls',valid_filtered_samples=int(supported.sum()),
        salient_samples=int((normalized[supported]>.6).sum()))


def score_salience(event, source_evidence, normalized, valid, time_s):
    entity = source_evidence[event['canonical_source_entity_id']]
    t = np.asarray(time_s, float); y = np.asarray(normalized, float); m = np.asarray(valid, bool)
    if y.shape != m.shape or y.ndim != 2 or y.shape[0] != len(t):
        raise ValueError('Expected one native wall with matching time and validity')
    ids = sorted(int(c) for c in entity['profile']); supported=[]; per_section=[]
    for col in ids:
        phases = entity['profile'].get(str(col),entity['profile'].get(col))
        lo = min(p['start_s'] for p in phases); hi = max(p['end_s'] for p in phases)
        a = int(np.searchsorted(t,lo-1e-8)); b = int(np.searchsorted(t,hi+1e-8,side='right'))
        good = b>a and m[a:b,col].all() and np.isfinite(y[a:b,col]).all()
        peak = float(y[a:b,col].max()) if good else None
        samples = int((y[a:b,col]>.6).sum()) if good else 0
        if good and peak>.6:supported.append(col)
        per_section.append(dict(section=col,normalized_peak=peak,salient_samples=samples,
                                phase_support_complete=bool(good)))
    blocks = [b.tolist() for b in np.split(np.asarray(supported,int),
              np.flatnonzero(np.diff(supported)!=1)+1) if len(b)>=3]
    retained = sorted(c for block in blocks for c in block)
    return dict(event_proposal_id=event['event_proposal_id'],
        canonical_source=event['canonical_source_entity_id'],cutoff=.6,
        decision='salient_support' if retained else 'insufficient_salient_support',
        original_sections=ids,salient_sections=supported,retained_sections=retained,
        native_contiguous_blocks=blocks,per_section=per_section,
        direction=event['direction'],direction_source='unchanged_original_proposal_not_refit_on_subset',
        ownership_status=event['ownership_status'],event_count=None,event_frequency=None)
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/normalized_activity.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/partial_phase_events.py | git_blob_sha=96355a97539334ea77eca2da7c443ca99dce43a0 ================
"""Sandbox: retain observable boundary peaks and propose fragment ownership.

Default detector and formal event interfaces are unchanged. Proposed groups
are not reviewed waves, and their number is not physiological frequency.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from unittest.mock import patch
import numpy as np
from uterine_wave import radial_events as baseline


def observed_lobes(y, valid, t, threshold):
    filtered = np.full_like(y, np.nan)
    columns = [[] for _ in range(y.shape[1])]
    unresolved = []
    minimum = max(3, int(np.ceil(12/np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    for col in range(y.shape[1]):
        for run_id, (begin, end) in enumerate(baseline._runs(valid[:, col])):
            if end-begin < minimum:
                continue
            signal = baseline.band_limited_signal(y[begin:end, col], t[begin:end])
            filtered[begin:end, col] = signal
            edges = np.r_[0, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(signal >= 0))+1, len(signal)]
            for sequence, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:])):
                peak = int(a+np.argmax(abs(signal[a:b])))
                amplitude = float(abs(signal[peak]))
                left, right = bool(a == 0), bool(b == len(signal))
                if amplitude <= max(float(threshold[col]), 1e-12):
                    continue
                if (left or right) and peak in (a, b-1):
                    unresolved.append(dict(column=col, run_id=run_id,
                        observed_start_s=float(t[begin+a]), observed_end_s=float(t[begin+b-1]),
                        peak_observed=False, left_censored=left, right_censored=right))
                    continue
                columns[col].append(dict(column=col, run_id=run_id, sequence=sequence,
                    sign=1 if signal[peak] > 0 else -1, peak_index=int(begin+peak),
                    peak_time_s=float(t[begin+peak]), amplitude=amplitude,
                    first=int(begin+a), last=int(begin+b-1),
                    start_s=float(t[begin+a]), end_s=float(t[begin+b-1]),
                    left_censored=left, right_censored=right, peak_observed=True))
    return filtered, columns, unresolved


def propose_groups(report):
    """Unique disjoint complementary ownership proposals, no automatic review.

    Only a whole single-phase candidate can attach, never silently assign a
    subset and lose its remaining support. Opposite temporal roles, ambiguous
    destinations and overlapping local phase nodes remain separate evidence.
    """
    events = report['events']
    paired = [e for e in events if e['evidence_kind'] == 'paired_phase']
    mapping = {e['event_id']:e['event_id'] for e in events}
    attachments, unresolved = {}, []
    used_sections = {e['event_id']:set(e['sections']) for e in paired}
    for event in events:
        if event['evidence_kind'] != 'single_phase':
            continue
        ridge = event['phase_ridge_ids'][0]
        choices = []
        for edge in report['alternative_phase_pairs']:
            if edge['selected'] or ridge not in edge['phase_ridge_ids']:
                continue
            if edge['sections'] != event['sections']:
                continue
            other = next(k for k in edge['phase_ridge_ids'] if k != ridge)
            for parent in paired:
                if (other in parent['phase_ridge_ids'] and
                    not set(edge['sections'])&set(parent['sections']) and
                    edge['leading_polarity'] == parent['leading_polarity'] and
                    edge['classification'] == parent['classification'] == event['classification'] and
                    event['classification'] in ('C2F', 'F2C')):
                    choices.append((parent, edge))
        if len(choices) != 1:
            if choices:
                unresolved.append(dict(candidate_id=event['event_id'], reason='multiple_possible_parent_events',
                                       possible_parents=[p['event_id'] for p, _ in choices]))
            continue
        parent, edge = choices[0]
        if set(edge['sections'])&used_sections[parent['event_id']]:
            unresolved.append(dict(candidate_id=event['event_id'], reason='competing_spatial_ownership'))
            continue
        used_sections[parent['event_id']].update(edge['sections'])
        mapping[event['event_id']] = parent['event_id']
        attachments[event['event_id']] = deepcopy(edge)
    groups = {}
    for event in events:
        groups.setdefault(mapping[event['event_id']], []).append(event['event_id'])
    return groups, attachments, unresolved


def detect_partial(signal, valid, time_s, space, *, noise_floor):
    original_ridges = baseline._ridges
    captured, unresolved_peaks, boundary_nodes = {}, [], []
    def lobes(y, supported, t, floor):
        filtered, columns, unresolved = observed_lobes(y, supported, t, floor)
        unresolved_peaks.extend(unresolved)
        boundary_nodes.extend(n for column in columns for n in column
                              if n['left_censored'] or n['right_censored'])
        return filtered, columns
    def ridges(columns, t, s):
        result = original_ridges(columns, t, s)
        captured.update({r['id']:r for r in result})
        return result
    with patch.object(baseline, '_lobes', lobes), patch.object(baseline, '_ridges', ridges):
        report = baseline.detect_radial_events(signal, valid, time_s, space, noise_floor=noise_floor)
    groups, attachments, unresolved = propose_groups(report)
    fragments = []
    for event in report['events']:
        piece = attachments.get(event['event_id'], event)
        nodes = []
        for rid in piece['phase_ridge_ids']:
            nodes.extend(n for n in captured[rid]['nodes'] if n['column'] in piece['sections'])
        leading = piece['phase_ridge_ids'][0]
        trailing = piece['phase_ridge_ids'][-1]
        # _measure_pair writes phase IDs in temporal order; singles have one.
        left = any(n['left_censored'] for n in captured[leading]['nodes'] if n['column'] in piece['sections'])
        right = any(n['right_censored'] for n in captured[trailing]['nodes'] if n['column'] in piece['sections'])
        fragments.append(dict(fragment_id=event['event_id'], source_candidate_id=event['event_id'],
            observed_start_s=piece['start_s'], observed_end_s=piece['end_s'],
            structure_start_s=None if left else piece['start_s'],
            structure_end_s=None if right else piece['end_s'],
            boundary_status=dict(start_unknown=left, end_unknown=right),
            sections=piece['sections'], arrival_time_s=piece['arrival_time_s'],
            arrival_marker=piece['arrival_marker'], direction=piece['classification'],
            phase_ridge_ids=piece['phase_ridge_ids'],
            local_phase_support=[dict(section=n['column'], run_id=n['run_id'], sign=n['sign'],
                observed_start_s=n['start_s'], observed_end_s=n['end_s'], peak_s=n['peak_time_s'],
                start_unknown=n['left_censored'], end_unknown=n['right_censored']) for n in nodes],
            time_bounds_semantics='Observed filtered-phase structure, not complete physiological contraction duration',
            relation='complementary_spatial_piece_proposal' if event['event_id'] in attachments else 'original_candidate',
            reviewed=False))
    report.update(candidate_algorithm='experimental_observable_partial_phase_v1',
        fragments=fragments,
        proposed_events=[dict(proposal_id=key, fragment_ids=ids, identity_reviewed=False)
                         for key, ids in groups.items()],
        ownership_ambiguities=unresolved,
        unresolved_boundary_segments=unresolved_peaks,
        observed_boundary_nodes=[dict(column=n['column'], run_id=n['run_id'],
            peak_time_s=n['peak_time_s'], observed_start_s=n['start_s'], observed_end_s=n['end_s'],
            left_censored=n['left_censored'], right_censored=n['right_censored'])
            for n in boundary_nodes],
        provisional_direction_groups=sum(any(report_event['classification'] in ('C2F', 'F2C')
            for report_event in report['events'] if report_event['event_id'] in ids) for ids in groups.values()),
        formal_patient_output=dict(event_count=None, event_frequency_cpm=None,
                                   status='identity_and_detection_not_validated'))
    return report


def anchor_to_existing_candidates(original, observed):
    """Use retained boundary evidence to repair existing proposals only.

    All original candidates remain traceable. Novel/unmatched boundary paths
    remain additional support, never automatically new independent events.
    Exact phase-node identity is used; no numerical match threshold is tuned.
    """
    def tokens(report, event):
        ridges = {r['ridge_id']:r for r in report['phase_ridges']}
        return {(ridges[k]['sign'], col, time) for k in event['phase_ridge_ids']
                for col, time in zip(ridges[k]['sections'], ridges[k]['peak_time_s'])
                if col in event['sections']}
    observed_by_id = {e['event_id']:e for e in observed['events']}
    pieces_by_id = {p['fragment_id']:p for p in observed['fragments']}
    group_tokens = {}
    for group in observed['proposed_events']:
        combined = set()
        for fragment_id in group['fragment_ids']:
            source = observed_by_id[fragment_id]
            piece = pieces_by_id[fragment_id]
            combined.update(tokens(observed, dict(source, sections=piece['sections'],
                                                   phase_ridge_ids=piece['phase_ridge_ids'])))
        group_tokens[group['proposal_id']] = combined
    destinations, ambiguity = {}, []
    for event in original['events']:
        signature = tokens(original, event)
        choices = [key for key, support in group_tokens.items() if signature <= support]
        if len(choices) == 1:
            destinations[event['event_id']] = choices[0]
        else:
            destinations[event['event_id']] = None
            ambiguity.append(dict(candidate_id=event['event_id'], reason='no_unique_complete_phase_support',
                                  possible_groups=choices))
    conflicting = set()
    for key in group_tokens:
        directions = {event['classification'] for event in original['events']
                      if destinations[event['event_id']] == key
                      and event['classification'] in ('C2F', 'F2C')}
        if len(directions) > 1:
            conflicting.add(key)
            ambiguity.append(dict(observed_group=key, reason='opposing_original_directions'))
    groups = {}
    for event in original['events']:
        destination = destinations[event['event_id']]
        key = ('observed', destination) if destination is not None and destination not in conflicting else ('original', event['event_id'])
        groups.setdefault(key, []).append(event)
    output = []
    used_observed = set()
    observed_groups = {g['proposal_id']:g for g in observed['proposed_events']}
    for number, (key, events) in enumerate(groups.items(), 1):
        if key[0] == 'observed':
            used_observed.add(key[1])
        output.append(dict(proposal_id=f'activity_proposal_{number:03d}',
            source_candidate_ids=[e['event_id'] for e in events],
            observed_fragment_ids=observed_groups[key[1]]['fragment_ids'] if key[0] == 'observed' else [],
            observed_support_group=key[1] if key[0] == 'observed' else None,
            direction_candidate=any(e['classification'] in ('C2F', 'F2C') for e in events),
            ownership_status='proposed_shared_identity' if len(events) > 1 else 'single_existing_candidate',
            identity_reviewed=False))
    return dict(existing_candidate_groups=output,
        additional_support_groups=[g for g in observed['proposed_events'] if g['proposal_id'] not in used_observed],
        mapping_ambiguities=ambiguity,
        existing_direction_group_proposals=sum(g['direction_candidate'] for g in output),
        formal_event_count=None, formal_event_frequency_cpm=None,
        interpretation='Support-assisted ownership proposals only; no new independent waves or reviewed identities')
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/partial_phase_events.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/patient_activity_aggregation.py | git_blob_sha=e63d565076d28e40e13229de257533f584eea137 ================
"""One patient-level proposal ledger; cross-wall identity is not event truth.

Reuse native sections and span+one-frame timing from existing ownership.
Walls are separate measurement channels, not separate patient wave counts.
"""
from copy import deepcopy
from itertools import combinations
import numpy as np

from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.direction_identity_trial import reconcile_identity_then_direction


def longest_adjacent(ids):
    ids=np.asarray(sorted(set(ids)),int)
    return max((len(b) for b in np.split(ids,np.flatnonzero(np.diff(ids)!=1)+1)),default=0)


def candidate_nodes(salience, ownership):
    nodes=[]
    for wall_record in salience['walls']:
        wall=wall_record['wall'];source=ownership['walls'][wall]['reconciliation']
        lookup={e['event_proposal_id']:e for e in source['event_proposals']}
        for score in wall_record['scores']:
            if score['decision']!='salient_support':continue
            e=lookup[score['event_proposal_id']];entity=source['source_evidence'][e['canonical_source_entity_id']]
            arrival=dict(zip(e['supporting_sections'],e['arrival_time_s']))
            ids=score['retained_sections']
            profile={c:deepcopy(entity['profile'][c] if c in entity['profile'] else entity['profile'][str(c)]) for c in ids}
            nodes.append(dict(node_id=f'wall{wall}/'+e['event_proposal_id'],wall=wall,
                source_proposal_id=e['event_proposal_id'],canonical_source=e['canonical_source_entity_id'],
                sections=ids,arrival={c:arrival[c] for c in ids},profile=profile,
                direction=e['direction'],original_classification=e['canonical_measurement'].get('classification'),
                arrival_marker=e['canonical_measurement'].get('arrival_marker'),
                original_ownership_status=e['ownership_status']))
    return nodes


def compare_walls(a,b,span,dt):
    if a['wall']==b['wall']:raise ValueError('Cross-wall comparison requires different walls')
    shared=sorted(set(a['sections'])&set(b['sections']));tolerance=span+dt
    matched=[];near=[];conflict=[]
    for c in shared:
        left,right=a['profile'][c],b['profile'][c]
        same_sequence=[p['sign'] for p in left]==[p['sign'] for p in right]
        pairs=[(p,q) for p in left for q in right if p['sign']==q['sign']]
        peak_near=any(abs(p['peak_s']-q['peak_s'])<=tolerance for p,q in pairs)
        comparable_arrival=a['arrival_marker']==b['arrival_marker']
        arrival_near=comparable_arrival and abs(a['arrival'][c]-b['arrival'][c])<=tolerance
        if peak_near or arrival_near:near.append(c)
        if not same_sequence or not comparable_arrival:
            if peak_near or arrival_near:conflict.append(c)
            continue
        # Each wall has its own continuity bounds. Their run indices need not
        # equal: they describe different tissue, not the same material point.
        valid=(all(p.get('epoch') is not None and q.get('epoch') is not None for p,q in pairs) and
            all(abs(p['peak_s']-q['peak_s'])<=tolerance and
                q['start_s']-dt<=p['peak_s']<=q['end_s']+dt and
                p['start_s']-dt<=q['peak_s']<=p['end_s']+dt for p,q in pairs) and arrival_near)
        if valid:matched.append(c)
        elif peak_near or arrival_near:conflict.append(c)
    direction_compatible=(a['direction']==b['direction'])
    relation='shared_identity_supported' if (longest_adjacent(matched)>=3 and
        len(matched)==len(shared) and not conflict and direction_compatible) else (
        'unresolved' if near else 'no_shared_identity_evidence')
    return dict(node_a=a['node_id'],node_b=b['node_id'],relation=relation,
        shared_sections=shared,matched_sections=matched,near_sections=near,
        phase_or_marker_conflict_sections=conflict,directions_compatible=direction_compatible,
        tolerance_s=tolerance,reason=('phase order, peaks, local arrival and direction agree' if relation=='shared_identity_supported'
            else 'partial/conflicting cross-wall evidence' if relation=='unresolved'
            else 'insufficient corresponding native phase/arrival evidence'))


def aggregate_nodes(nodes,span,dt):
    comparisons=[compare_walls(a,b,span,dt) for a,b in combinations(nodes,2) if a['wall']!=b['wall']]
    choices={n['node_id']:[] for n in nodes};uncertain={n['node_id']:[] for n in nodes}
    for edge in comparisons:
        a,b=edge['node_a'],edge['node_b']
        if edge['relation']=='shared_identity_supported':choices[a].append(b);choices[b].append(a)
        elif edge['relation']=='unresolved':uncertain[a].append(b);uncertain[b].append(a)
    # No transitive or one-to-many merging of consecutive activities.
    pairs={tuple(sorted((a,bs[0]))) for a,bs in choices.items() if len(bs)==1 and
           choices[bs[0]]==[a] and not uncertain[a] and not uncertain[bs[0]]}
    lookup={n['node_id']:n for n in nodes};used=set();groups=[]
    for a,b in sorted(pairs):groups.append([lookup[a],lookup[b]]);used.update((a,b))
    groups.extend([[n] for n in nodes if n['node_id'] not in used])
    groups.sort(key=lambda group:(min(float(np.median(list(n['arrival'].values()))) for n in group),group[0]['node_id']))
    ledger=[]
    for index,group in enumerate(groups,1):
        members=[n['node_id'] for n in group];known={n['direction'] for n in group if n['direction'] is not None}
        direction=next(iter(known)) if len(known)==1 else None
        unresolved=sorted({k for node in members for k in uncertain[node]+choices[node] if k not in members})
        phases=[p for n in group for ps in n['profile'].values() for p in ps]
        ledger.append(dict(patient_candidate_id=f'activity_{index:03d}',source_nodes=members,
            walls=sorted({n['wall'] for n in group}),classification='propagation_candidate' if direction else 'local_or_unresolved_candidate',
            direction=direction,source_measurements=deepcopy(group),cross_wall_merged=len(group)==2,
            ownership_status='unresolved_cross_wall' if unresolved else 'shared_identity_proposal' if len(group)==2 else 'single_wall_proposal',
            unresolved_related_nodes=unresolved,evidence_start_s=min(p['start_s'] for p in phases),
            evidence_end_s=max(p['end_s'] for p in phases),reviewed=False))
    # Conditional count range: how many identities remain if unresolved
    # cross-wall pairs are eventually shown to be shared? Not a confidence
    # interval and not permission to merge them now.
    graph={n['node_id']:[] for n in nodes if n['wall']==0 and n['node_id'] not in used}
    for edge in comparisons:
        a,b=edge['node_a'],edge['node_b']
        if a in used or b in used or edge['relation']=='no_shared_identity_evidence':continue
        if lookup[a]['wall']==1:a,b=b,a
        graph[a].append(b)
    owner={}
    def augment(a,seen):
        for b in graph[a]:
            if b in seen:continue
            seen.add(b)
            if b not in owner or augment(owner[b],seen):owner[b]=a;return True
        return False
    possible=sum(augment(a,set()) for a in graph)
    return dict(activities=ledger,cross_wall_evidence=comparisons,
        input_wall_proposal_rows=len(nodes),patient_candidate_rows=len(ledger),
        cross_wall_pairs_merged=len(pairs),unresolved_cross_wall_rows=sum(bool(r['unresolved_related_nodes']) for r in ledger),
        conditional_identity_count_range=[len(ledger)-possible,len(ledger)],
        conditional_range_semantics='if recorded unresolved cross-wall matches are shared; not a statistical confidence interval',
        formal_event_count=None,formal_event_frequency_cpm=None)


def _interval_union(begin, end, supported):
    intervals = []
    for a, b in zip(begin[supported], end[supported]):
        a = float(a); b = float(b)
        if intervals and a <= intervals[-1][1] + 1e-9:
            intervals[-1][1] = max(intervals[-1][1], b)
        else:
            intervals.append([a, b])
    return intervals


def _minimum_run_mask(raw_v, time_s, min_run_s):
    """Mirror filtered_runs() continuous-support eligibility, without filtering A.

    raw_v: time x wall x section boolean support.
    A sample becomes detector-eligible only if it belongs to a continuous
    per-(wall,section) run long enough for the current detector.
    """
    raw_v = np.asarray(raw_v, bool)
    t = np.asarray(time_s, float)
    if raw_v.ndim != 3 or raw_v.shape[0] != len(t):
        raise ValueError('raw_v must be time x wall x section and match time_s')
    if len(t) < 2 or not np.isfinite(t).all() or np.any(np.diff(t) <= 0):
        raise ValueError('time_s must contain at least two finite increasing samples')
    if min_run_s is None:
        return raw_v.copy(), None
    if not np.isfinite(min_run_s) or min_run_s <= 0:
        raise ValueError('min_run_s must be positive or None')

    minimum = max(3, int(np.ceil(float(min_run_s) / np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    eligible = np.zeros_like(raw_v)
    for wall in range(raw_v.shape[1]):
        for section in range(raw_v.shape[2]):
            runs = np.flatnonzero(
                np.diff(np.r_[False, raw_v[:, wall, section], False])
            ).reshape(-1, 2)
            for first, last in runs:
                if last - first >= minimum:
                    eligible[first:last, wall, section] = True
    return eligible, minimum


def observation_union(maps, *, min_run_s=12.0):
    """Detector-aligned observation-time denominator.

    Frequency exposure is the union of measurement intervals for which at
    least one wall has >=3 adjacent native sections that belong to a
    continuous per-section run satisfying the detector's minimum support.

    This is NOT a claim that 12 s is sufficient to estimate the whole
    0.5--5 cpm band. It only mirrors the current detector's minimum
    continuous-support rule so numerator and denominator use the same domain.
    """
    raw_v = np.asarray(maps['valid_mask'], bool) & np.isfinite(maps['A'])
    begin = np.asarray(maps['interval_start_s'], float)
    end = np.asarray(maps['interval_end_s'], float)
    t = np.asarray(maps.get('interval_center_s', 0.5 * (begin + end)), float)
    if (raw_v.ndim != 3 or raw_v.shape[1] != 2 or len(raw_v) != len(begin) or
            end.shape != begin.shape or t.shape != begin.shape or
            not np.isfinite(begin).all() or not np.isfinite(end).all() or
            np.any(end <= begin)):
        raise ValueError('Expected physical radial interval maps')

    v, minimum = _minimum_run_mask(raw_v, t, min_run_s)

    def spatially_observable(mask):
        if mask.shape[2] < 3:
            return np.zeros(len(mask), bool)
        return (mask[:, :, :-2] & mask[:, :, 1:-1] & mask[:, :, 2:]).any((1, 2))

    raw_supported = spatially_observable(raw_v)
    supported = spatially_observable(v)
    raw_intervals = _interval_union(begin, end, raw_supported)
    intervals = _interval_union(begin, end, supported)
    raw_seconds = float(sum(b-a for a, b in raw_intervals))
    seconds = float(sum(b-a for a, b in intervals))

    return dict(
        seconds=seconds,
        intervals_s=intervals,
        raw_seconds=raw_seconds,
        raw_observation_seconds=raw_seconds,
        filtered_observation_seconds=seconds,
        filtered_observation_fraction=(seconds/raw_seconds if raw_seconds > 0 else None),
        raw_intervals_s=raw_intervals,
        filtered_over_raw_observation_fraction=(seconds/raw_seconds if raw_seconds > 0 else None),
        min_run_s=None if min_run_s is None else float(min_run_s),
        minimum_run_samples=None if minimum is None else int(minimum),
        rule=('union of measurement intervals with >=3 adjacent native sections in either wall, '
              'after per-section continuous-run eligibility aligned to the event detector'),
        coverage_scope='locally observable radial activity, not proof of full-cavity visibility',
    )


def aggregate_by_direction(nodes,span,dt,seconds):
    """Direction first; tissue wall never becomes the reporting category.

    Same direction is necessary, not sufficient, for shared identity. Local,
    complex and unknown-direction proposals stay outside C2F/F2C frequency.
    """
    buckets={}
    for label in ('C2F','F2C','other'):
        selected=[n for n in nodes if n['direction']==label] if label!='other' else [n for n in nodes if n['direction'] not in ('C2F','F2C')]
        result=aggregate_nodes(selected,span,dt)
        for i,event in enumerate(result['activities'],1):event['patient_candidate_id']=f'{label}_{i:03d}'
        low,high=result['conditional_identity_count_range']
        result['candidate_rate_range_cpm']=[low*60/seconds,high*60/seconds] if seconds>0 else [None,None]
        result['direction_bucket']=label
        result['direction_source']='original arrival-order proposal, not radial strain sign or wall label'
        buckets[label]=result
    return buckets


def _identity_first_buckets(reconciled,seconds):
    """Adapt identity-first activities to the existing patient report interface."""
    buckets={}
    for label in ('C2F','F2C','other'):
        activities=[deepcopy(activity) for activity in reconciled['activities']
                    if (activity['direction']==label if label!='other'
                        else activity['direction'] not in ('C2F','F2C'))]
        for index,activity in enumerate(activities,1):
            activity['patient_candidate_id']=f'{label}_{index:03d}'
        count=len(activities);rate=count*60/seconds if seconds>0 else None
        buckets[label]=dict(activities=activities,patient_candidate_rows=count,
            conditional_identity_count_range=[count,count],
            candidate_rate_range_cpm=[rate,rate],direction_bucket=label,
            direction_source=('arrival refit on each contiguous salience-retained block; '
                              'cross-wall identity reconciled before direction'),
            formal_event_count=None,formal_event_frequency_cpm=None)
    return buckets


def patient_summary(salience,ownership,maps):
    dt=float(np.median(np.diff(maps['interval_center_s'])));span=float(np.median(maps['interval_dt_s']))
    nodes=candidate_nodes(salience,ownership)
    exposure=observation_union(maps);seconds=exposure['seconds']
    legacy=aggregate_nodes(nodes,span,dt)
    reconciled=reconcile_identity_then_direction(nodes,np.asarray(maps['s'],float),span,dt)
    activities=reconciled['activities'];count=len(activities)
    directional=sum(a['direction'] in ('C2F','F2C') for a in activities)
    unresolved_cross_wall=sum(bool(a['unresolved_related_nodes']) for a in activities)
    result=dict(activities=activities,cross_wall_evidence=reconciled['identity_comparisons'],
        input_wall_proposal_rows=len(nodes),patient_candidate_rows=count,
        cross_wall_pairs_merged=reconciled['cross_wall_identity_pairs'],
        unresolved_cross_wall_rows=unresolved_cross_wall,
        conditional_identity_count_range=[count,count],
        conditional_range_semantics='exact output of current identity-first algorithm; not a confidence interval',
        formal_event_count=None,formal_event_frequency_cpm=None)
    result.update(exposure=exposure,candidate_activity_rate_cpm=count*60/seconds if seconds>0 else None,
        unresolved_ledger_row_density_cpm=count*60/seconds if seconds>0 else None,
        conditional_candidate_rate_range_cpm=[count*60/seconds,count*60/seconds] if seconds>0 else [None,None],
        directional_candidate_rows=directional,local_or_unresolved_rows=count-directional,
        candidate_propagation_rate_cpm=directional*60/seconds if seconds>0 else None,
        directional_candidate_counts={d:sum(a['direction']==d for a in activities) for d in ('C2F','F2C')},
        rates_are_exploratory_candidate_densities=True,timing_tolerance_s=span+dt)
    buckets=_identity_first_buckets(reconciled,seconds)
    result.update(primary_reporting='identity_first_direction_second',directional_summaries={d:buckets[d] for d in ('C2F','F2C')},
        non_directional_summary=buckets['other'],
        directional_candidate_count_range=[sum(buckets[d]['conditional_identity_count_range'][i] for d in ('C2F','F2C')) for i in (0,1)],
        combined_activity_fields_are_secondary_QC=True,
        legacy_direction_first_QC=dict(patient_candidate_rows=legacy['patient_candidate_rows'],
            conditional_identity_count_range=legacy['conditional_identity_count_range'],
            cross_wall_pairs_merged=legacy['cross_wall_pairs_merged']),
        retained_direction_refit=True,identity_before_direction=True)
    return result

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/record_mse_core.py | git_blob_sha=20d99c73fd1aed6768c6a00ee9ed4263c9be4a89 ================
"""Frozen numeric-only ER-MSE core, copied verbatim from the historical implementation.

No files, clinical fields, labels or association/model modules are imported.
"""
import numpy as np


def _segments(rows):
    """Split one spatial block into chronological runs without crossing gaps."""
    rows = sorted(rows, key=lambda row: row["window_center_s"])
    times = np.asarray([row["window_center_s"] for row in rows], float)
    if len(times) == 1:
        return [rows]
    positive = np.diff(times)
    positive = positive[positive > 0]
    if not len(positive):
        raise ValueError("Repeated paired-window timestamps")
    step = float(np.median(positive))
    cuts = np.flatnonzero(np.diff(times) > 1.5 * step) + 1
    bounds = np.r_[0, cuts, len(rows)]
    return [rows[int(left):int(right)] for left, right in zip(bounds[:-1], bounds[1:])]


def _coordination(rows):
    x = np.asarray([row["anterior_ER"] for row in rows], float)
    y = np.asarray([row["posterior_ER"] for row in rows], float)
    if not np.isfinite(x).all() or not np.isfinite(y).all():
        raise ValueError("Paired ER values must be finite")
    mse = float(np.mean((x - y) ** 2))
    cc = (
        float(np.corrcoef(x, y)[0, 1])
        if len(rows) >= 3 and np.std(x) > 1e-12 and np.std(y) > 1e-12
        else None
    )
    return mse, cc


def _decompose(ant, post, observed_mse):
    """Decompose observed ER-MSE into bias, scale and time-shape terms."""
    ant = np.asarray(ant, float)
    post = np.asarray(post, float)
    mean_ant = float(ant.mean())
    mean_post = float(post.mean())
    sd_ant = float(ant.std(ddof=0))
    sd_post = float(post.std(ddof=0))
    mean_bias_sq = float((mean_ant - mean_post) ** 2)
    scale_difference_sq = float((sd_ant - sd_post) ** 2)
    if len(ant) >= 3 and sd_ant > 1e-12 and sd_post > 1e-12:
        cc = float(np.corrcoef(ant, post)[0, 1])
        shape_term = float(2 * sd_ant * sd_post * (1 - cc))
        standardized_mse = float(
            np.mean(
                (
                    (ant - mean_ant) / sd_ant
                    - (post - mean_post) / sd_post
                )
                ** 2
            )
        )
    else:
        shape_term = float(observed_mse - mean_bias_sq - scale_difference_sq)
        standardized_mse = None
    return dict(
        record_ER_mean_anterior=mean_ant,
        record_ER_mean_posterior=mean_post,
        record_ER_sd_anterior=sd_ant,
        record_ER_sd_posterior=sd_post,
        record_mean_bias_sq=mean_bias_sq,
        record_sd_scale_sq=scale_difference_sq,
        record_time_shape_term=shape_term,
        record_MSE_z=standardized_mse,
    )


def summarize_paired_windows(paired):
    """Return record-level coordination and all contributing temporal runs.

    Primary MSE gives each unique window time equal weight, then each spatial
    block at that time equal weight. ``pooled_MSE`` is a sensitivity metric in
    which every window-block observation is equally weighted. No missing time
    or unsupported spatial section is represented as zero.
    """
    if not paired:
        return dict(
            status="no_shared_supported_ER_windows",
            record_MSE_time_equal=None,
            record_MSE_pooled=None,
            record_CC_time_equal=None,
            record_ER_mean_anterior=None,
            record_ER_mean_posterior=None,
            record_ER_sd_anterior=None,
            record_ER_sd_posterior=None,
            record_mean_bias_sq=None,
            record_sd_scale_sq=None,
            record_time_shape_term=None,
            record_MSE_z=None,
            decomposition_status="unavailable_no_shared_support",
            paired_window_count=0,
            unique_window_count=0,
            spatial_block_count=0,
            temporal_segment_count=0,
            segment_MSE_mean=None,
            segment_MSE_median=None,
            window_span_s=None,
            segment_evidence=[],
        )

    by_block = {}
    for row in paired:
        key = (int(row["first_section"]), int(row["last_section"]))
        by_block.setdefault(key, []).append(row)

    segment_evidence = []
    all_blocks = []
    for (lo, hi), block_rows in sorted(by_block.items()):
        all_blocks.append(block_rows)
        for index, part in enumerate(_segments(block_rows), 1):
            mse, cc = _coordination(part)
            segment_evidence.append(
                dict(
                    first_section=lo,
                    last_section=hi,
                    segment_index=index,
                    window_count=len(part),
                    start_center_s=float(min(row["window_center_s"] for row in part)),
                    end_center_s=float(max(row["window_center_s"] for row in part)),
                    ER_MSE=mse,
                    ER_CC=cc,
                )
            )

    flat = [row for block in all_blocks for row in block]
    pooled_mse = _coordination(flat)[0]

    # One series per recording: average available spatial-block ER at each
    # identical window time, then calculate the MSE and CC over time.
    by_time = {}
    for row in flat:
        key = (float(row["window_start_s"]), float(row["window_end_s"]), float(row["window_center_s"]))
        by_time.setdefault(key, []).append(row)
    ordered_times = sorted(by_time)
    ant = np.asarray([np.mean([row["anterior_ER"] for row in by_time[key]]) for key in ordered_times])
    post = np.asarray([np.mean([row["posterior_ER"] for row in by_time[key]]) for key in ordered_times])
    error_by_time = np.asarray(
        [np.mean([(row["anterior_ER"] - row["posterior_ER"]) ** 2 for row in by_time[key]]) for key in ordered_times]
    )
    observed_mse = float(np.mean(error_by_time))
    if max(len(rows) for rows in by_time.values()) == 1:
        decomposition = _decompose(ant, post, observed_mse)
        decomposition["decomposition_status"] = "one_block_per_window_time"
    else:
        decomposition = {
            "record_ER_mean_anterior": None,
            "record_ER_mean_posterior": None,
            "record_ER_sd_anterior": None,
            "record_ER_sd_posterior": None,
            "record_mean_bias_sq": None,
            "record_sd_scale_sq": None,
            "record_time_shape_term": None,
            "record_MSE_z": None,
            "decomposition_status": "not_defined_for_multiple_blocks_per_time",
        }
    record_cc = (
        float(np.corrcoef(ant, post)[0, 1])
        if len(ordered_times) >= 3 and np.std(ant) > 1e-12 and np.std(post) > 1e-12
        else None
    )
    centers = np.asarray([key[2] for key in ordered_times], float)
    positive = np.diff(centers)
    positive = positive[positive > 0]
    step = float(np.median(positive)) if len(positive) else 0.0
    gaps = np.diff(centers) > 1.5 * step if step else np.zeros(0, bool)
    segment_array = np.asarray([row["ER_MSE"] for row in segment_evidence], float)
    return dict(
        status="available_on_shared_ER_support",
        record_MSE_time_equal=observed_mse,
        record_MSE_pooled=float(pooled_mse),
        record_CC_time_equal=record_cc,
        **decomposition,
        paired_window_count=len(flat),
        unique_window_count=len(ordered_times),
        spatial_block_count=len(by_block),
        temporal_segment_count=len(segment_evidence),
        temporal_gap_count=int(gaps.sum()),
        segment_MSE_mean=float(segment_array.mean()),
        segment_MSE_median=float(np.median(segment_array)),
        window_span_s=float(max(row["window_end_s"] for row in flat) - min(row["window_start_s"] for row in flat)),
        first_section=min(key[0] for key in by_block),
        last_section=max(key[1] for key in by_block),
        segment_evidence=segment_evidence,
    )

"""Research measurements of uterine tissue deformation and wave patterns."""
__version__ = "0.1.0"

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/_lk.py | git_blob_sha=8d0c6b2182b49ddcfbfa353a9084d7556916363d ================
"""Existing CUDA LK and grayscale QC, without machine-specific imports."""
import cv2
import numpy as np

_FLOWS = {}


def local_contrast(gray, sigma=5.):
    source = gray.astype(np.float32)
    mean = cv2.GaussianBlur(source, (0, 0), sigma)
    square = cv2.GaussianBlur(source*source, (0, 0), sigma)
    std = np.sqrt(np.maximum(square-mean*mean, 0))
    mapped = 128+35*(source-mean)/(std+5)
    return np.uint8(np.where(gray > 12, np.clip(np.rint(mapped), 0, 255), 0))


def patch(image, point, radius):
    x, y = point
    h, w = image.shape
    if not np.isfinite(point).all() or min(x,y) < radius+1 or x >= w-radius-1 or y >= h-radius-1:
        return None
    return cv2.getRectSubPix(image, (2*radius+1, 2*radius+1), (float(x), float(y)))


def flow(a, b, p, window, initial=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, np.float32).reshape(1, -1, 2)
    if initial is not None:
        initial = np.asarray(initial, np.float32)
        if initial.shape != p.reshape(-1, 2).shape or not np.isfinite(initial).all():
            raise ValueError("Initial target must be finite Nx2")
    key = (window, initial is not None)
    if key not in _FLOWS:
        if cv2.cuda.getCudaEnabledDeviceCount() < 1:
            raise RuntimeError("CUDA OpenCV with cudaoptflow is required; no CPU fallback is used")
        cv2.cuda.setDevice(0)
        factory = getattr(cv2, 'cuda_SparsePyrLKOpticalFlow', None)
        _FLOWS[key] = (factory.create((window, window), 3, 30, initial is not None)
                      if factory is not None else
                      cv2.cuda.SparsePyrLKOpticalFlow_create((window, window), 3, 30, initial is not None))
    def upload(x):
        gpu = cv2.cuda_GpuMat()
        gpu.upload(np.ascontiguousarray(x))
        return gpu
    target = upload(initial.reshape(1, -1, 2)) if initial is not None else None
    q, status, err = _FLOWS[key].calc(upload(a), upload(b), upload(p), target)
    return q.download().reshape(-1, 1, 2), status.download().reshape(-1, 1), None


def match(a,b,qa,qb,p,window,initial=None,*,max_fb_px=.75,qc_scale=1.,qc_window=None,return_diagnostics=False):
    found,ok,_=flow(a,b,p,window,initial)
    points=found[:,0];back,bok,_=flow(b,a,points,window,p if initial is not None else None)
    fb=np.linalg.norm(back[:,0]-p,axis=1)
    good=ok.ravel().astype(bool)&bok.ravel().astype(bool)&np.isfinite(points).all(1)&(fb<=max_fb_px)
    # Pixel-center convention of spatial downsampling; localization stays native.
    qp,qq=(p,points) if qc_scale==1 else ((p+.5)/qc_scale-.5,(points+.5)/qc_scale-.5)
    radius=int(round((window//2)/qc_scale)) if qc_window is None else qc_window//2
    score=np.full(len(p),-1.);reason=np.full(len(p),'fb_or_flow_rejected',dtype='U24')
    for j in np.flatnonzero(good):
        x,y=patch(qa,qp[j],radius),patch(qb,qq[j],radius)
        if x is None or y is None:good[j]=False;reason[j]='patch_outside';continue
        if min(x.std(),y.std())<2:good[j]=False;reason[j]='low_texture';continue
        x=x.astype(float).ravel();y=y.astype(float).ravel();x-=x.mean();y-=y.mean();den=np.linalg.norm(x)*np.linalg.norm(y)
        score[j]=float(x@y/den) if den>1e-10 else -1.
        good[j]=score[j]>=.75;reason[j]='accepted' if good[j] else 'ncc_rejected'
    result = (points,good,score,fb,reason)
    if return_diagnostics:
        return (*result, dict(forward_status=ok.ravel().astype(bool),
                              backward_status=bok.ravel().astype(bool)))
    return result

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/amplitude.py | git_blob_sha=458e7da3f63af67a0eaba34d18402f72adb764fc ================
"""Amplitude-related summaries for complete radial-strain time blocks."""
import numpy as np


def band_limited_signal(values, time_s, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Return a zero-phase FFT band pass of one complete time block.

    ``values`` may have any trailing spatial dimensions.  The result has those
    trailing dimensions.  This is an interval-strain variability measure, not
    a single-event peak amplitude.  Missing samples are not filled.
    """
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    time_s = np.asarray(time_s, float)
    if values.ndim < 1 or values.shape[0] != len(time_s) or len(time_s) < 3:
        raise ValueError('Expected time-first values with at least three samples')
    if not np.isfinite(values).all() or not np.isfinite(time_s).all():
        raise ValueError('Band-limited SD requires a complete finite block')
    dt = np.diff(time_s)
    if np.any(dt <= 0) or not np.allclose(dt, np.median(dt), rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-9):
        raise ValueError('FFT amplitude requires uniform real timestamps')
    if not 0 < band_cpm[0] < band_cpm[1]:
        raise ValueError('Invalid temporal frequency band')
    centered = values - values.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    spectrum = np.fft.rfft(centered, axis=0)
    frequencies = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(time_s), np.median(dt))
    keep = (frequencies >= band_cpm[0]/60) & (frequencies <= band_cpm[1]/60)
    spectrum[~keep] = 0
    return np.fft.irfft(spectrum, n=len(time_s), axis=0)


def band_limited_sd(values, time_s, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Return per-column SD after band limiting a complete time block."""
    return np.std(band_limited_signal(values, time_s, band_cpm), axis=0)


def summarize_interval_strain(values, time_s, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Raw and physiological-band SD for one complete interval-strain block."""
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    band_sd = band_limited_sd(values, time_s, band_cpm)
    raw_sd = np.std(values, axis=0)
    resolution = 60/(len(time_s)*np.median(np.diff(np.asarray(time_s, float))))
    bins = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(time_s), np.median(np.diff(time_s)))
    positive_bins = int(((bins >= band_cpm[0]/60) & (bins <= band_cpm[1]/60)).sum())
    return dict(
        definition='band_limited_interval_radial_strain_SD',
        interpretation='radial_stretch_activity_amplitude',
        units='dimensionless',
        depends_on_measurement_interval_and_motion_rate=True,
        raw_SD_per_pair=raw_sd,
        raw_SD_mean=float(raw_sd.mean()),
        band_SD_per_pair=band_sd,
        band_SD_mean=float(band_sd.mean()),
        band_cpm=list(band_cpm),
        time_resolution_cpm=float(resolution),
        positive_frequency_bins=positive_bins,
        duration_s=float(time_s[-1]-time_s[0]),
    )

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/deformation.py | git_blob_sha=15ebcbd140a72dc36a0205116220b54bf874e7af ================
"""Signed interval deformation with explicit topology and continuity masks."""
import numpy as np

DEFAULT_PROPAGATION_INTERVAL_S = 2.0
SENSITIVITY_INTERVAL_S = .96


def validate_time(time_s, count, hop):
    t = np.asarray(time_s, float)
    if t.shape != (count,) or not np.isfinite(t).all() or np.any(np.diff(t) <= 0):
        raise ValueError("Expected finite, strictly increasing acquisition times")
    if not isinstance(hop, (int, np.integer)) or not 1 <= hop < count:
        raise ValueError("hop must be an integer between 1 and frame_count-1")
    return t


def interval_support(tracks, hop):
    """No invalid step, segment change or correction-epoch change may be crossed."""
    valid = np.asarray(tracks['target_valid'], bool).copy()
    if valid.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError("target_valid must be time x point")
    validate_time(tracks['time_s'], len(valid), hop)
    if 'pose_valid' in tracks:
        pose_valid = np.asarray(tracks['pose_valid'], bool)
        if pose_valid.shape != (len(valid),):
            raise ValueError('pose_valid must have one entry per frame')
        valid &= pose_valid[:, None]
    step = np.asarray(tracks['step_valid'], bool).copy()
    segment = np.asarray(tracks['segment_id'])
    if step.shape != valid.shape or segment.shape != valid.shape:
        raise ValueError("Track validity and segment arrays must share a shape")
    epoch = np.asarray(tracks.get('pose_epoch', np.zeros(len(valid), int)))
    if epoch.shape != (len(valid),):
        raise ValueError("pose_epoch must have one entry per frame")
    step[1:] &= valid[1:] & valid[:-1]
    step[1:] &= segment[1:] == segment[:-1]
    step[1:] &= (epoch[1:] == epoch[:-1])[:, None]
    failures = np.vstack((np.zeros((1, valid.shape[1]), int), np.cumsum(~step[1:], axis=0)))
    return valid[hop:] & valid[:-hop] & (failures[hop:] - failures[:-hop] == 0)


def rate_A(points, grid, time_s, hop=8):
    """(L(t)-L(t-hop))/L(t-hop)/dt, positive for radial lengthening; s^-1."""
    points = np.asarray(points, float)
    t = validate_time(time_s, len(points), hop)
    p = points[:, grid['A_pair_ids']]
    lengths = np.linalg.norm(p[:, :, :, 1] - p[:, :, :, 0], axis=-1)
    result = np.full_like(lengths[hop:], np.nan)
    denominator = lengths[:-hop] * (t[hop:] - t[:-hop])[:, None, None]
    np.divide(lengths[hop:] - lengths[:-hop], denominator, out=result, where=denominator > 0)
    return result


def curvature(points, arc):
    """Signed curvature of a local 5-seed quadratic in initial arc coordinates."""
    points = np.asarray(points, float)
    arc = np.asarray(arc, float)
    if points.ndim != 3 or points.shape[2] != 2 or points.shape[1] != len(arc):
        raise ValueError("Expected time x spatial_point x xy and matching arc")
    if len(arc) < 5 or not np.isfinite(arc).all() or np.any(np.diff(arc) <= 0):
        raise ValueError("Curvature needs five distinct ordered spatial samples")
    out = np.full((len(points), len(arc) - 4), np.nan)
    for j in range(2, len(arc) - 2):
        s = arc[j-2:j+3] - arc[j]
        inverse = np.linalg.pinv(np.column_stack((np.ones(5), s, s*s)))
        coef = np.einsum('ij,tjk->tik', inverse, points[:, j-2:j+3])
        v, a = coef[:, 1], 2*coef[:, 2]
        denominator = np.linalg.norm(v, axis=1)**3
        numerator = v[:, 0]*a[:, 1] - v[:, 1]*a[:, 0]
        np.divide(numerator, denominator, out=out[:, j-2], where=denominator > 1e-20)
    return out


def rate_B(points, grid, time_s, hop=8):
    """Curvature change per second; (processed pixel * second)^-1."""
    t = validate_time(time_s, len(points), hop)
    values = []
    arcs = []
    for ids, arc in zip(grid['B_curve_ids'], grid['B_arc_positions']):
        kappa = curvature(np.asarray(points)[:, ids], arc)
        values.append((kappa[hop:] - kappa[:-hop]) / (t[hop:] - t[:-hop])[:, None])
        arcs.append(arc[2:-2])
    return np.stack(values, axis=1), np.asarray(arcs)


def compute_maps(tracks, grid, hop=8):
    """A IDs address A seeds, B IDs address B seeds; tracking order is [A, B]."""
    points = np.asarray(tracks['corrected'], float)
    count_a, count_b = len(grid['A_points']), len(grid['B_points'])
    if points.shape != (len(tracks['time_s']), count_a + count_b, 2):
        raise ValueError("Expected corrected trajectories in explicit [A_points, B_points] order")
    effective = dict(tracks)
    effective['target_valid'] = np.asarray(tracks['target_valid'], bool) & np.isfinite(points).all(2)
    support = interval_support(effective, hop)
    if support.shape[1] != count_a + count_b:
        raise ValueError("Validity shape differs from trajectory shape")
    support &= np.isfinite(points[hop:]).all(2) & np.isfinite(points[:-hop]).all(2)
    a = rate_A(points[:, :count_a], grid, tracks['time_s'], hop)
    good_a = support[:, :count_a][:, grid['A_pair_ids']].all(3) & np.isfinite(a)
    b, arcs = rate_B(points[:, count_a:], grid, tracks['time_s'], hop)
    good_b = np.stack([
        np.stack([support[:, count_a:][:, ids[j-2:j+3]].all(1)
                  for j in range(2, len(ids)-2)], axis=1)
        for ids in grid['B_curve_ids']
    ], axis=1) & np.isfinite(b)
    return dict(A=np.where(good_a, a, np.nan), B=np.where(good_b, b, np.nan),
                A_valid=good_a, B_valid=good_b, time_s=np.asarray(tracks['time_s'])[hop:],
                A_arc_positions=grid['arc_positions'], B_arc_positions=arcs)


def compute_spacetime(tracks, grid, *, interval_s=DEFAULT_PROPAGATION_INTERVAL_S):
    """A0 trajectories with a ~2 s primary propagation measurement interval.

    Choose a native-frame hop near the requested physical interval; retain its
    actual per-sample duration. Legacy compute_maps time_s stays right-ended.
    s remains the full annotated wall's normalized arc, without stretching a
    retained/valid subregion back to 0..1. No interpolation or smoothing.
    Explicit interval_s=.96 retains the higher-resolution sensitivity scale.
    """
    if not np.isfinite(interval_s) or interval_s <= 0:
        raise ValueError('interval_s must be finite and positive')
    t = validate_time(tracks['time_s'], len(tracks['corrected']), 1)
    hop = max(1, int(np.floor(interval_s/np.median(np.diff(t))+.5)))
    maps = compute_maps(tracks, grid, hop)
    s = np.asarray(grid['u'], float)
    if s.shape != (maps['A'].shape[2],) or not np.isfinite(s).all() or np.any(np.diff(s) <= 0) or np.any((s < 0) | (s > 1)):
        raise ValueError('Expected ordered normalized full-wall spatial coordinates')
    duration = t[hop:]-t[:-hop]
    return dict(A=maps['A'], epsilon=maps['A']*duration[:, None, None],
                valid_mask=maps['A_valid'], s=s.copy(),
                interval_start_s=t[:-hop].copy(), interval_end_s=t[hop:].copy(),
                interval_center_s=(t[:-hop]+t[hop:])/2, interval_dt_s=duration,
                hop=hop, nominal_interval_s=float(interval_s),
                A_arc_positions=maps['A_arc_positions'])
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/deformation.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/events.py | git_blob_sha=9bd0b170b58104b006fd16c53ee1112935a955ab ================
"""Measure reviewed propagation events on the existing radial maps.

Arrival annotations describe the same contraction/relaxation structure across
positions. They are not detected from individual colour bands or ER windows.
One event_id identifies one activity across walls; a reflection is a new event.
"""
import numpy as np


def measure_event(spacetime, event):
    """Fit arrival time = intercept + slope*s; speed = 1/slope.

    Required: event_id, wall (0/1), start_s, end_s, sections (zero based),
    arrival_time_s, reviewed. Three distinct adjacent sections are a numerical
    fitting requirement, not a published physiological minimum wave extent.
    Non-monotone arrival sequences remain complex, with no single speed.
    """
    t = np.asarray(spacetime['interval_center_s'], float)
    s = np.asarray(spacetime['s'], float)
    wall = event['wall']
    ids = np.asarray(event['sections'])
    arrival = np.asarray(event['arrival_time_s'], float)
    start, end = float(event['start_s']), float(event['end_s'])
    if (wall not in (0, 1) or ids.ndim != 1 or len(ids) < 3 or
            not np.issubdtype(ids.dtype, np.integer) or
            np.any(np.diff(ids) != 1) or ids[0] < 0 or ids[-1] >= len(s)):
        raise ValueError('Event needs wall 0/1 and at least three adjacent section indices')
    if (not event['event_id'] or arrival.shape != ids.shape or not np.isfinite(arrival).all()
            or not np.isfinite([start, end]).all() or not start < end
            or np.any(arrival < start) or np.any(arrival > end)):
        raise ValueError('Event arrivals must be finite and inside its time bounds')
    row = dict(event_id=str(event['event_id']), wall=int(wall),
               start_s=start, end_s=end, sections=ids.tolist(),
               s_start=float(s[ids[0]]), s_end=float(s[ids[-1]]),
               spatial_span=float(s[ids[-1]]-s[ids[0]]),
               arrival_time_s=arrival.tolist(), reviewed=bool(event['reviewed']),
               arrival_time_slope=None, velocity_paths_per_s=None,
               event_interval_strain_SD=None, direction=None)
    if not row['reviewed']:
        return dict(row, status='needs_review')
    select = (t >= start) & (t <= end)
    valid = np.asarray(spacetime['valid_mask'], bool)
    a = np.asarray(spacetime['A'], float)
    eps = np.asarray(spacetime['epsilon'], float)
    if (start < t[0] or end > t[-1] or select.sum() < 3 or
            not valid[select, wall][:, ids].all() or
            not np.isfinite(a[select, wall][:, ids]).all() or
            not np.isfinite(eps[select, wall][:, ids]).all()):
        return dict(row, status='unsupported_time_or_segment_gap')
    # Keep the original finite-interval meaning. A short-event SD is unfiltered:
    # narrow event windows cannot represent the full physiological frequency band.
    row['event_interval_strain_SD'] = float(np.std(eps[select, wall][:, ids], axis=0).mean())
    if row['event_interval_strain_SD'] <= 1e-12:
        return dict(row, direction='local_or_synchronous', status='no_resolved_propagation')
    slope, intercept = np.polyfit(s[ids], arrival, 1)
    residual = arrival-(intercept+slope*s[ids])
    total = float(np.sum((arrival-arrival.mean())**2))
    row.update(arrival_time_slope=float(slope),
               arrival_fit_R2=float(1-np.sum(residual**2)/total) if total > 1e-20 else None,
               arrival_fit_RMSE_s=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(residual**2))),
               arrival_time_intercept=float(intercept), amplitude_units='dimensionless',
               amplitude_definition='mean_per_pair_unfiltered_event_interval_strain_SD')
    tolerance = float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
    delta = np.diff(arrival)
    if np.ptp(arrival) <= tolerance:
        return dict(row, direction='local_or_synchronous', status='no_resolved_propagation')
    monotone = np.all(delta >= -tolerance) or np.all(delta <= tolerance)
    if not monotone:
        return dict(row, direction='complex', status='reviewed_complex_activity')
    if abs(slope)*(s[ids[-1]]-s[ids[0]]) <= tolerance:
        return dict(row, direction='local_or_synchronous', status='no_resolved_propagation')
    return dict(row, direction='C2F' if slope > 0 else 'F2C',
                velocity_paths_per_s=float(1/slope), status='reviewed_propagation')


def pair_wall_events(events, space):
    """Pair only explicitly reviewed shared event IDs; never sum both walls."""
    pairs = []
    for event_id in sorted({row['event_id'] for row in events}):
        rows = [row for row in events if row['event_id'] == event_id and
                row['status'] in ('reviewed_propagation', 'reviewed_complex_activity')]
        if len(rows) != 2 or {row['wall'] for row in rows} != {0, 1}:
            continue
        anterior, posterior = sorted(rows, key=lambda row: row['wall'])
        common = sorted(set(anterior['sections']) & set(posterior['sections']))
        if not common:
            continue
        # Only shared sampled positions; no spatial interpolation.
        ta = dict(zip(anterior['sections'], anterior['arrival_time_s']))
        tp = dict(zip(posterior['sections'], posterior['arrival_time_s']))
        lag = [tp[j]-ta[j] for j in common]
        pairs.append(dict(event_id=event_id, sections=common,
                          s=np.asarray(space)[common].tolist(),
                          directions_agree=anterior['direction'] == posterior['direction'],
                          posterior_minus_anterior_arrival_s=lag,
                          mean_arrival_difference_s=float(np.mean(lag))))
    return pairs

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/geometry.py | git_blob_sha=6cc63a645a0a81f4c1d4b77d7134b8c8bbfcae00 ================
"""Anatomically ordered seeds in processed-image xy coordinates."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np

CONFIG = dict(window_px=21, spacing_px=10.5, radial_baseline_px=21.,
              endpoint_margin_px=21., band_fractions=[.25, .5, .75],
              coordinate_scale=.5)


def read_labelme(path, *, coordinate_scale=.5):
    """Read only first-frame geometry; imageData and other labels are ignored."""
    if not np.isfinite(coordinate_scale) or coordinate_scale <= 0:
        raise ValueError("coordinate_scale must be positive")
    document = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    names = ("ant_wall", "post_wall", "cervix_point", "fundus_point")
    shapes = {}
    for name in names:
        entries = [s for s in document["shapes"] if s["label"] == name]
        if len(entries) != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected one {name} annotation")
        shapes[name] = np.asarray(entries[0]["points"], float) * coordinate_scale
    validate_shapes(shapes)
    return shapes


def validate_shapes(shapes):
    for name in ("ant_wall", "post_wall", "cervix_point", "fundus_point"):
        p = np.asarray(shapes[name], float)
        required = 2 if name.endswith("wall") else 1
        if p.ndim != 2 or p.shape[1] != 2 or len(p) < required or not np.isfinite(p).all():
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid {name} geometry")
        if not name.endswith("wall") and len(p) != 1:
            raise ValueError(f"{name} must contain one point")
    if np.linalg.norm(shapes["cervix_point"][0] - shapes["fundus_point"][0]) < 1e-8:
        raise ValueError("Cervix and fundus must be distinct")


def orient(rail,cervix,fundus):
 rail=np.asarray(rail,float)
 if rail.ndim!=2 or rail.shape[1]!=2 or len(rail)<2:raise ValueError('Expected 2D wall polyline')
 if np.linalg.norm(rail[0]-cervix)+np.linalg.norm(rail[-1]-fundus)>np.linalg.norm(rail[-1]-cervix)+np.linalg.norm(rail[0]-fundus):rail=rail[::-1]
 if np.any(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(rail,axis=0),axis=1)<=1e-8):raise ValueError('Repeated wall vertices')
 return rail
def sample(rail,u):
 distances=np.r_[0.,np.cumsum(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(rail,axis=0),axis=1))]
 s=u*distances[-1]
 return np.column_stack([np.interp(s,distances,rail[:,dim]) for dim in range(2)]),distances[-1]
def layout(shapes,config=CONFIG):
 validate_shapes(shapes)
 if min(config['spacing_px'],config['radial_baseline_px'])<=0 or config['endpoint_margin_px']<0:raise ValueError('Invalid sampling lengths')
 if not config['band_fractions'] or any(not 0<f<1 for f in config['band_fractions']):raise ValueError('Band fractions must lie inside (0,1)')
 cervix=shapes['cervix_point'][0];fundus=shapes['fundus_point'][0]
 ra=orient(shapes['ant_wall'],cervix,fundus);rp=orient(shapes['post_wall'],cervix,fundus)
 lengths=[float(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(r,axis=0),axis=1).sum()) for r in [ra,rp]]
 trim=config['endpoint_margin_px']/min(lengths)
 if trim>=.5:raise ValueError('Annotated path shorter than the proposed endpoint margins')
 count=int(np.ceil(max(lengths)*(1-2*trim)/config['spacing_px']))+1
 u=np.linspace(trim,1-trim,count)
 aa,la=sample(ra,u);pp,lp=sample(rp,u)
 rails=np.stack((aa,pp));center=(aa+pp)/2
 normals=[];radial=[];normal_tangent_error=[]
 for wi,boundary in enumerate((aa,pp)):
  tangent=np.gradient(boundary,axis=0)
  tangent/=np.linalg.norm(tangent,axis=1)[:,None]
  normal=np.column_stack((-tangent[:,1],tangent[:,0]))
  # Orient the normal away from the annotated endometrial cavity.
  normal*=np.where(np.einsum('ij,ij->i',normal,boundary-center)>=0,1.,-1.)[:,None]
  outer=boundary+config['radial_baseline_px']*normal
  radial.append(np.stack((boundary,outer),axis=1));normals.append(normal)
  normal_tangent_error.append(float(np.max(np.abs(np.einsum('ij,ij->i',normal,tangent)))))
 band=np.stack([(1-frac)*aa+frac*pp for frac in config['band_fractions']])
 # All IDs are stable; pair topology is explicit instead of relying on adjacency.
 a_points=np.concatenate([pairs.reshape(-1,2) for pairs in radial])
 a_pairs=np.arange(len(a_points)).reshape(2,count,2)
 b_points=band.reshape(-1,2);b_curves=np.arange(len(b_points)).reshape(len(config['band_fractions']),count)
 arc=[u*la,u*lp]
 # Geometry is resampled only at initialization; measured trajectories are not interpolated.
 return dict(u=u,radial=np.array(radial),normals=np.array(normals),band=band,wall=rails,
  A_points=a_points,A_pair_ids=a_pairs,B_points=b_points,B_curve_ids=b_curves,
  arc_positions=np.array(arc),B_arc_positions=np.array([np.r_[0.,np.cumsum(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(curve,axis=0),axis=1))] for curve in band]),wall_lengths=lengths,tangent_normal_error=max(normal_tangent_error))

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/io.py | git_blob_sha=48ab492f0e947af0b104b2f08aab6a9288ce317e ================
"""Local video decoding. Retain all decoded frames and source presentation times."""
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np


def read_video(path, *, time_factor, coordinate_scale=.5):
    """time_factor=3 for a verified 3x speed-up; factor is never inferred from a name."""
    import cv2
    if time_factor <= 0 or not np.isfinite(time_factor) or not 0 < coordinate_scale <= 1:
        raise ValueError("Invalid time factor or coordinate scale")
    capture = cv2.VideoCapture(str(Path(path)))
    if not capture.isOpened():
        raise ValueError("Cannot open video")
    frames, times = [], []
    try:
        while True:
            ok, image = capture.read()
            if not ok:
                break
            times.append(capture.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_MSEC)/1000*time_factor)
            if coordinate_scale != 1:
                image = cv2.resize(image, None, fx=coordinate_scale, fy=coordinate_scale,
                                   interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
            frames.append(cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY))
    finally:
        capture.release()
    t = np.asarray(times, float)
    if len(t) < 2 or not np.isfinite(t).all() or np.any(np.diff(t) <= 0):
        raise ValueError("Decoder did not provide increasing timestamps; supply explicit acquisition times via the Python API")
    return frames, t-t[0]


def jsonable(value):
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return value.tolist()
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {k: jsonable(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [jsonable(v) for v in value]
    return value

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/modes.py | git_blob_sha=f325b7ec20ba0486bf2b65ebe5d57bc64a5f77c9 ================
"""Segment-supported propagation mode features, separate from event detection."""
import numpy as np
from .spectrum import direction, mode_velocity
from .amplitude import summarize_interval_strain


def contiguous_blocks(mask, minimum=3):
    edges = np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False, mask, False])).reshape(-1, 2)
    return [(int(a), int(b)) for a, b in edges if b-a >= minimum]


def analyze_modes(spacetime, *, window_s=20., step_s=1., regions=None, refine_velocity=False,
                  include_velocity=True):
    """Each spectrum uses an intact rectangular block; no concatenated gaps.

    20 s follows the prior literature-inspired mode protocol; 1 s step and
    three-column numerical minimum are project settings, not event criteria.
    All supported spatial blocks are kept. Coordination compares identical
    sections and window times on the two walls, grouped by spatial block.
    """
    if refine_velocity and not include_velocity:
        raise ValueError('Velocity refinement requires include_velocity=True')
    def measure(values, clock, positions):
        return (mode_velocity(values,clock,positions,refine=refine_velocity) if include_velocity
                else direction(values,clock,positions))
    a=np.asarray(spacetime['A'],float);v=np.asarray(spacetime['valid_mask'],bool)
    epsilon=np.asarray(spacetime['epsilon'],float) if 'epsilon' in spacetime else None
    t=np.asarray(spacetime['interval_center_s'],float);s=np.asarray(spacetime['s'],float)
    if a.shape != v.shape or a.shape != (len(t),2,len(s)) or len(t)<3:
        raise ValueError('Expected matching time x 2 walls x spatial section maps')
    if epsilon is not None and epsilon.shape != a.shape:
        raise ValueError('epsilon must match the time x wall x section map')
    dt=np.diff(t)
    if np.any(dt<=0) or not np.allclose(dt,np.median(dt),rtol=1e-5,atol=1e-9):
        raise ValueError('Mode FFT requires native uniform timestamps, without filling')
    if window_s<=0 or step_s<=0:raise ValueError('Positive window and step required')
    n=max(3,int(round(window_s/np.median(dt))));step=max(1,int(round(step_s/np.median(dt))))
    support=v&np.isfinite(a);rows=[];paired=[];missing=[]
    requested=regions or [(0,0,len(s),'anterior'),(1,0,len(s),'posterior')]
    requested_mask=np.zeros((2,len(s)),bool)
    for wall,begin,end,name in requested:
        if not 0<=wall<2 or not 0<=begin<end<=len(s):raise ValueError('Invalid spatial region')
        requested_mask[wall,begin:end]=True
    support &= requested_mask[None]
    for first in range(0,len(t)-n+1,step):
        last=first+n;complete=support[first:last].all(0)
        window_results={}
        def base(lo,hi):
            return dict(window_start_s=float(t[first]),window_end_s=float(t[last-1]),
                window_center_s=float((t[first]+t[last-1])/2),first_section=lo+1,last_section=hi,
                s_start=float(s[lo]),s_end=float(s[hi-1]),center_span=float(s[hi-1]-s[lo]))
        for wall,begin,end,name in requested:
            if not 0<=wall<2 or not 0<=begin<end<=len(s):raise ValueError('Invalid spatial region')
            blocks=contiguous_blocks(complete[wall,begin:end])
            if not blocks:missing.append(dict(region=name,window_start_s=float(t[first]),reason='no_contiguous_three_column_support'))
            for lo,hi in blocks:
                lo+=begin;hi+=begin
                result=measure(a[first:last,wall,lo:hi],t[first:last],s[lo:hi])
                if epsilon is not None:
                    amplitude=summarize_interval_strain(epsilon[first:last,wall,lo:hi],t[first:last])
                    result.update(interval_strain_raw_SD_mean=float(amplitude['raw_SD_mean']),
                        interval_strain_band_SD_mean=float(amplitude['band_SD_mean']),
                        amplitude_band_cpm=amplitude['band_cpm'],
                        amplitude_positive_frequency_bins=amplitude['positive_frequency_bins'])
                window_results[wall,lo,hi]=result
                rows.append(dict(**base(lo,hi),wall=wall,region=name,**result))
        # Compare the same original spatial block, not two unrelated local ROIs.
        for lo,hi in contiguous_blocks(complete.all(0)):
            pair_results=[]
            for wall in (0,1):
                key=(wall,lo,hi)
                if key not in window_results:
                    window_results[key]=measure(a[first:last,wall,lo:hi],t[first:last],s[lo:hi])
                pair_results.append(window_results[key])
            left,right=pair_results
            if left['ER'] is not None and right['ER'] is not None:
                paired.append(dict(**base(lo,hi),anterior_ER=left['ER'],posterior_ER=right['ER']))
    coordination=[]
    groups=sorted(set((r['first_section'],r['last_section']) for r in paired))
    for lo,hi in groups:
        group=[r for r in paired if (r['first_section'],r['last_section'])==(lo,hi)]
        # Split temporal gaps too; do not align separated fragments by row index.
        times=np.array([r['window_center_s'] for r in group]);boundaries=np.r_[0,np.flatnonzero(np.diff(times)>step*np.median(dt)*1.5)+1,len(group)]
        for begin,end in zip(boundaries[:-1],boundaries[1:]):
            part=group[begin:end];x=np.array([r['anterior_ER'] for r in part]);y=np.array([r['posterior_ER'] for r in part])
            cc=float(np.corrcoef(x,y)[0,1]) if len(x)>=3 and np.std(x)>1e-12 and np.std(y)>1e-12 else None
            coordination.append(dict(first_section=lo,last_section=hi,window_count=len(part),
                start_center_s=part[0]['window_center_s'],end_center_s=part[-1]['window_center_s'],
                zero_lag_ER_Pearson_CC=cc,ER_MSE=float(np.mean((x-y)**2)),
                status='mode_similarity_candidate',overlapping_windows=True))
    return dict(windows=rows,paired_ER_windows=paired,coordination=coordination,missing=missing,
        nominal_window_s=float(window_s),nominal_step_s=float(step_s),
        actual_window_sample_span_s=float((n-1)*np.median(dt)),
        actual_step_s=float(step*np.median(dt)),
        numerical_minimum_columns=3,refined_velocity=bool(refine_velocity),event_count=None,event_frequency_cpm=None)
