================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/patient.py | git_blob_sha=aa9103e93c7fdb24235d4725474c59f05d87fc08 ================
"""Fixed-region patient summaries, with explicit observation support."""
import numpy as np
from .amplitude import summarize_interval_strain
from .events import measure_event, pair_wall_events
from .modes import analyze_modes, contiguous_blocks


def _duration(mask, interval_start_s, interval_end_s):
    """Length of the selected measurement-interval union, in seconds.

    Endpoints are validated by summarize_patient. Only overlapping/touching
    intervals merge; positive gaps between supported intervals stay missing.
    """
    intervals = sorted(zip(interval_start_s[mask], interval_end_s[mask]))
    if not intervals:
        return 0.
    start, end = intervals[0]
    total = 0.
    for left, right in intervals[1:]:
        if left <= end:
            end = max(end, right)
        else:
            total += end-start
            start, end = left, right
    return float(total+end-start)


def summarize_patient(spacetime, *, s_range, time_range=None, review=None, refine_mode_speed=False,
                      include_mode_velocity=True):
    """Same preselected anatomical region for amplitude, modes and exposure.

    review=None means no event review, not zero waves. A review contains
    complete (bool), intervals [[start,end], ...], and events (see events.py).
    Frequency is emitted only for an exhaustive review of supported exposure.
    Separate fragments are never concatenated before filtering or FFT.
    """
    t = np.asarray(spacetime['interval_center_s'], float)
    s = np.asarray(spacetime['s'], float)
    a = np.asarray(spacetime['A'], float)
    eps = np.asarray(spacetime['epsilon'], float)
    valid = np.asarray(spacetime['valid_mask'], bool)
    if (t.ndim != 1 or len(t) < 3 or not np.isfinite(t).all() or
            np.any(np.diff(t) <= 0) or not np.allclose(np.diff(t), np.median(np.diff(t)), rtol=1e-5)):
        raise ValueError('Patient summary requires increasing uniform native times')
    if (s.ndim != 1 or not np.isfinite(s).all() or np.any(np.diff(s) <= 0) or
            a.shape != (len(t), 2, len(s)) or eps.shape != a.shape or valid.shape != a.shape):
        raise ValueError('Expected matching time x wall x section maps')
    dt = np.asarray(spacetime['interval_dt_s'], float)
    if dt.shape != t.shape or not np.isfinite(dt).all() or np.any(dt <= 0):
        raise ValueError('interval_dt_s must contain one finite positive duration per sample')
    if ('interval_start_s' in spacetime) != ('interval_end_s' in spacetime):
        raise ValueError('Supply both interval_start_s and interval_end_s')
    # Interval center and duration determine the exact endpoints for older
    # maps that archived only these two fields. No motion is interpolated.
    interval_start = np.asarray(spacetime.get('interval_start_s', t-dt/2), float)
    interval_end = np.asarray(spacetime.get('interval_end_s', t+dt/2), float)
    if (interval_start.shape != t.shape or interval_end.shape != t.shape or
            not np.isfinite(interval_start).all() or not np.isfinite(interval_end).all() or
            not np.allclose(interval_end-interval_start, dt, rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-9) or
            not np.allclose((interval_start+interval_end)/2, t, rtol=0, atol=1e-6)):
        raise ValueError('Interval endpoints must agree with the archived centers and durations')
    if time_range is not None:
        if (len(time_range) != 2 or not np.isfinite(time_range).all() or
                not interval_start.min() <= time_range[0] < time_range[1] <= interval_end.max()):
            raise ValueError('time_range must lie inside the acquisition interval bounds')
        select = (interval_start >= time_range[0]) & (interval_end <= time_range[1])
        if select.sum() < 3:
            raise ValueError('The selected time range needs at least three native samples')
        spacetime = dict(spacetime)
        for key in ('A', 'epsilon', 'valid_mask', 'interval_center_s', 'interval_dt_s',
                    'interval_start_s', 'interval_end_s'):
            if key in spacetime:
                spacetime[key] = np.asarray(spacetime[key])[select]
        t, a, eps, valid = t[select], a[select], eps[select], valid[select]
        interval_start, interval_end, dt = interval_start[select], interval_end[select], dt[select]
    if (len(s_range) != 2 or not np.isfinite(s_range).all() or
            not 0 <= s_range[0] < s_range[1] <= 1):
        raise ValueError('s_range must be ordered cervix-to-fundus coordinates inside 0..1')
    ids = np.flatnonzero((s >= s_range[0]) & (s <= s_range[1]))
    if len(ids) < 3:
        raise ValueError('The selected fixed region needs at least three sampled sections')
    good = valid & np.isfinite(a) & np.isfinite(eps)
    lo, hi = int(ids[0]), int(ids[-1]+1)
    walls = []
    # At least one retained FFT frequency is needed, not a demand for 120 s.
    minimum = max(3, int(np.ceil(60/5/np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    for wall in (0, 1):
        per_pair = []
        for section in ids:
            blocks = []
            for start, end in contiguous_blocks(good[:, wall, section], minimum):
                amplitude = summarize_interval_strain(eps[start:end, wall, section], t[start:end])
                if amplitude['positive_frequency_bins'] == 0:
                    continue
                blocks.append(dict(start_s=float(t[start]), end_s=float(t[end-1]),
                                   samples=end-start, band_SD=float(amplitude['band_SD_mean']),
                                   raw_SD=float(amplitude['raw_SD_mean']),
                                   frequency_resolution_cpm=amplitude['time_resolution_cpm'],
                                   positive_frequency_bins=amplitude['positive_frequency_bins']))
            samples = sum(row['samples'] for row in blocks)
            pooled = {kind: float(np.sqrt(sum(row['samples']*row[kind]**2 for row in blocks)/samples))
                      if samples else None for kind in ('band_SD', 'raw_SD')}
            per_pair.append(dict(section=int(section), s=float(s[section]),
                                 analyzed_samples=samples, blocks=blocks, **pooled))
        analyzed = [row for row in per_pair if row['band_SD'] is not None]
        covers_region = len(analyzed) == len(ids)
        available_amplitude = float(np.mean([row['band_SD'] for row in analyzed])) if analyzed else None
        common = good[:, wall, lo:hi].all(1)
        walls.append(dict(wall=wall, per_pair=per_pair,
                          radial_stretch_activity_amplitude=available_amplitude if covers_region else None,
                          partial_region_activity_amplitude_QC=available_amplitude if not covers_region else None,
                          amplitude_status=('complete_requested_region' if covers_region else
                                            'insufficient_section_support' if analyzed else 'no_analyzable_sections'),
                          raw_interval_strain_SD_QC=float(np.mean([row['raw_SD'] for row in analyzed])) if analyzed else None,
                          analyzed_sections=len(analyzed), requested_sections=len(ids),
                          amplitude_covers_all_requested_sections=covers_region,
                          valid_fraction=float(good[:, wall, lo:hi].mean()),
                          common_region_duration_s=_duration(common, interval_start, interval_end)))
    # Retain the exact region: a varying set of surviving columns would change
    # the patient's physiological measurement between windows.
    fixed = dict(spacetime, valid_mask=good.copy())
    complete = good[:, :, lo:hi].all(2)
    fixed['valid_mask'][:, :, lo:hi] &= complete[:, :, None]
    modes = analyze_modes(fixed, regions=[(0, lo, hi, 'anterior'), (1, lo, hi, 'posterior')],
                          refine_velocity=refine_mode_speed, include_velocity=include_mode_velocity)
    events, pairs = [], []
    counts = dict(status='not_reviewed', event_count=None, event_frequency_cpm=None)
    if review is not None:
        intervals = np.asarray(review['intervals'], float)
        if (intervals.ndim != 2 or intervals.shape[1] != 2 or not np.isfinite(intervals).all()
                or np.any(intervals[:, 1] <= intervals[:, 0]) or
                np.any(intervals[:, 0] < interval_start.min()) or
                np.any(intervals[:, 1] > interval_end.max())):
            raise ValueError('Reviewed intervals must lie inside the retained acquisition bounds')
        reviewed = np.zeros(len(t), bool)
        for start, end in intervals:
            reviewed |= (interval_start >= start) & (interval_end <= end)
        seen = set()
        for item in review['events']:
            key = (str(item['event_id']), item['wall'])
            if key in seen:
                raise ValueError('Duplicate event_id and wall; merge the annotations first')
            seen.add(key)
            row = measure_event(spacetime, item)
            inside_region = row['sections'][0] >= lo and row['sections'][-1] < hi
            inside_review = any(start <= row['start_s'] and row['end_s'] <= end for start, end in intervals)
            supported = (t >= row['start_s']) & (t <= row['end_s'])
            if not inside_region or not inside_review or not complete[supported, row['wall']].all():
                row['status'] = 'outside_fixed_observation_support'
            events.append(row)
        for event_id in {row['event_id'] for row in events}:
            rows = [row for row in events if row['event_id'] == event_id]
            if len(rows) == 2 and max(row['start_s'] for row in rows) > min(row['end_s'] for row in rows):
                raise ValueError('Shared event_id on two walls requires overlapping event time bounds')
        exposure = complete & reviewed[:, None]
        duration = _duration(exposure.any(1), interval_start, interval_end)
        accepted = [row for row in events if row['status'] in ('reviewed_propagation', 'reviewed_complex_activity')]
        ids_counted = {row['event_id'] for row in accepted}
        failed = [row for row in events if row['status'] not in
                  ('reviewed_propagation', 'reviewed_complex_activity', 'no_resolved_propagation')]
        exhaustive = bool(review['complete']) and not failed and duration > 0
        directions = {}
        for event_id in sorted(ids_counted):
            values = {row['direction'] for row in accepted if row['event_id'] == event_id}
            directions[event_id] = next(iter(values)) if len(values) == 1 else 'complex'
        speeds = {name: [] for name in ('C2F', 'F2C')}
        for event_id, name in directions.items():
            if name in speeds:
                values = [row['velocity_paths_per_s'] for row in accepted
                          if row['event_id'] == event_id and row['velocity_paths_per_s'] is not None]
                if values:
                    speeds[name].append(float(np.mean(values)))
        counts = dict(status='reviewed' if exhaustive else 'partial_review_or_support',
                      event_count=len(ids_counted) if exhaustive else None,
                      supported_reviewed_event_count=len(ids_counted),
                      event_frequency_cpm=60*len(ids_counted)/duration if exhaustive else None,
                      effective_duration_s=duration,
                      duration_rule='union_of_reviewed_fixed_region_support_on_either_wall',
                      median_event_velocity_paths_per_s={name: float(np.median(values)) if values else None
                                                        for name, values in speeds.items()},
                      velocity_pooling='mean_across_paired_walls_then_median_across_events_per_direction',
                      direction_proportions={name: sum(value == name for value in directions.values())/len(directions)
                                             if directions else None for name in ('C2F', 'F2C', 'complex')})
        pairs = pair_wall_events(accepted, s)
    return dict(definition_version='radial_wave_features_v2',
                requested_s_range=list(s_range), sampled_s_range=[float(s[lo]), float(s[hi-1])],
                analyzed_time_range_s=[float(t[0]), float(t[-1])],
                analyzed_interval_center_range_s=[float(t[0]), float(t[-1])],
                analyzed_acquisition_time_range_s=[float(interval_start.min()), float(interval_end.max())],
                requested_acquisition_time_range_s=list(time_range) if time_range is not None else None,
                time_range_rule='full_measurement_interval_containment',
                duration_definition='union_of_supported_measurement_intervals',
                sections=ids.tolist(), nominal_interval_s=float(spacetime.get('nominal_interval_s', np.median(dt))),
                actual_interval_s_range=[float(dt.min()), float(dt.max())],
                amplitude_units='dimensionless', amplitude_band_cpm=[.5, 5.],
                amplitude_pooling='sample_weighted_within_segment_variance_per_pair_then_spatial_mean',
                primary_amplitude_requires_all_requested_sections=True,
                amplitude_interpretation='interval_radial_stretch_activity_not_total_single_contraction_strain',
                walls=walls, modes=modes, events=events, event_summary=counts,
                paired_wall_events=pairs, event_velocity_units='full_cervix_fundus_paths_per_second')
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/patient.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/radial_events.py | git_blob_sha=2d4f18133d72b87dca6cb4e672a569b930f425a2 ================
"""Reviewable propagation evidence from radial A(s,t).

Keep short and unpaired structures. Pairing ranks propagation coherence rather
than event count; engineering diagnostics do not establish physiological truth.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import numpy as np
from .amplitude import band_limited_signal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RadialWaveConfig:
    # Fit reference is used jointly with order/phase evidence, not as a veto.
    min_fit_r2: float = .5
    synchronous_tolerance_s: float = .5


def _runs(mask):
    return np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False, mask, False])).reshape(-1, 2)


def _fit(s, t):
    slope, intercept = np.polyfit(s, t, 1)
    residual = t-(slope*s+intercept)
    total = float(np.sum((t-t.mean())**2))
    return dict(slope=float(slope), intercept=float(intercept),
                r2=float(1-np.sum(residual**2)/total) if total > 1e-20 else None,
                rmse_s=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(residual**2))))


def _arrival_evidence(s, arrival, dt):
    fit = _fit(s, arrival)
    differences = (arrival[None, :] - arrival[:, None])[np.triu_indices(len(s), 1)]
    resolved = abs(differences) > dt
    agreement = float(np.mean(differences[resolved] * fit['slope'] > 0)) if resolved.any() else .5
    extent = abs(fit['slope']) * float(np.ptp(s))
    # Continuous engineering ranking, not a calibrated probability or p-value.
    quality = (max(fit['r2'] or 0., 0.) * max(2*agreement-1, 0.) *
               extent/(extent + 2*fit['rmse_s'] + dt))
    return dict(**fit, order_agreement=agreement, fitted_time_extent_s=extent,
                coherence=float(quality))


def _describe_arrival(ids, arrival, s, dt, phase_fits, config):
    evidence = _arrival_evidence(s[ids], arrival, dt)
    warnings = []
    delta = np.diff(arrival)
    monotone = bool(np.all(delta >= -dt) or np.all(delta <= dt))
    weak_fit = (evidence['r2'] or 0.) < config.min_fit_r2
    phase_conflict = any(p['slope']*evidence['slope'] < 0 for p in phase_fits)
    if not monotone: warnings.append('nonmonotone_arrivals')
    if weak_fit: warnings.append('weak_arrival_fit')
    if phase_conflict: warnings.append('phase_direction_conflict')
    if any((p['r2'] or 0.) < config.min_fit_r2 for p in phase_fits):
        warnings.append('weak_phase_fit')
    if np.ptp(arrival) <= max(dt, config.synchronous_tolerance_s):
        kind = 'synchronous'
    elif not monotone and (weak_fit or phase_conflict):
        kind = 'complex'
    else:
        kind = 'C2F' if evidence['slope'] > 0 else 'F2C'
    return dict(classification=kind, sections=ids.tolist(),
                s_start=float(s[ids[0]]), s_end=float(s[ids[-1]]),
                spatial_span=float(s[ids[-1]]-s[ids[0]]),
                arrival_time_s=arrival.tolist(), arrival_fit_R2=evidence['r2'],
                arrival_fit_RMSE_s=evidence['rmse_s'],
                arrival_time_slope=evidence['slope'], arrival_evidence=evidence,
                velocity_paths_per_s=float(1/evidence['slope']) if kind in ('C2F','F2C') else None,
                phase_arrival_fits=phase_fits, quality_flags=warnings)


def _measure_pair(edge, ridges, filtered, t, s, config):
    first, second = [ridges[edge[name]] for name in ('first', 'second')]
    left = {x['column']: x for x in first['nodes']}
    right = {x['column']: x for x in second['nodes']}
    ids = np.asarray(edge['columns'])
    arrival = []
    for j in ids:
        k, k2 = left[j]['last'], right[j]['first']
        before, after = filtered[k, j], filtered[k2, j]
        arrival.append(t[k] - before/(after-before)*(t[k2]-t[k]))
    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
    phases = [_arrival_evidence(s[ids], np.array([p[j]['peak_time_s'] for j in ids]), dt)
              for p in (left, right)]
    event = _describe_arrival(ids, np.asarray(arrival), s, dt, phases, config)
    quality = event['arrival_evidence']['coherence']
    for phase in phases:
        quality *= phase['coherence']
    agreeing = sum(p['slope']*event['arrival_time_slope'] > 0 for p in phases)
    # Shared support contributes only in proportion to temporal coherence.
    edge['association_weight'] = len(ids)*quality*(1+agreeing)/3
    event.update(evidence_kind='paired_phase', arrival_marker='between_phase_zero_crossing',
                 association_weight=edge['association_weight'],
                 start_s=min(left[j]['start_s'] for j in ids),
                 end_s=max(right[j]['end_s'] for j in ids),
                 leading_polarity=first['sign'], phase_ridge_ids=[first['id'], second['id']],
                 first_phase_arrival_s=[left[j]['peak_time_s'] for j in ids],
                 second_phase_arrival_s=[right[j]['peak_time_s'] for j in ids],
                 peak_absolute_rate_per_phase=[max(left[j]['amplitude'] for j in ids),
                                               max(right[j]['amplitude'] for j in ids)])
    return event


def _lobes(y, valid, t, threshold):
    filtered = np.full_like(y, np.nan)
    columns = [[] for _ in range(y.shape[1])]
    minimum = max(3, int(np.ceil(12/np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    for col in range(y.shape[1]):
        for run_id, (begin, end) in enumerate(_runs(valid[:, col])):
            if end-begin < minimum:
                continue
            signal = band_limited_signal(y[begin:end, col], t[begin:end])
            filtered[begin:end, col] = signal
            edges = np.r_[0, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(signal >= 0))+1, len(signal)]
            for sequence, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:])):
                # A censored phase cannot establish a complete bipolar event.
                if a == 0 or b == len(signal):
                    continue
                peak = int(a+np.argmax(abs(signal[a:b])))
                amplitude = float(abs(signal[peak]))
                if amplitude <= max(float(threshold[col]), 1e-12):
                    continue
                columns[col].append(dict(column=col, run_id=run_id, sequence=sequence,
                    sign=1 if signal[peak] > 0 else -1, peak_index=int(begin+peak),
                    peak_time_s=float(t[begin+peak]), amplitude=amplitude,
                    first=int(begin+a), last=int(begin+b-1),
                    start_s=float(t[begin+a]), end_s=float(t[begin+b-1])))
    return filtered, columns


def _ridges(columns, t, s):
    nodes=[]
    for column in columns:
        for node in column:
            node['id']=len(nodes);nodes.append(node)
    parent=list(range(len(nodes)))

    def find(i):
        while parent[i] != i:
            parent[i]=parent[parent[i]];i=parent[i]
        return i

    for left, right in zip(columns[:-1], columns[1:]):
        choices=[]
        for a in left:
            for b in right:
                delay=abs(a['peak_time_s']-b['peak_time_s'])
                scale=.5*((a['end_s']-a['start_s'])+(b['end_s']-b['start_s']))
                overlap=min(a['end_s'],b['end_s'])-max(a['start_s'],b['start_s'])
                if a['sign']==b['sign'] and delay<=scale and overlap>=0:
                    choices.append((delay,a['id'],b['id']))
        used_left=set();used_right=set()
        for _, a, b in sorted(choices):
            if a in used_left or b in used_right:continue
            parent[find(a)]=find(b);used_left.add(a);used_right.add(b)
    groups={}
    for node in nodes:groups.setdefault(find(node['id']),[]).append(node)
    ridges=[]
    for group in groups.values():
        if len(group)<3:continue
        group=sorted(group,key=lambda node:node['column'])
        ids=np.array([node['column'] for node in group])
        arrival=np.array([node['peak_time_s'] for node in group])
        ridges.append(dict(id=len(ridges), sign=group[0]['sign'], nodes=group,
                           fit=_fit(s[ids],arrival), center_s=float(np.median(arrival))))
    return ridges


def _phase_edges(ridges):
    edges={}
    for i,a in enumerate(ridges):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ridges)):
            b=ridges[j]
            if a['sign']==b['sign']:continue
            left={node['column']:node for node in a['nodes']}
            right={node['column']:node for node in b['nodes']}
            common=sorted(set(left)&set(right))
            # Adjacent phases must belong to the same continuous track run in
            # each shared column. No phase pairing over a missing interval.
            common=[k for k in common if left[k]['run_id']==right[k]['run_id'] and
                    abs(left[k]['sequence']-right[k]['sequence'])==1]
            if len(common)<3 or np.any(np.diff(common)!=1):continue
            delta=np.array([right[k]['peak_time_s']-left[k]['peak_time_s'] for k in common])
            if not (np.all(delta>0) or np.all(delta<0)):continue
            early,late=(a,b) if delta.mean()>0 else (b,a)
            edges[i,j]=dict(first=early['id'], second=late['id'], columns=common,
                            score=(1,len(common),int(early['sign']<0)))
    return edges


def _pair_phases(ridges, edges):
    """Non-overlapping adjacent phase pairs, without counting both polarities.

    Maximize shared support weighted by propagation coherence. No reward for
    pair count or preferred polarity. Unselected phases remain review evidence.
    """
    neighbors={i:[] for i in range(len(ridges))}
    for i,j in edges:neighbors[i].append(j);neighbors[j].append(i)
    ambiguous=[i for i in neighbors if len(neighbors[i])>1]
    negative=[i for i in neighbors if ridges[i]['sign']<0 and neighbors[i]]
    positive=[i for i in neighbors if ridges[i]['sign']>0 and neighbors[i]]
    if not negative or not positive:return [],ambiguous
    n=len(negative);m=len(positive)
    # Dummy columns permit unmatched phases, including zero-coherence pairs.
    cost=np.zeros((n,m+n),float)
    cost[:,:m]=1
    for row,i in enumerate(negative):
        for col,j in enumerate(positive):
            edge=edges.get(tuple(sorted((i,j))))
            if edge is not None:
                cost[row,col]=-edge['association_weight']
    # Rectangular shortest-augmenting-path assignment (rows <= columns).
    u=np.zeros(n+1);v=np.zeros(m+n+1)
    owner=np.zeros(m+n+1,int);previous=np.zeros(m+n+1,int)
    for row in range(1,n+1):
        owner[0]=row;col=0;minimum=np.full(m+n+1,np.inf);used=np.zeros(m+n+1,bool)
        while True:
            used[col]=True;current_row=owner[col];delta=np.inf;next_col=0
            for j in range(1,m+n+1):
                if used[j]:continue
                reduced=cost[current_row-1,j-1]-u[current_row]-v[j]
                if reduced<minimum[j]:minimum[j]=reduced;previous[j]=col
                if minimum[j]<delta:delta=minimum[j];next_col=j
            u[owner[used]]+=delta;v[used]-=delta;minimum[~used]-=delta
            col=next_col
            if owner[col]==0:break
        while col:
            before=previous[col];owner[col]=owner[before];col=before
    chosen=[]
    for col in range(1,m+1):
        if owner[col] and cost[owner[col]-1,col-1]<0:
            chosen.append(tuple(sorted((negative[owner[col]-1],positive[col-1]))))
    return chosen,ambiguous


def detect_radial_events(signal, valid, time_s, space, *, noise_floor, config=None):
    """Find radial propagation evidence, with an explicit noise scale.

    noise_floor is an absolute A threshold (s^-1), scalar or one per section.
    It must come from a stated noise reference, not per-video robust-z scaling.
    Space stays on the original full cervix-to-fundus axis, without rescaling.
    """
    config=config or RadialWaveConfig()
    y=np.asarray(signal,float);m=np.asarray(valid,bool);t=np.asarray(time_s,float);s=np.asarray(space,float)
    if (y.shape!=m.shape or y.shape!=(len(t),len(s)) or len(t)<3 or len(s)<3 or
            not np.isfinite(t).all() or not np.isfinite(s).all() or np.any(np.diff(s)<=0) or
            np.any((s<0)|(s>1)) or np.any(np.diff(t)<=0) or
            not np.allclose(np.diff(t),np.median(np.diff(t)),rtol=1e-5,atol=1e-9)):
        raise ValueError('Expected matching native-uniform time x ordered normalized-space maps')
    threshold=np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(noise_floor,float),(len(s),))
    if not np.isfinite(threshold).all() or np.any(threshold<0):raise ValueError('Invalid absolute noise floor')
    if not 0<=config.min_fit_r2<=1 or config.synchronous_tolerance_s<0:
        raise ValueError('Invalid radial event support settings')
    filtered,columns=_lobes(y,m&np.isfinite(y),t,threshold)
    ridges=_ridges(columns,t,s);edges=_phase_edges(ridges)
    measured={key:_measure_pair(edge,ridges,filtered,t,s,config) for key,edge in edges.items()}
    pairs,ambiguous=_pair_phases(ridges,edges);events=[]
    for key in pairs:
        events.append(dict(measured[key],event_id='',reviewed=False,
                           phase_pairing_ambiguous=any(i in ambiguous for i in key)))
    used={i for key in pairs for i in key}
    for ridge in ridges:
        if ridge['id'] in used:continue
        nodes=ridge['nodes'];ids=np.array([n['column'] for n in nodes])
        arrival=np.array([n['peak_time_s'] for n in nodes])
        event=_describe_arrival(ids,arrival,s,float(np.median(np.diff(t))),[],config)
        event['quality_flags'].append('single_phase_event_identity_unresolved')
        events.append(dict(event,event_id='',reviewed=False,evidence_kind='single_phase',
            arrival_marker='phase_peak',start_s=min(n['start_s'] for n in nodes),
            end_s=max(n['end_s'] for n in nodes),leading_polarity=ridge['sign'],
            phase_ridge_ids=[ridge['id']],phase_pairing_ambiguous=ridge['id'] in ambiguous,
            first_phase_arrival_s=arrival.tolist(),second_phase_arrival_s=[],
            peak_absolute_rate_per_phase=[max(n['amplitude'] for n in nodes)]))
    for event in events:
        if event['phase_pairing_ambiguous']:
            event['quality_flags'].append('alternative_phase_pairing')
    events.sort(key=lambda event:(np.median(event['arrival_time_s']),event['s_start']))
    for i,event in enumerate(events,1):event['event_id']=f'candidate_{i:03d}'
    return dict(status='automatic_radial_wave_candidates_require_review',config=asdict(config),
        candidate_algorithm='radial_phase_evidence_v2',
        noise_floor_rate=threshold.copy(),filter_band_cpm=[.5,5.],filtered_A=filtered,
        events=events,candidate_count=len(events),
        traveling_candidate_count=sum(e['classification'] in ('C2F','F2C') for e in events),
        ambiguous_phase_ridge_ids=ambiguous,unpaired_ridge_count=len(ridges)-2*len(pairs),
        alternative_phase_pairs=[dict(measured[key],selected=key in pairs)
                                 for key in edges],
        event_count=None,event_frequency_cpm=None,
        phase_ridges=[dict(ridge_id=r['id'],sign=r['sign'],sections=[x['column'] for x in r['nodes']],
                           peak_time_s=[x['peak_time_s'] for x in r['nodes']],fit=r['fit']) for r in ridges])


def radial_candidates_from_maps(spacetime, *, noise_floor, s_range=(0.,1.), config=None):
    """Use current 2 s radial-map interface and retain original section IDs."""
    a=np.asarray(spacetime['A'],float);valid=np.asarray(spacetime['valid_mask'],bool)
    t=np.asarray(spacetime['interval_center_s'],float);s=np.asarray(spacetime['s'],float)
    if a.shape!=(len(t),2,len(s)) or valid.shape!=a.shape:
        raise ValueError('Expected time x two-wall x section radial maps')
    if len(s_range)!=2 or not 0<=s_range[0]<s_range[1]<=1:
        raise ValueError('Invalid anatomical s_range')
    ids=np.flatnonzero((s>=s_range[0])&(s<=s_range[1]))
    if len(ids)<3:raise ValueError('Selected region needs at least three native sections')
    filtered=np.full_like(a,np.nan);walls=[]
    for wall in (0,1):
        result=detect_radial_events(a[:,wall][:,ids],valid[:,wall][:,ids],t,s[ids],
                                   noise_floor=noise_floor,config=config)
        filtered[:,wall,ids]=result.pop('filtered_A')
        for event in result['events']:
            event['event_id']=f"wall{wall}_{event['event_id']}"
            event['wall']=wall;event['sections']=ids[event['sections']].tolist()
        for ridge in result['phase_ridges']:ridge['sections']=ids[ridge['sections']].tolist()
        for edge in result['alternative_phase_pairs']:
            edge['sections']=ids[edge['sections']].tolist()
            edge['wall']=wall
        walls.append(dict(wall=wall,**result))
    report=dict(status='automatic_radial_wave_candidates_require_review',
        measurement_source='A_rate',measurement_units='s^-1',source_section_indices=ids.tolist(),
        requested_s_range=list(s_range),sampled_s_range=[float(s[ids[0]]),float(s[ids[-1]])],
        center_time_range_s=[float(t[0]),float(t[-1])],
        nominal_interval_s=float(spacetime.get('nominal_interval_s',np.median(spacetime['interval_dt_s']))),
        walls=walls,event_count=None,event_frequency_cpm=None,
        note='Two-wall candidate counts are not summed; review event identity and arrivals before patient rates.')
    arrays=dict(filtered_A=filtered,candidate_valid_mask=valid&np.isfinite(filtered),
                interval_center_s=t.copy(),s=s.copy())
    return report,arrays

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/spectrum.py | git_blob_sha=2517fe0c5b74f09856c0747e3cf16a59535be049 ================
"""Directional spectra; raw FFT power is QC, not cross-grid physical energy."""
import numpy as np


def _spectrum(y, time_s, space, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """ER>0 means cervix-to-fundus; space must increase anatomically C to F.

    No temporal filling or spatial filling. This diagnostic operates on a
    complete contiguous block. The numerical energy floor is not a noise gate.
    """
    y, t, space = np.asarray(y, float), np.asarray(time_s, float), np.asarray(space, float)
    if y.shape != (len(t), len(space)) or len(t) < 3 or len(space) < 3 or not np.isfinite(y).all():
        raise ValueError("Expected a finite time x space map with at least 3 samples per axis")
    if not np.isfinite(t).all() or not np.isfinite(space).all() or np.any(np.diff(space) <= 0):
        raise ValueError("Coordinates must be finite and ordered")
    dt = np.diff(t)
    if np.any(dt <= 0) or not np.allclose(dt, np.median(dt), rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-9):
        raise ValueError("FFT requires uniform real timestamps; do not fill missing frames")
    if not 0 < band_cpm[0] < band_cpm[1]:
        raise ValueError("Invalid temporal frequency band")
    ft = np.fft.fftfreq(len(t), np.median(dt))
    g = np.diff(space)
    weights = np.r_[g[0], (g[:-1]+g[1:])/2, g[-1]]
    weights /= weights.mean()
    fx = np.fft.fftfreq(len(space), d=(space[-1]-space[0])/(len(space)-1))
    transformed = np.fft.fft(y-y.mean(0), axis=0)*weights
    spatial = np.exp(-2j*np.pi*(space-space[0])[:, None]*fx[None, :])
    power = np.abs(transformed @ spatial)**2
    band = (np.abs(ft) >= band_cpm[0]/60) & (np.abs(ft) <= band_cpm[1]/60)
    return y, t, space, ft, fx, power, band


def _direction_summary(y, t, space, ft, fx, power, band, band_cpm):
    dt = np.diff(t)
    ef = float(power[band[:, None] & (ft[:, None]*fx[None, :] < 0)].sum())
    eb = float(power[band[:, None] & (ft[:, None]*fx[None, :] > 0)].sum())
    total = float(power.sum())
    band_energy = float(power[band].sum())
    directional_energy = ef+eb
    er = (ef-eb)/(ef+eb) if ef+eb > max(1e-25, total*1e-12) else None
    return dict(ER=er, forward_energy=ef, backward_energy=eb,
                directional_energy=directional_energy,
                spectral_energy_normalization='unnormalized_fft_power',
                spectral_energy_role='QC_only',
                spectral_energy_cross_grid_comparable=False,
                directional_rms=float(np.sqrt(directional_energy)/(len(t)*len(space))),
                directional_band_energy_fraction=(directional_energy/band_energy if band_energy > 1e-25 else None),
                time_resolution_cpm=60/(len(t)*np.median(dt)),
                duration_s=float(t[-1]-t[0]), spatial_columns=len(space),
                band_cpm=list(band_cpm), event_detected=None)


def direction(y, time_s, space, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Unchanged ER convention: positive C2F; this does not detect events."""
    return _direction_summary(*_spectrum(y, time_s, space, band_cpm), band_cpm)


def mode_velocity(y, time_s, space, band_cpm=(.5, 5.), *, refine=False):
    """Directional spectral peak speed, not individual-wave arrival speed.

    v=-ft/fs for the Fourier convention used here. When space is the original
    full-wall normalized arc, units are full-wall paths per second. Both
    directions are retained, including standing/balanced activity. No new
    physiological activity threshold or smoothing. The coarse grid peak is
    archived when refine=True requests the continuous candidate. The default
    preserves the baseline pending real-video validation of the candidate.
    """
    arrays = _spectrum(y, time_s, space, band_cpm)
    y, t, space, ft, fx, power, band = arrays
    result = _direction_summary(*arrays, band_cpm)
    result['space_frequency_resolution'] = float(abs(fx[1]))
    result['positive_temporal_band_bins'] = int((band & (ft > 0)).sum())
    floor = max(1e-25, float(power.sum())*1e-12)
    for name, sign in [('C2F', -1), ('F2C', 1)]:
        mask = band[:, None] & (ft[:, None] > 0) & (ft[:, None]*fx[None, :]*sign > 0)
        energy = float(power[mask].sum())
        if not mask.any() or energy <= floor:
            result[name] = dict(status='no_resolved_directional_energy', velocity=None)
            continue
        peak = np.where(mask, power, -np.inf)
        i, j = np.unravel_index(np.argmax(peak), peak.shape)
        result[name] = dict(status='spectral_mode_candidate', velocity=float(-ft[i]/fx[j]),
            temporal_frequency_hz=float(ft[i]), spatial_frequency=float(fx[j]),
            peak_energy_fraction=float(power[i, j]/energy), directional_energy=energy,
            available_spectral_cells=int(mask.sum()),
            lowest_nonzero_spatial_peak=bool(np.isclose(abs(fx[j]),abs(fx[1]))),
            spatial_nyquist_peak=bool(len(fx)%2 == 0 and j == len(fx)//2))
    if refine:
        from .wavefit import fit_wave_modes
        fit = fit_wave_modes(y, t, space, band_cpm)
        result['velocity_fit'] = {key: value for key, value in fit.items() if key != 'components'}
        for name in ('C2F', 'F2C'):
            coarse = result[name]
            component = fit['components'][name]
            result[name] = dict(coarse, grid_peak=coarse.copy())
            result[name].update(component)
            result[name]['velocity_method'] = 'continuous_two_direction_fit'
    return result


def describe(measured, reference, valid, time_s, arc):
    """Matched-support error; direction uses the longest complete spatial run."""
    measured, reference, valid = np.asarray(measured), np.asarray(reference), np.asarray(valid, bool)
    valid = valid & np.isfinite(measured) & np.isfinite(reference)
    a, b = measured[valid], reference[valid]
    baseline = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(b*b))) if len(b) else None
    row = dict(valid_fraction=float(valid.mean()),
               rmse=float(np.sqrt(np.mean((a-b)**2))) if len(a) else None,
               reference_rms=baseline, measured_rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(a*a))) if len(a) else None)
    if baseline is not None and baseline > 1e-10:
        row.update(normalized_rmse=row['rmse']/baseline, rms_ratio=row['measured_rms']/baseline,
                   correlation=float(np.corrcoef(a, b)[0, 1]) if np.std(a) > 1e-20 and np.std(b) > 1e-20 else None)
    complete = valid.all(0)
    bounds = np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False, complete, False])).reshape(-1, 2)
    choices = [(int(u), int(v)) for u, v in bounds if v-u >= 3]
    if choices:
        u, v = max(choices, key=lambda p: arc[p[1]-1]-arc[p[0]])
        row.update(direction_columns=[u, v],
                   measured_ER=direction(measured[:, u:v], time_s, arc[u:v])['ER'],
                   reference_ER=direction(reference[:, u:v], time_s, arc[u:v])['ER'])
    return row
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/spectrum.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/tracking.py | git_blob_sha=1e6fd1ac3592b8eb855a547deafb53301e208a84 ================
"""Tracklets with last-reference re-localization and one-frame confirmation.

target_valid describes supported positions. step_valid additionally excludes
every re-anchor transition. Features must consume step_valid + segment_id.
"""
import numpy as np
import cv2
from ._lk import match, local_contrast

def track_segments(gray,initial,time_s,poses,*,recover=True,retry_frames=4,
                   window=21,max_fb_px=.75,contrast_sigma=5.,qc_downsample=1,qc_window=None,pose_epoch=None,pose_valid=None):
    t=np.asarray(time_s,float);ini=np.asarray(initial,np.float32);poses=np.asarray(poses,float)
    if t.ndim!=1 or len(t)<2 or not np.isfinite(t).all():raise ValueError('Expected at least two finite timestamps')
    if ini.ndim!=2 or ini.shape[1]!=2 or len(ini)==0 or not np.isfinite(ini).all():raise ValueError('Expected finite nonempty Nx2 seeds')
    if not np.isfinite(poses).all():raise ValueError('Poses must be finite')
    if poses.shape!=(len(t),3,3) or not np.allclose(poses[:,2],[0,0,1]) or np.any(np.abs(np.linalg.det(poses))<1e-12):raise ValueError('Expected invertible affine image-to-reference poses')
    if len(gray)!=len(t) or poses.shape!=(len(t),3,3) or np.any(np.diff(t)<=0):raise ValueError('Frame/time/pose mismatch')
    if gray[0].ndim!=2 or gray[0].dtype!=np.uint8:raise ValueError('Expected uint8 grayscale frames')
    epoch=np.zeros(len(t),int) if pose_epoch is None else np.asarray(pose_epoch)
    if epoch.shape!=t.shape:raise ValueError('pose_epoch must match timestamps')
    pose_valid=None if pose_valid is None else np.asarray(pose_valid,bool)
    if pose_valid is not None and pose_valid.shape!=t.shape:raise ValueError('pose_valid must match timestamps')
    if retry_frames<1:raise ValueError('retry_frames must be positive')
    if window<7 or window%2!=1 or max_fb_px<=0 or contrast_sigma<=0:raise ValueError('Invalid tracking scale')
    if not isinstance(qc_downsample,int) or qc_downsample<1:raise ValueError('QC downsample must be a positive integer')
    if qc_window is not None and (qc_window<3 or qc_window%2!=1):raise ValueError('QC window must be odd and at least 3')
    qc=gray if qc_downsample==1 else [cv2.resize(frame,None,fx=1/qc_downsample,fy=1/qc_downsample,interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA) for frame in gray]
    n,k=len(t),len(ini)
    p=np.full((n,k,2),np.nan,np.float32);v=np.zeros((n,k),bool);step=np.zeros((n,k),bool)
    segment=np.full((n,k),-1,int);reanchor=np.zeros((n,k),bool);attempt=np.zeros((n,k),bool)
    score=np.full((n,k),np.nan);fb=np.full_like(score,np.nan);reason=np.full((n,k),'waiting',dtype='U28')
    p[0]=ini;v[0]=True;segment[0]=0;reason[0]='seed'
    last_index=np.zeros(k,int);last_point=ini.copy();next_retry=np.zeros(k,int);sid=np.zeros(k,int)
    pending_index=np.full(k,-1,int);pending_point=np.full((k,2),np.nan,np.float32)
    enhanced=[local_contrast(gray[0],sigma=contrast_sigma)]
    for i in range(1,n):
        if gray[i].shape!=gray[0].shape or gray[i].dtype!=np.uint8:raise ValueError('Frame geometry or dtype changed')
        enhanced.append(local_contrast(gray[i],sigma=contrast_sigma))
        # A pending seed is committed only after a successful native-frame step.
        pending=np.flatnonzero(pending_index==i-1)
        if len(pending):
            q,g,s,f,r=match(enhanced[i-1],enhanced[i],qc[i-1],qc[i],pending_point[pending],window,max_fb_px=max_fb_px,qc_scale=qc_downsample,qc_window=qc_window)
            for j,col in enumerate(pending):
                score[i,col]=s[j];fb[i,col]=f[j]
                if g[j]:
                    seed=int(pending_index[col]);sid[col]+=1
                    p[seed,col]=pending_point[col];v[seed,col]=True;segment[seed,col]=sid[col];reanchor[seed,col]=True
                    reason[seed,col]='reanchor_confirmed';step[seed,col]=False
                    p[i,col]=q[j];v[i,col]=True;segment[i,col]=sid[col];step[i,col]=True;reason[i,col]='accepted_after_reanchor'
                    last_index[col]=i;last_point[col]=q[j]
                else:reason[i,col]='confirmation_failed'
                pending_index[col]=-1
        # Existing tracks keep the exact original localization/QC behavior.
        active=np.flatnonzero(v[i-1]&~reanchor[i-1])
        # Confirmed seeds were handled above, so do not process them twice.
        active=np.setdiff1d(active,pending,assume_unique=True)
        if len(active):
            q,g,s,f,r=match(enhanced[i-1],enhanced[i],qc[i-1],qc[i],p[i-1,active],window,max_fb_px=max_fb_px,qc_scale=qc_downsample,qc_window=qc_window)
            score[i,active]=s;fb[i,active]=f;reason[i,active]=r
            keep=active[g];p[i,keep]=q[g];v[i,keep]=True;segment[i,keep]=sid[keep];step[i,keep]=True
            last_index[keep]=i;last_point[keep]=q[g]
            next_retry[active[~g]]=i+retry_frames
        if recover:
            waiting=np.flatnonzero(~v[i]&(pending_index<0)&(i>=next_retry))
            for ref in np.unique(last_index[waiting]):
                cols=waiting[last_index[waiting]==ref]
                m=np.linalg.inv(poses[i])@poses[int(ref)]
                if not np.isfinite(m).all():continue
                guess=last_point[cols]@m[:2,:2].T+m[:2,2]
                q,g,s,f,r=match(enhanced[int(ref)],enhanced[i],qc[int(ref)],qc[i],last_point[cols],window,guess,max_fb_px=max_fb_px,qc_scale=qc_downsample,qc_window=qc_window)
                attempt[i,cols]=True;next_retry[cols]=i+retry_frames
                for j,col in enumerate(cols):
                    if g[j]:
                        pending_index[col]=i;pending_point[col]=q[j]
                        reason[i,col]='awaiting_confirmation';score[i,col]=s[j];fb[i,col]=f[j]
    corrected=np.einsum('tni,tji->tnj',p.astype(float),poses[:,:2,:2])+poses[:,None,:2,2]
    result=dict(positions=p,corrected=corrected,target_valid=v,step_valid=step,segment_id=segment,reanchor=reanchor,
        recovery_attempted=attempt,time_s=t,frame_indices=np.arange(n),initial_points=ini,poses=poses,
        target_ncc=score,target_fb=fb,reject_reason=reason,pose_epoch=epoch)
    if pose_valid is not None:result['pose_valid']=pose_valid.copy()
    return result

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/analysis/mse_pregnancy_association/code/measurement/wavefit.py | git_blob_sha=60888d1088ce966e78fe32d6782a85fe7648312b ================
"""Continuous travelling-plane fit for mode speed, without event counting.

Two counterpropagating components are fitted jointly. Per-position offsets and
linear trends are nuisance terms, projected out of both data and model. This
refines the speed measurement; the original directional-energy ER is separate.
"""
import numpy as np


def _minimize(objective, start, *, iterations=240):
    """Bounded Nelder-Mead in unit coordinates; small 2/4-parameter problem."""
    start = np.asarray(start, float)
    simplex = np.tile(start, (len(start)+1, 1))
    for j in range(len(start)):
        simplex[j+1, j] += .06 if start[j] <= .94 else -.06
    values = np.array([objective(p) for p in simplex])
    for _ in range(iterations):
        order = np.argsort(values)
        simplex, values = simplex[order], values[order]
        if np.max(np.abs(simplex[1:]-simplex[0])) < 1e-6:
            break
        center = simplex[:-1].mean(0)
        reflected = np.clip(2*center-simplex[-1], 0, 1)
        reflected_value = objective(reflected)
        if reflected_value < values[0]:
            expanded = np.clip(center+2*(reflected-center), 0, 1)
            expanded_value = objective(expanded)
            simplex[-1], values[-1] = ((expanded, expanded_value)
                if expanded_value < reflected_value else (reflected, reflected_value))
        elif reflected_value < values[-2]:
            simplex[-1], values[-1] = reflected, reflected_value
        else:
            outside = reflected_value < values[-1]
            contracted = np.clip(center+.5*((reflected if outside else simplex[-1])-center), 0, 1)
            contracted_value = objective(contracted)
            if contracted_value < (reflected_value if outside else values[-1]):
                simplex[-1], values[-1] = contracted, contracted_value
            else:
                simplex[1:] = (simplex[1:]+simplex[0])/2
                values[1:] = [objective(p) for p in simplex[1:]]
    best = int(np.argmin(values))
    converged = bool(np.max(np.abs(simplex-simplex[best])) < 1e-6)
    return simplex[best], float(values[best]), converged


def fit_wave_modes(y, time_s, space, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Measure signed mode speeds using actual coordinates, not FFT grid bins.

    Inputs are already validated complete rectangular blocks by spectrum.py.
    Fit quality and parameter boundaries are diagnostics, not biological gates.
    A spatially uniform component has no resolved propagation speed. The only
    energy cutoff is numerical; this routine never declares an event present.
    """
    y, time, space = np.asarray(y, float), np.asarray(time_s, float), np.asarray(space, float)
    t = time-time.mean()
    x = space-space.mean()
    span = float(np.ptp(space))
    trend = t/np.linalg.norm(t)
    gaps = np.diff(space)
    weights = np.r_[gaps[0], (gaps[:-1]+gaps[1:])/2, gaps[-1]]
    weights = np.sqrt(weights/weights.mean())

    def project(values):
        values = values-values.mean(0)
        return (values-trend[:, None]*(trend@values)[None, :])*weights

    data = project(y).ravel()
    energy = float(data@data)
    floor = max(1e-25, float(np.sum((y-y.mean(0))**2))*1e-12)
    if energy <= floor:
        return dict(status='no_residual_motion_after_linear_trend', residual_fraction=None,
                    components={name: dict(velocity=None, temporal_frequency_hz=None,
                                           spatial_frequency=None, status='no_resolved_motion')
                                for name in ('C2F', 'F2C')})
    low, high = np.asarray(band_cpm)/60
    nyquist = .5/np.mean(gaps)

    def parameters(unit, sign):
        return low+unit[0]*(high-low), sign*unit[1]*nyquist

    def basis(unit, sign):
        frequency, wavenumber = parameters(unit, sign)
        phase = 2*np.pi*(frequency*t[:, None]+wavenumber*x[None, :])
        return np.column_stack((project(np.cos(phase)).ravel(), project(np.sin(phase)).ravel()))

    def solve(unit, signs, details=False):
        matrix = np.column_stack([basis(unit[2*j:2*j+2], sign) for j, sign in enumerate(signs)])
        coefficients = np.linalg.lstsq(matrix, data, rcond=np.sqrt(np.finfo(float).eps))[0]
        residual = data-matrix@coefficients
        loss = float(residual@residual/energy)
        return (loss, matrix, coefficients) if details else loss

    starts = []
    for sign in (-1, 1):
        objective = lambda unit: solve(unit, [sign])
        candidates = [(objective([f, k]), [f, k])
                      for f in np.linspace(0, 1, 7)
                      for k in np.linspace(0, 1, 2*len(space)+1)]
        initial = min(candidates, key=lambda item: item[0])[1]
        fitted, _, _ = _minimize(objective, initial, iterations=160)
        starts.extend(fitted)
    fitted, _, converged = _minimize(lambda unit: solve(unit, [-1, 1]), starts)
    loss, matrix, coefficients = solve(fitted, [-1, 1], details=True)
    singular = np.linalg.svd(matrix, compute_uv=False)
    separable = bool(singular[-1] > singular[0]*np.sqrt(np.finfo(float).eps))
    components = {}
    for j, (name, sign) in enumerate([('C2F', -1), ('F2C', 1)]):
        unit = fitted[2*j:2*j+2]
        frequency, wavenumber = parameters(unit, sign)
        component = matrix[:, 2*j:2*j+2]@coefficients[2*j:2*j+2]
        fraction = float(component@component/energy)
        uniform = abs(wavenumber)*span <= 1e-5
        present = fraction > 1e-10
        components[name] = dict(
            velocity=float(-frequency/wavenumber) if present and not uniform and separable else None,
            temporal_frequency_hz=float(frequency), spatial_frequency=float(wavenumber),
            component_energy_fraction=fraction if separable else None,
            temporal_boundary=bool(unit[0] < 1e-4 or unit[0] > 1-1e-4),
            spatial_boundary=bool(unit[1] < 1e-4 or unit[1] > 1-1e-4),
            status=('unresolved_counterpropagating_components' if not separable else
                    'no_resolved_component' if not present else
                    'spatially_uniform_component' if uniform else 'continuous_mode_candidate'))
    return dict(status='continuous_two_direction_fit', residual_fraction=loss,
                optimizer_converged=converged,
                component_condition_number=float(singular[0]/singular[-1]) if singular[-1] > 0 else None,
                nuisance='per_position_offset_and_linear_trend', components=components)

"""Step 2 infrastructure only; no production replacement or selected thresholds."""

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/experiments/step2_salience_sandbox/upstream.py | git_blob_sha=b553ce63f79aa9e35faa4cb27fcf60585409cf6b ================
"""One extraction per input, shared by every salience branch."""
import hashlib
import json
import numpy as np

from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.activity_strength import filtered_runs
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.partial_phase_events import detect_partial
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.candidate_split_verifier import prepare_parent, decide_split
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.global_event_ownership import reconcile
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code.patient_activity_aggregation import observation_union


def stable_hash(value):
    def encode(x):
        if isinstance(x, np.ndarray):
            return dict(shape=x.shape, dtype=str(x.dtype), sha256=hashlib.sha256(x.tobytes()).hexdigest())
        if isinstance(x, dict):
            return {str(k):encode(v) for k,v in sorted(x.items(),key=lambda item:str(item[0]))}
        if isinstance(x, (list,tuple)):
            return [encode(v) for v in x]
        if isinstance(x, np.generic):
            return x.item()
        return x
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(encode(value),sort_keys=True,allow_nan=True).encode()).hexdigest()


def pool_hash(shared):
    return stable_hash({k:shared[k] for k in ('A','valid','filtered','t','s','span','dt','noise_floor','walls')})


def extract_shared_upstream(maps, noise_floor):
    a = np.asarray(maps['A'],float).copy()
    valid = np.asarray(maps['valid_mask'],bool) & np.isfinite(a)
    t = np.asarray(maps['interval_center_s'],float).copy()
    s = np.asarray(maps['s'],float).copy()
    if a.shape != (len(t),2,len(s)) or valid.shape != a.shape:
        raise ValueError('Expected native time x wall x section arrays')
    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
    span = float(np.median(maps['interval_dt_s']))
    filtered = filtered_runs(a,valid,t)
    finite = np.isfinite(filtered)
    observable = bool((finite[:,:,:-2] & finite[:,:,1:-1] & finite[:,:,2:]).any()) if len(s)>=3 else False
    exposure = observation_union(dict(maps,valid_mask=valid),min_run_s=12.)
    if observable != bool(exposure['seconds']>0):
        raise RuntimeError('Detector support and observation support diverged')
    walls=[]
    if observable:
        for wall in (0,1):
            report=detect_partial(a[:,wall],valid[:,wall],t,s,noise_floor=noise_floor)
            report.pop('filtered_A')
            splits=[decide_split(prepare_parent(a[:,wall],valid[:,wall],t,s,parent,
                         noise_floor=noise_floor,measurement_span_s=span),minimum_adjacent_support=3)
                    for parent in report['events']]
            ownership=reconcile(report,splits,t,valid[:,wall],measurement_span_s=span)
            walls.append(dict(wall=wall,parent_report=report,split_proposals=splits,reconciliation=ownership))
    shared=dict(A=a,valid=valid,filtered=filtered,t=t,s=s,span=span,dt=dt,
                noise_floor=float(noise_floor),walls=walls,observable=observable,exposure=exposure)
    for x in (a,valid,filtered,t,s):
        x.setflags(write=False)
    shared['pool_hash']=pool_hash(shared)
    return shared

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/experiments/step2_salience_sandbox/mechanism_2x2/mechanisms.py | git_blob_sha=0035adc24aef465dc24be4a8676869c840239259 ================
"""Exactly one amplitude rule and one Butterworth filter; scoped experiment hooks."""
from contextlib import contextmanager,ExitStack
from unittest.mock import patch
import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import butter,sosfiltfilt
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code import activity_strength as strength
from analysis.mse_pregnancy_association.code import partial_phase_events as partial
from ..upstream import stable_hash

MODELS=('M0','M1','M2','M3')
CORE=partial.baseline
ORIGINAL_PAIR=CORE._pair_phases
ORIGINAL_DETECT=CORE.detect_radial_events
ORIGINAL_OBSERVED=partial.observed_lobes
ORIGINAL_FFT=CORE.band_limited_signal
OLD_STRENGTH_FILTER=strength.band_limited_signal

@contextmanager
def filter_only(model):
    with ExitStack() as stack:
        if model in ('M2','M3'):
            stack.enter_context(patch.object(CORE,'band_limited_signal',butter_run))
            stack.enter_context(patch.object(strength,'band_limited_signal',butter_run))
        yield

def butter_run(values,t,band_cpm=(.5,5.)):
    assert tuple(band_cpm)==(.5,5.),'Only the one prelocked band is allowed'
    fs=1/float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
    sos=butter(2,[.5/60,5/60],btype='bandpass',fs=fs,output='sos')
    return sosfiltfilt(sos,np.asarray(values,float),axis=0)

def component_factors(ridges,edges,filtered):
    """Normalize only within the pre-weight candidate-edge connected component."""
    adjacency={i:set() for edge in edges for i in edge}
    for a,b in edges:adjacency[a].add(b);adjacency[b].add(a)
    components=[];owner={}
    for first in sorted(adjacency):
        if first in owner:continue
        members=set();todo=[first]
        while todo:
            n=todo.pop()
            if n in members:continue
            members.add(n);todo.extend(adjacency[n]-members)
        index=len(components);components.append(sorted(members))
        for n in members:owner[n]=index
    amplitudes={};references={}
    for index,members in enumerate(components):
        references[index]={}
        for rid in members:
            for node in ridges[rid]['nodes']:
                c=node['column'];samples=filtered[node['first']:node['last']+1,c]
                # This is a production lobe inside an already-valid run. An
                # inconsistent/missing amplitude is an implementation failure,
                # never a new validity exclusion or silently dropped section.
                assert len(samples)>0 and np.isfinite(samples).all()
                value=float(np.max(abs(samples)))
                assert value==node['amplitude']
                amplitudes[rid,c]=value
                references[index][c]=max(references[index].get(c,0.),value)
    result={}
    for key,edge in edges.items():
        a,b=key;index=owner[a];assert owner[b]==index
        ids=edge['columns'];ai=np.array([amplitudes[a,c] for c in ids]);aj=np.array([amplitudes[b,c] for c in ids])
        reference=np.array([references[index][c] for c in ids])
        joint=np.zeros(len(ids));positive=reference>0
        joint[positive]=np.minimum(ai[positive]/reference[positive],aj[positive]/reference[positive])
        factor=float(np.median(joint));assert 0<=factor<=1
        result[key]=dict(component_id=index,component_ridge_ids=components[index],sections=list(ids),
            phase_i_amplitude=ai.tolist(),phase_j_amplitude=aj.tolist(),R=reference.tolist(),section_joint_factor=joint.tolist(),G=factor,
            phase_i_amplitude_summary=dict(min=float(ai.min()),median=float(np.median(ai)),max=float(ai.max())),
            phase_j_amplitude_summary=dict(min=float(aj.min()),median=float(np.median(aj)),max=float(aj.max())))
    return result

def weighted_pair(ridges,edges,filtered,apply_factor,force_one=False):
    factors=component_factors(ridges,edges,filtered);weighted={};audit=[]
    for key,edge in edges.items():
        factor=1. if force_one else factors[key]['G']
        old=edge['association_weight'];new=old*factor
        used=new if apply_factor else old
        weighted[key]=dict(edge,association_weight=used)
        audit.append(dict(ridge_key=list(key),phase_ridge_ids=[edge['first'],edge['second']],W_old=old,
                          **factors[key],W_new=new,W_used=used))
    selected,ambiguous=ORIGINAL_PAIR(ridges,weighted)
    for row in audit:row['selected']=tuple(row['ridge_key']) in selected
    return selected,ambiguous,audit

@contextmanager
def intervention(model,logs=None,force_one=False):
    """Only global FFT detector/scoring filter and global pairing inputs change.

    split.prepare_parent also calls _pair_phases locally. Those calls bypass
    the experimental weight entirely. Its original _measure_pair and
    measurement_span_mean remain untouched, including child ranking.
    OOB MAD imports its separate original FFT function and stays unchanged.
    """
    assert model in MODELS
    logs=[] if logs is None else logs
    state={}
    def detector(*args,**kwargs):
        assert not state.get('global_detector_active',False)
        state['global_detector_active']=True
        try:return ORIGINAL_DETECT(*args,**kwargs)
        finally:state['global_detector_active']=False
    def observed(y,valid,t,floor):
        filtered,columns,unresolved=ORIGINAL_OBSERVED(y,valid,t,floor)
        state.update(filtered=filtered,t=t,columns=columns)
        return filtered,columns,unresolved
    def pairing(ridges,edges):
        if not state.get('global_detector_active',False):return ORIGINAL_PAIR(ridges,edges)
        assert 'filtered' in state
        selected,ambiguous,audit=weighted_pair(ridges,edges,state['filtered'],model in ('M1','M3'),force_one)
        logs.append(dict(wall=len(logs),ridges=ridges,edges=audit,selected=[list(x) for x in selected],
                         raw_edge_signature=stable_hash(edges),filtered_signature=stable_hash(state['filtered'])))
        return selected,ambiguous
    with ExitStack() as stack:
        if model in ('M2','M3'):
            stack.enter_context(patch.object(CORE,'band_limited_signal',butter_run))
            stack.enter_context(patch.object(strength,'band_limited_signal',butter_run))
        stack.enter_context(patch.object(partial,'observed_lobes',observed))
        stack.enter_context(patch.object(CORE,'detect_radial_events',detector))
        stack.enter_context(patch.object(CORE,'_pair_phases',pairing))
        yield logs
    assert CORE._pair_phases is ORIGINAL_PAIR
    assert CORE.band_limited_signal is ORIGINAL_FFT
    assert strength.band_limited_signal is OLD_STRENGTH_FILTER

def run_inventory(maps,execute=False):
    t=maps['interval_center_s'];A=maps['A'];valid=maps['valid_mask']&np.isfinite(A)
    minimum=max(3,int(np.ceil(12/np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    rows=[]
    for wall in (0,1):
        for c in range(A.shape[2]):
            for ordinal,(a,b) in enumerate(np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False,valid[:,wall,c],False])).reshape(-1,2)):
                if b-a<minimum:continue
                row=dict(wall=wall,section=c,run_id=ordinal,first=int(a),last_exclusive=int(b),n_samples=int(b-a),
                    center_span_s=float(t[b-1]-t[a]),support_seconds=float(maps['interval_end_s'][b-1]-maps['interval_start_s'][a]),
                    fs=1/float(np.median(np.diff(t[a:b]))),M2_filter_unsupported=False)
                if execute:
                    try:
                        values=butter_run(A[a:b,wall,c],t[a:b])
                    except ValueError as error:
                        row.update(M2_filter_unsupported=True,reason=str(error),exception_type=type(error).__name__)
                    else:
                        assert np.isfinite(values).all(),'Butterworth produced nonfinite output'
                rows.append(row)
    return rows

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/uterine_wave/amplitude.py | git_blob_sha=458e7da3f63af67a0eaba34d18402f72adb764fc ================
"""Amplitude-related summaries for complete radial-strain time blocks."""
import numpy as np


def band_limited_signal(values, time_s, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Return a zero-phase FFT band pass of one complete time block.

    ``values`` may have any trailing spatial dimensions.  The result has those
    trailing dimensions.  This is an interval-strain variability measure, not
    a single-event peak amplitude.  Missing samples are not filled.
    """
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    time_s = np.asarray(time_s, float)
    if values.ndim < 1 or values.shape[0] != len(time_s) or len(time_s) < 3:
        raise ValueError('Expected time-first values with at least three samples')
    if not np.isfinite(values).all() or not np.isfinite(time_s).all():
        raise ValueError('Band-limited SD requires a complete finite block')
    dt = np.diff(time_s)
    if np.any(dt <= 0) or not np.allclose(dt, np.median(dt), rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-9):
        raise ValueError('FFT amplitude requires uniform real timestamps')
    if not 0 < band_cpm[0] < band_cpm[1]:
        raise ValueError('Invalid temporal frequency band')
    centered = values - values.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    spectrum = np.fft.rfft(centered, axis=0)
    frequencies = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(time_s), np.median(dt))
    keep = (frequencies >= band_cpm[0]/60) & (frequencies <= band_cpm[1]/60)
    spectrum[~keep] = 0
    return np.fft.irfft(spectrum, n=len(time_s), axis=0)


def band_limited_sd(values, time_s, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Return per-column SD after band limiting a complete time block."""
    return np.std(band_limited_signal(values, time_s, band_cpm), axis=0)


def summarize_interval_strain(values, time_s, band_cpm=(.5, 5.)):
    """Raw and physiological-band SD for one complete interval-strain block."""
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    band_sd = band_limited_sd(values, time_s, band_cpm)
    raw_sd = np.std(values, axis=0)
    resolution = 60/(len(time_s)*np.median(np.diff(np.asarray(time_s, float))))
    bins = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(time_s), np.median(np.diff(time_s)))
    positive_bins = int(((bins >= band_cpm[0]/60) & (bins <= band_cpm[1]/60)).sum())
    return dict(
        definition='band_limited_interval_radial_strain_SD',
        interpretation='radial_stretch_activity_amplitude',
        units='dimensionless',
        depends_on_measurement_interval_and_motion_rate=True,
        raw_SD_per_pair=raw_sd,
        raw_SD_mean=float(raw_sd.mean()),
        band_SD_per_pair=band_sd,
        band_SD_mean=float(band_sd.mean()),
        band_cpm=list(band_cpm),
        time_resolution_cpm=float(resolution),
        positive_frequency_bins=positive_bins,
        duration_s=float(time_s[-1]-time_s[0]),
    )
