================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/uterine_wave/radial_events.py | git_blob_sha=2d4f18133d72b87dca6cb4e672a569b930f425a2 ================
"""Reviewable propagation evidence from radial A(s,t).

Keep short and unpaired structures. Pairing ranks propagation coherence rather
than event count; engineering diagnostics do not establish physiological truth.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import numpy as np
from .amplitude import band_limited_signal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RadialWaveConfig:
    # Fit reference is used jointly with order/phase evidence, not as a veto.
    min_fit_r2: float = .5
    synchronous_tolerance_s: float = .5


def _runs(mask):
    return np.flatnonzero(np.diff(np.r_[False, mask, False])).reshape(-1, 2)


def _fit(s, t):
    slope, intercept = np.polyfit(s, t, 1)
    residual = t-(slope*s+intercept)
    total = float(np.sum((t-t.mean())**2))
    return dict(slope=float(slope), intercept=float(intercept),
                r2=float(1-np.sum(residual**2)/total) if total > 1e-20 else None,
                rmse_s=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(residual**2))))


def _arrival_evidence(s, arrival, dt):
    fit = _fit(s, arrival)
    differences = (arrival[None, :] - arrival[:, None])[np.triu_indices(len(s), 1)]
    resolved = abs(differences) > dt
    agreement = float(np.mean(differences[resolved] * fit['slope'] > 0)) if resolved.any() else .5
    extent = abs(fit['slope']) * float(np.ptp(s))
    # Continuous engineering ranking, not a calibrated probability or p-value.
    quality = (max(fit['r2'] or 0., 0.) * max(2*agreement-1, 0.) *
               extent/(extent + 2*fit['rmse_s'] + dt))
    return dict(**fit, order_agreement=agreement, fitted_time_extent_s=extent,
                coherence=float(quality))


def _describe_arrival(ids, arrival, s, dt, phase_fits, config):
    evidence = _arrival_evidence(s[ids], arrival, dt)
    warnings = []
    delta = np.diff(arrival)
    monotone = bool(np.all(delta >= -dt) or np.all(delta <= dt))
    weak_fit = (evidence['r2'] or 0.) < config.min_fit_r2
    phase_conflict = any(p['slope']*evidence['slope'] < 0 for p in phase_fits)
    if not monotone: warnings.append('nonmonotone_arrivals')
    if weak_fit: warnings.append('weak_arrival_fit')
    if phase_conflict: warnings.append('phase_direction_conflict')
    if any((p['r2'] or 0.) < config.min_fit_r2 for p in phase_fits):
        warnings.append('weak_phase_fit')
    if np.ptp(arrival) <= max(dt, config.synchronous_tolerance_s):
        kind = 'synchronous'
    elif not monotone and (weak_fit or phase_conflict):
        kind = 'complex'
    else:
        kind = 'C2F' if evidence['slope'] > 0 else 'F2C'
    return dict(classification=kind, sections=ids.tolist(),
                s_start=float(s[ids[0]]), s_end=float(s[ids[-1]]),
                spatial_span=float(s[ids[-1]]-s[ids[0]]),
                arrival_time_s=arrival.tolist(), arrival_fit_R2=evidence['r2'],
                arrival_fit_RMSE_s=evidence['rmse_s'],
                arrival_time_slope=evidence['slope'], arrival_evidence=evidence,
                velocity_paths_per_s=float(1/evidence['slope']) if kind in ('C2F','F2C') else None,
                phase_arrival_fits=phase_fits, quality_flags=warnings)


def _measure_pair(edge, ridges, filtered, t, s, config):
    first, second = [ridges[edge[name]] for name in ('first', 'second')]
    left = {x['column']: x for x in first['nodes']}
    right = {x['column']: x for x in second['nodes']}
    ids = np.asarray(edge['columns'])
    arrival = []
    for j in ids:
        k, k2 = left[j]['last'], right[j]['first']
        before, after = filtered[k, j], filtered[k2, j]
        arrival.append(t[k] - before/(after-before)*(t[k2]-t[k]))
    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(t)))
    phases = [_arrival_evidence(s[ids], np.array([p[j]['peak_time_s'] for j in ids]), dt)
              for p in (left, right)]
    event = _describe_arrival(ids, np.asarray(arrival), s, dt, phases, config)
    quality = event['arrival_evidence']['coherence']
    for phase in phases:
        quality *= phase['coherence']
    agreeing = sum(p['slope']*event['arrival_time_slope'] > 0 for p in phases)
    # Shared support contributes only in proportion to temporal coherence.
    edge['association_weight'] = len(ids)*quality*(1+agreeing)/3
    event.update(evidence_kind='paired_phase', arrival_marker='between_phase_zero_crossing',
                 association_weight=edge['association_weight'],
                 start_s=min(left[j]['start_s'] for j in ids),
                 end_s=max(right[j]['end_s'] for j in ids),
                 leading_polarity=first['sign'], phase_ridge_ids=[first['id'], second['id']],
                 first_phase_arrival_s=[left[j]['peak_time_s'] for j in ids],
                 second_phase_arrival_s=[right[j]['peak_time_s'] for j in ids],
                 peak_absolute_rate_per_phase=[max(left[j]['amplitude'] for j in ids),
                                               max(right[j]['amplitude'] for j in ids)])
    return event


def _lobes(y, valid, t, threshold):
    filtered = np.full_like(y, np.nan)
    columns = [[] for _ in range(y.shape[1])]
    minimum = max(3, int(np.ceil(12/np.median(np.diff(t)))))
    for col in range(y.shape[1]):
        for run_id, (begin, end) in enumerate(_runs(valid[:, col])):
            if end-begin < minimum:
                continue
            signal = band_limited_signal(y[begin:end, col], t[begin:end])
            filtered[begin:end, col] = signal
            edges = np.r_[0, np.flatnonzero(np.diff(signal >= 0))+1, len(signal)]
            for sequence, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:])):
                # A censored phase cannot establish a complete bipolar event.
                if a == 0 or b == len(signal):
                    continue
                peak = int(a+np.argmax(abs(signal[a:b])))
                amplitude = float(abs(signal[peak]))
                if amplitude <= max(float(threshold[col]), 1e-12):
                    continue
                columns[col].append(dict(column=col, run_id=run_id, sequence=sequence,
                    sign=1 if signal[peak] > 0 else -1, peak_index=int(begin+peak),
                    peak_time_s=float(t[begin+peak]), amplitude=amplitude,
                    first=int(begin+a), last=int(begin+b-1),
                    start_s=float(t[begin+a]), end_s=float(t[begin+b-1])))
    return filtered, columns


def _ridges(columns, t, s):
    nodes=[]
    for column in columns:
        for node in column:
            node['id']=len(nodes);nodes.append(node)
    parent=list(range(len(nodes)))

    def find(i):
        while parent[i] != i:
            parent[i]=parent[parent[i]];i=parent[i]
        return i

    for left, right in zip(columns[:-1], columns[1:]):
        choices=[]
        for a in left:
            for b in right:
                delay=abs(a['peak_time_s']-b['peak_time_s'])
                scale=.5*((a['end_s']-a['start_s'])+(b['end_s']-b['start_s']))
                overlap=min(a['end_s'],b['end_s'])-max(a['start_s'],b['start_s'])
                if a['sign']==b['sign'] and delay<=scale and overlap>=0:
                    choices.append((delay,a['id'],b['id']))
        used_left=set();used_right=set()
        for _, a, b in sorted(choices):
            if a in used_left or b in used_right:continue
            parent[find(a)]=find(b);used_left.add(a);used_right.add(b)
    groups={}
    for node in nodes:groups.setdefault(find(node['id']),[]).append(node)
    ridges=[]
    for group in groups.values():
        if len(group)<3:continue
        group=sorted(group,key=lambda node:node['column'])
        ids=np.array([node['column'] for node in group])
        arrival=np.array([node['peak_time_s'] for node in group])
        ridges.append(dict(id=len(ridges), sign=group[0]['sign'], nodes=group,
                           fit=_fit(s[ids],arrival), center_s=float(np.median(arrival))))
    return ridges


def _phase_edges(ridges):
    edges={}
    for i,a in enumerate(ridges):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ridges)):
            b=ridges[j]
            if a['sign']==b['sign']:continue
            left={node['column']:node for node in a['nodes']}
            right={node['column']:node for node in b['nodes']}
            common=sorted(set(left)&set(right))
            # Adjacent phases must belong to the same continuous track run in
            # each shared column. No phase pairing over a missing interval.
            common=[k for k in common if left[k]['run_id']==right[k]['run_id'] and
                    abs(left[k]['sequence']-right[k]['sequence'])==1]
            if len(common)<3 or np.any(np.diff(common)!=1):continue
            delta=np.array([right[k]['peak_time_s']-left[k]['peak_time_s'] for k in common])
            if not (np.all(delta>0) or np.all(delta<0)):continue
            early,late=(a,b) if delta.mean()>0 else (b,a)
            edges[i,j]=dict(first=early['id'], second=late['id'], columns=common,
                            score=(1,len(common),int(early['sign']<0)))
    return edges


def _pair_phases(ridges, edges):
    """Non-overlapping adjacent phase pairs, without counting both polarities.

    Maximize shared support weighted by propagation coherence. No reward for
    pair count or preferred polarity. Unselected phases remain review evidence.
    """
    neighbors={i:[] for i in range(len(ridges))}
    for i,j in edges:neighbors[i].append(j);neighbors[j].append(i)
    ambiguous=[i for i in neighbors if len(neighbors[i])>1]
    negative=[i for i in neighbors if ridges[i]['sign']<0 and neighbors[i]]
    positive=[i for i in neighbors if ridges[i]['sign']>0 and neighbors[i]]
    if not negative or not positive:return [],ambiguous
    n=len(negative);m=len(positive)
    # Dummy columns permit unmatched phases, including zero-coherence pairs.
    cost=np.zeros((n,m+n),float)
    cost[:,:m]=1
    for row,i in enumerate(negative):
        for col,j in enumerate(positive):
            edge=edges.get(tuple(sorted((i,j))))
            if edge is not None:
                cost[row,col]=-edge['association_weight']
    # Rectangular shortest-augmenting-path assignment (rows <= columns).
    u=np.zeros(n+1);v=np.zeros(m+n+1)
    owner=np.zeros(m+n+1,int);previous=np.zeros(m+n+1,int)
    for row in range(1,n+1):
        owner[0]=row;col=0;minimum=np.full(m+n+1,np.inf);used=np.zeros(m+n+1,bool)
        while True:
            used[col]=True;current_row=owner[col];delta=np.inf;next_col=0
            for j in range(1,m+n+1):
                if used[j]:continue
                reduced=cost[current_row-1,j-1]-u[current_row]-v[j]
                if reduced<minimum[j]:minimum[j]=reduced;previous[j]=col
                if minimum[j]<delta:delta=minimum[j];next_col=j
            u[owner[used]]+=delta;v[used]-=delta;minimum[~used]-=delta
            col=next_col
            if owner[col]==0:break
        while col:
            before=previous[col];owner[col]=owner[before];col=before
    chosen=[]
    for col in range(1,m+1):
        if owner[col] and cost[owner[col]-1,col-1]<0:
            chosen.append(tuple(sorted((negative[owner[col]-1],positive[col-1]))))
    return chosen,ambiguous


def detect_radial_events(signal, valid, time_s, space, *, noise_floor, config=None):
    """Find radial propagation evidence, with an explicit noise scale.

    noise_floor is an absolute A threshold (s^-1), scalar or one per section.
    It must come from a stated noise reference, not per-video robust-z scaling.
    Space stays on the original full cervix-to-fundus axis, without rescaling.
    """
    config=config or RadialWaveConfig()
    y=np.asarray(signal,float);m=np.asarray(valid,bool);t=np.asarray(time_s,float);s=np.asarray(space,float)
    if (y.shape!=m.shape or y.shape!=(len(t),len(s)) or len(t)<3 or len(s)<3 or
            not np.isfinite(t).all() or not np.isfinite(s).all() or np.any(np.diff(s)<=0) or
            np.any((s<0)|(s>1)) or np.any(np.diff(t)<=0) or
            not np.allclose(np.diff(t),np.median(np.diff(t)),rtol=1e-5,atol=1e-9)):
        raise ValueError('Expected matching native-uniform time x ordered normalized-space maps')
    threshold=np.broadcast_to(np.asarray(noise_floor,float),(len(s),))
    if not np.isfinite(threshold).all() or np.any(threshold<0):raise ValueError('Invalid absolute noise floor')
    if not 0<=config.min_fit_r2<=1 or config.synchronous_tolerance_s<0:
        raise ValueError('Invalid radial event support settings')
    filtered,columns=_lobes(y,m&np.isfinite(y),t,threshold)
    ridges=_ridges(columns,t,s);edges=_phase_edges(ridges)
    measured={key:_measure_pair(edge,ridges,filtered,t,s,config) for key,edge in edges.items()}
    pairs,ambiguous=_pair_phases(ridges,edges);events=[]
    for key in pairs:
        events.append(dict(measured[key],event_id='',reviewed=False,
                           phase_pairing_ambiguous=any(i in ambiguous for i in key)))
    used={i for key in pairs for i in key}
    for ridge in ridges:
        if ridge['id'] in used:continue
        nodes=ridge['nodes'];ids=np.array([n['column'] for n in nodes])
        arrival=np.array([n['peak_time_s'] for n in nodes])
        event=_describe_arrival(ids,arrival,s,float(np.median(np.diff(t))),[],config)
        event['quality_flags'].append('single_phase_event_identity_unresolved')
        events.append(dict(event,event_id='',reviewed=False,evidence_kind='single_phase',
            arrival_marker='phase_peak',start_s=min(n['start_s'] for n in nodes),
            end_s=max(n['end_s'] for n in nodes),leading_polarity=ridge['sign'],
            phase_ridge_ids=[ridge['id']],phase_pairing_ambiguous=ridge['id'] in ambiguous,
            first_phase_arrival_s=arrival.tolist(),second_phase_arrival_s=[],
            peak_absolute_rate_per_phase=[max(n['amplitude'] for n in nodes)]))
    for event in events:
        if event['phase_pairing_ambiguous']:
            event['quality_flags'].append('alternative_phase_pairing')
    events.sort(key=lambda event:(np.median(event['arrival_time_s']),event['s_start']))
    for i,event in enumerate(events,1):event['event_id']=f'candidate_{i:03d}'
    return dict(status='automatic_radial_wave_candidates_require_review',config=asdict(config),
        candidate_algorithm='radial_phase_evidence_v2',
        noise_floor_rate=threshold.copy(),filter_band_cpm=[.5,5.],filtered_A=filtered,
        events=events,candidate_count=len(events),
        traveling_candidate_count=sum(e['classification'] in ('C2F','F2C') for e in events),
        ambiguous_phase_ridge_ids=ambiguous,unpaired_ridge_count=len(ridges)-2*len(pairs),
        alternative_phase_pairs=[dict(measured[key],selected=key in pairs)
                                 for key in edges],
        event_count=None,event_frequency_cpm=None,
        phase_ridges=[dict(ridge_id=r['id'],sign=r['sign'],sections=[x['column'] for x in r['nodes']],
                           peak_time_s=[x['peak_time_s'] for x in r['nodes']],fit=r['fit']) for r in ridges])


def radial_candidates_from_maps(spacetime, *, noise_floor, s_range=(0.,1.), config=None):
    """Use current 2 s radial-map interface and retain original section IDs."""
    a=np.asarray(spacetime['A'],float);valid=np.asarray(spacetime['valid_mask'],bool)
    t=np.asarray(spacetime['interval_center_s'],float);s=np.asarray(spacetime['s'],float)
    if a.shape!=(len(t),2,len(s)) or valid.shape!=a.shape:
        raise ValueError('Expected time x two-wall x section radial maps')
    if len(s_range)!=2 or not 0<=s_range[0]<s_range[1]<=1:
        raise ValueError('Invalid anatomical s_range')
    ids=np.flatnonzero((s>=s_range[0])&(s<=s_range[1]))
    if len(ids)<3:raise ValueError('Selected region needs at least three native sections')
    filtered=np.full_like(a,np.nan);walls=[]
    for wall in (0,1):
        result=detect_radial_events(a[:,wall][:,ids],valid[:,wall][:,ids],t,s[ids],
                                   noise_floor=noise_floor,config=config)
        filtered[:,wall,ids]=result.pop('filtered_A')
        for event in result['events']:
            event['event_id']=f"wall{wall}_{event['event_id']}"
            event['wall']=wall;event['sections']=ids[event['sections']].tolist()
        for ridge in result['phase_ridges']:ridge['sections']=ids[ridge['sections']].tolist()
        for edge in result['alternative_phase_pairs']:
            edge['sections']=ids[edge['sections']].tolist()
            edge['wall']=wall
        walls.append(dict(wall=wall,**result))
    report=dict(status='automatic_radial_wave_candidates_require_review',
        measurement_source='A_rate',measurement_units='s^-1',source_section_indices=ids.tolist(),
        requested_s_range=list(s_range),sampled_s_range=[float(s[ids[0]]),float(s[ids[-1]])],
        center_time_range_s=[float(t[0]),float(t[-1])],
        nominal_interval_s=float(spacetime.get('nominal_interval_s',np.median(spacetime['interval_dt_s']))),
        walls=walls,event_count=None,event_frequency_cpm=None,
        note='Two-wall candidate counts are not summed; review event identity and arrivals before patient rates.')
    arrays=dict(filtered_A=filtered,candidate_valid_mask=valid&np.isfinite(filtered),
                interval_center_s=t.copy(),s=s.copy())
    return report,arrays

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/build_medium_consensus.py | git_blob_sha=95689af03008b0ddf21765ae24dd7a6bcc8c75f6 ================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Outcome-blind test of robust multi-window consensus for medium confidence."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

import extract_longitudinal_frequency as longitudinal
from validate_reproducibility import icc_absolute_agreement


REGIONS = ("cervical", "middle", "fundal")
WALLS = ("anterior", "posterior")
TOLERANCE_CPM = 0.25
DETAIL_COLUMNS = [
    "case_id", "wall", "region", "full_frequency_cpm",
    "trim_front_10_frequency_cpm", "trim_back_10_frequency_cpm",
    "three_window_range_cpm", "consensus_candidate",
    "consensus_frequency_cpm", "holdout_trim_both_frequency_cpm",
    "holdout_trim_both_retained", "holdout_absolute_difference_cpm",
    "validated_stable_medium",
]


def perturbed_source(source: pd.Series, trim_front: float, trim_back: float) -> pd.Series:
    output = source.copy()
    start = int(longitudinal.finite_float(source.get("selected_start_frame"), -1))
    stop = int(longitudinal.finite_float(source.get("selected_stop_frame_exclusive"), -1))
    if start < 0 or stop <= start:
        return output
    length = stop - start
    output["selected_start_frame"] = start + int(math.floor(length * trim_front))
    output["selected_stop_frame_exclusive"] = stop - int(math.floor(length * trim_back))
    return output


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--source-run", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--longitudinal-run", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--reproducibility-run", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.output.exists():
        raise FileExistsError(args.output)
    args.output.mkdir(parents=True)

    source_regions = pd.read_csv(
        args.source_run / "merged_results" / "optimized_region_frequency.csv",
        dtype={"case_id": str},
    )
    baseline = pd.read_csv(
        args.longitudinal_run / "longitudinal_region_frequency.csv",
        dtype={"case_id": str},
    )
    reproducibility = pd.read_csv(
        args.reproducibility_run / "region_reproducibility_details.csv",
        dtype={"case_id": str},
    )
    source_regions["case_id"] = source_regions["case_id"].astype(str).str.strip()
    baseline["case_id"] = baseline["case_id"].astype(str).str.strip()
    reproducibility["case_id"] = reproducibility["case_id"].astype(str).str.strip()
    medium = baseline.loc[
        baseline["longitudinal_technical_confidence"].eq("medium")
    ].copy()
    cache_paths = longitudinal.cache_map(args.source_run)
    records = []

    for case_order, (case_id, own_medium) in enumerate(
        medium.groupby(medium["case_id"].astype(str), sort=True), start=1
    ):
        tracked, initial, anterior_inward, posterior_inward = longitudinal.load_cache(cache_paths[case_id])
        count = len(initial) // 2
        tangents = np.vstack((
            longitudinal.local_tangents(initial[:count]),
            longitudinal.local_tangents(initial[count:]),
        ))
        inward = np.vstack((anterior_inward, posterior_inward))
        region_map = dict(zip(REGIONS, np.array_split(np.arange(count), 3)))
        for _, base in own_medium.iterrows():
            wall, region = str(base["wall"]), str(base["region"])
            source = source_regions.loc[
                (source_regions["case_id"].astype(str) == case_id)
                & source_regions["wall"].eq(wall)
                & source_regions["region"].eq(region)
            ].iloc[0]
            local = region_map[region]
            columns = local + (0 if wall == "anterior" else count)
            estimates = {}
            for name, front, back in [
                ("trim_front_10", 0.10, 0.0),
                ("trim_back_10", 0.0, 0.10),
            ]:
                result, _ = longitudinal.analyse_region(
                    perturbed_source(source, front, back), tracked, initial,
                    tangents, inward, local, columns,
                )
                estimates[name] = result
            holdout = reproducibility.loc[
                (reproducibility["case_id"].astype(str) == case_id)
                & reproducibility["wall"].eq(wall)
                & reproducibility["region"].eq(region)
            ].iloc[0]
            full_frequency = float(base["longitudinal_frequency_cpm"])
            front_frequency = longitudinal.finite_float(
                estimates["trim_front_10"].get("longitudinal_frequency_cpm")
            )
            back_frequency = longitudinal.finite_float(
                estimates["trim_back_10"].get("longitudinal_frequency_cpm")
            )
            three = np.asarray([full_frequency, front_frequency, back_frequency], float)
            all_finite = bool(np.isfinite(three).all())
            all_retained = bool(
                estimates["trim_front_10"]["retained_for_longitudinal_model"]
                and estimates["trim_back_10"]["retained_for_longitudinal_model"]
            )
            range_cpm = float(np.ptp(three)) if all_finite else math.nan
            consensus_candidate = bool(
                all_finite and all_retained and range_cpm <= TOLERANCE_CPM + 1e-12
            )
            consensus = float(np.median(three)) if consensus_candidate else math.nan
            holdout_frequency = pd.to_numeric(
                holdout["cropped_window_frequency_cpm"], errors="coerce"
            )
            holdout_retained = bool(holdout["cropped_window_retained"])
            holdout_difference = (
                abs(float(holdout_frequency) - consensus)
                if consensus_candidate and np.isfinite(holdout_frequency) else math.nan
            )
            validated = bool(
                consensus_candidate and holdout_retained
                and np.isfinite(holdout_difference)
                and holdout_difference <= TOLERANCE_CPM + 1e-12
            )
            records.append({
                "case_id": case_id,
                "wall": wall,
                "region": region,
                "full_frequency_cpm": full_frequency,
                "trim_front_10_frequency_cpm": front_frequency,
                "trim_back_10_frequency_cpm": back_frequency,
                "three_window_range_cpm": range_cpm,
                "consensus_candidate": consensus_candidate,
                "consensus_frequency_cpm": consensus,
                "holdout_trim_both_frequency_cpm": holdout_frequency,
                "holdout_trim_both_retained": holdout_retained,
                "holdout_absolute_difference_cpm": holdout_difference,
                "validated_stable_medium": validated,
            })
        if case_order % 25 == 0:
            print(f"medium-patient groups {case_order}", flush=True)

    table = pd.DataFrame(records, columns=DETAIL_COLUMNS)
    candidates = table.loc[table["consensus_candidate"]].copy()
    paired_candidates = candidates.loc[
        candidates["consensus_frequency_cpm"].notna()
        & candidates["holdout_trim_both_frequency_cpm"].notna()
    ].copy()
    validated = table.loc[table["validated_stable_medium"]].copy()
    patient_availability = baseline.groupby("case_id").agg(
        has_high=("longitudinal_technical_confidence", lambda s: bool((s == "high").any())),
        has_medium=("longitudinal_technical_confidence", lambda s: bool((s == "medium").any())),
    ).reset_index()
    validated_cases = set(validated["case_id"].astype(str))
    patient_availability["has_validated_stable_medium"] = (
        patient_availability["case_id"].astype(str).isin(validated_cases)
    )
    patient_availability["eligible_high_or_validated_medium"] = (
        patient_availability["has_high"]
        | patient_availability["has_validated_stable_medium"]
    )

    if len(paired_candidates) >= 3:
        icc = icc_absolute_agreement(
            paired_candidates["consensus_frequency_cpm"].to_numpy(float),
            paired_candidates["holdout_trim_both_frequency_cpm"].to_numpy(float),
        )
        holdout_within = float(
            (paired_candidates["holdout_absolute_difference_cpm"] <= TOLERANCE_CPM + 1e-12).mean()
        )
    else:
        icc, holdout_within = math.nan, math.nan
    summary = {
        "clinical_outcome_read": False,
        "medium_regions_tested": int(len(table)),
        "three_window_consensus_candidates": int(len(candidates)),
        "three_window_consensus_candidate_fraction": (
            float(len(candidates) / len(table)) if len(table) else 0.0
        ),
        "consensus_candidates_with_finite_holdout": int(len(paired_candidates)),
        "validated_stable_medium_regions": int(len(validated)),
        "validated_stable_medium_fraction": (
            float(len(validated) / len(table)) if len(table) else 0.0
        ),
        "consensus_vs_independent_both_trim_icc": icc,
        "consensus_vs_independent_both_trim_within_0_25_fraction_among_candidates": holdout_within,
        "validated_stable_medium_patients": int(len(validated_cases)),
        "patients_with_high": int(patient_availability["has_high"].sum()),
        "patients_high_or_validated_stable_medium": int(
            patient_availability["eligible_high_or_validated_medium"].sum()
        ),
        "additional_patients_beyond_high_only": int(
            patient_availability["eligible_high_or_validated_medium"].sum()
            - patient_availability["has_high"].sum()
        ),
        "consensus_definition": "median of full, trim-front-10%, trim-back-10%; all retained and max-min <=0.25 cpm",
        "independent_perturbation": "trim both ends by 10%, not used to form consensus",
    }
    table.to_csv(args.output / "medium_consensus_details.csv", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig")
    patient_availability.to_csv(
        args.output / "patient_availability_after_consensus.csv", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig"
    )
    (args.output / "medium_consensus_summary.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
    )
    report = f"""# 中可信纵向频率多窗口共识测试

- 测试中可信区域：{summary['medium_regions_tested']}个。
- 完整/去前10%/去后10%三窗口一致候选：{summary['three_window_consensus_candidates']}个（{summary['three_window_consensus_candidate_fraction']:.1%}）。
- 再经独立首尾各裁10%验证：{summary['validated_stable_medium_regions']}个（{summary['validated_stable_medium_fraction']:.1%}）。
- 共识频率与独立扰动ICC：{summary['consensus_vs_independent_both_trim_icc']:.3f}。
- 高可信患者：{summary['patients_with_high']}例；加入验证稳定的中可信后：{summary['patients_high_or_validated_stable_medium']}例，新增{summary['additional_patients_beyond_high_only']}例。

本测试不读取临床结局。只有通过独立扰动验证的中可信区域，才可考虑与高可信区域共同进入主要分析；其余中可信区域仍仅作探索。
"""
    (args.output / "中可信共识测试报告.md").write_text(report, encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/ab_block_tracking.py | git_blob_sha=caffa3490fe19ddd9fe44c9310a4442e81415c14 ================
"""Continuous image-block tracking. Saved tracks are seeds, never filled data."""
import cv2
import numpy as np
from cuda_sparse_lk import calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk

PATCH_RADIUS = 10
MIN_NCC = .75
MAX_FB_PX = .75
MIN_PATCH_STD = 2.


def patch(image, point):
    x, y = point
    h, w = image.shape
    if not np.isfinite(point).all() or min(x, y) < PATCH_RADIUS+1 or x >= w-PATCH_RADIUS-1 or y >= h-PATCH_RADIUS-1:
        return None
    return cv2.getRectSubPix(image, (21, 21), (float(x), float(y)))


def ncc(a, b):
    x, y = a.astype(float).ravel(), b.astype(float).ravel()
    x, y = x-x.mean(), y-y.mean()
    norm = np.linalg.norm(x)*np.linalg.norm(y)
    return float(x @ y/norm) if norm > 1e-10 else -1.


def track_step(previous, current, points):
    p = np.asarray(points, np.float32).reshape(-1, 1, 2)
    found, ok, _ = calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk(
        previous, current, p, win_size=(21, 21), max_level=3, iterations=30)
    if found is None:
        return np.full((len(p), 2), np.nan), np.zeros(len(p), bool), np.full(len(p), -1.), np.full(len(p), np.inf)
    back, bok, _ = calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk(
        current, previous, found, win_size=(21, 21), max_level=3, iterations=30)
    if back is None:
        return found[:, 0], np.zeros(len(p), bool), np.full(len(p), -1.), np.full(len(p), np.inf)
    fb = np.linalg.norm(back[:, 0]-p[:, 0], axis=1)
    good = ok.ravel().astype(bool) & bok.ravel().astype(bool) & (fb <= MAX_FB_PX)
    scores = np.full(len(p), -1.)
    for j in np.flatnonzero(good):
        a, b = patch(previous, p[j, 0]), patch(current, found[j, 0])
        if a is not None and b is not None and min(a.std(), b.std()) >= MIN_PATCH_STD:
            scores[j] = ncc(a, b)
    return found[:, 0], good & (scores >= MIN_NCC), scores, fb
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/ab_block_tracking.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/contrast.py | git_blob_sha=3e585c9bc6ae9ad94347fa7c6c7b84609908acce ================
import cv2
import numpy as np


def local_contrast(gray: np.ndarray, sigma: float = 5.) -> np.ndarray:
    source = gray.astype(np.float32)
    mean = cv2.GaussianBlur(source, (0, 0), sigma)
    square = cv2.GaussianBlur(source * source, (0, 0), sigma)
    std = np.sqrt(np.maximum(square - mean * mean, 0))
    mapped = 128 + 35 * (source - mean) / (std + 5)
    return np.uint8(np.where(gray > 12, np.clip(np.rint(mapped), 0, 255), 0))
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/contrast.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/cuda_sparse_lk.py | git_blob_sha=547ef154f6ccd0df566b6c5ecaf7cda2e5d83324 ================
"""CUDA-backed sparse pyramidal Lucas-Kanade optical flow."""
import cv2
import numpy as np


_FLOWS = {}


def _create_flow(win_size, max_level, iterations):
    if cv2.cuda.getCudaEnabledDeviceCount() < 1:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "CUDA-enabled OpenCV with the cudaoptflow module is required"
        )
    cv2.cuda.setDevice(0)
    key = (tuple(win_size), int(max_level), int(iterations))
    if key not in _FLOWS:
        sparse_lk = getattr(cv2, "cuda_SparsePyrLKOpticalFlow", None)
        if sparse_lk is not None:
            _FLOWS[key] = sparse_lk.create(key[0], key[1], key[2], False)
        else:
            factory = getattr(cv2.cuda, "SparsePyrLKOpticalFlow_create", None)
            if factory is None:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "OpenCV was built without the cudaoptflow module"
                )
            _FLOWS[key] = factory(key[0], key[1], key[2], False)
    return _FLOWS[key]


def _upload(array):
    gpu = cv2.cuda_GpuMat()
    gpu.upload(np.ascontiguousarray(array))
    return gpu


def calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk(
    previous, current, points, *, win_size, max_level, iterations=30
):
    """Return CUDA LK results in the same array layout used by the CPU call."""
    point_count = len(points)
    if point_count == 0:
        return (
            np.empty((0, 1, 2), np.float32),
            np.empty((0, 1), np.uint8),
            np.empty((0, 1), np.float32),
        )
    flow = _create_flow(win_size, max_level, iterations)
    previous_gpu = _upload(previous)
    current_gpu = _upload(current)
    points_gpu = _upload(
        np.asarray(points, np.float32).reshape(1, point_count, 2)
    )
    found_gpu, status_gpu, error_gpu = flow.calc(
        previous_gpu, current_gpu, points_gpu, None
    )
    found = found_gpu.download().reshape(point_count, 1, 2)
    status = status_gpu.download().reshape(point_count, 1)
    error = (
        error_gpu.download().reshape(point_count, 1)
        if error_gpu is not None else None
    )
    return found, status, error
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/cuda_sparse_lk.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/frequency.py | git_blob_sha=06b985847106ded3ee465f96158dff843680f6cd ================
import numpy as np
from scipy.signal import find_peaks
GRID_CPM = np.round(np.arange(.5, 5.0001, .05), 8)
MIN_CYCLES = 2.0


def segments(mask: np.ndarray, epoch: np.ndarray, seeded: np.ndarray, time: np.ndarray):
    """Every invalid frame, epoch change, seed, or missing timestamp starts a new part."""
    starts = np.flatnonzero(mask & (~np.r_[False, mask[:-1]] |
                    np.r_[True, np.diff(epoch) != 0] | seeded |
                    np.r_[True, np.diff(time) > 1.5 * np.median(np.diff(time))]))
    ends = np.flatnonzero(mask & ~np.r_[mask[1:], False]) + 1
    # A reset can occur without an invalid frame.
    breaks = sorted(set(starts.tolist() + ends.tolist()))
    return [(a, b) for a, b in zip(breaks[:-1], breaks[1:])
            if a < len(mask) and mask[a] and b > a]

def fit_frequency(time: np.ndarray, signal: np.ndarray):
    """Use the project's linear-trend + sine/cosine score on one raw curve."""
    span = float(time[-1] - time[0])
    allowed = GRID_CPM[GRID_CPM * span / 60 >= MIN_CYCLES - 1e-9]
    base = dict(candidate_period_s=None, candidate_cpm=None, score=None,
                lag_correlation=None, half_lag_correlation=None,
                alternative_peak_cpm=None, status='period_unclear', reason='')
    if len(allowed) < 3 or len(time) < 20:
        base['reason'] = 'continuous_segment_too_short_for_two_cycles_in_search_range'
        return base, allowed, np.array([]), None, None
    tt = time - time.mean()
    trend_x = np.column_stack((np.ones(len(tt)), tt))
    trend_beta = np.linalg.lstsq(trend_x, signal, rcond=None)[0]
    trend = trend_x @ trend_beta
    resid = signal - trend
    energy = float(resid @ resid)
    if energy / len(time) < 1e-12:
        base['reason'] = 'nearly_constant_after_linear_trend'
        return base, allowed, np.zeros(len(allowed)), trend, None
    scores = []
    for f in allowed:
        phase = 2 * np.pi * tt * f / 60
        design = np.column_stack((trend_x, np.sin(phase), np.cos(phase)))
        fitted = design @ np.linalg.lstsq(design, signal, rcond=None)[0]
        scores.append(np.clip(1 - float(((signal - fitted) ** 2).sum()) / energy, 0, 1))
    scores = np.asarray(scores)
    k = int(np.argmax(scores))
    best = float(allowed[k])
    period = 60 / best
    phase = 2 * np.pi * tt * best / 60
    design = np.column_stack((trend_x, np.sin(phase), np.cos(phase)))
    fitted = design @ np.linalg.lstsq(design, signal, rcond=None)[0]
    # Cycle-to-cycle similarity is descriptive. It is not a validated test.
    lag = np.interp(time + period, time, resid, left=np.nan, right=np.nan)
    half = np.interp(time + period / 2, time, resid, left=np.nan, right=np.nan)
    def corr(x):
        good = np.isfinite(x)
        if good.sum() < 20 or np.std(resid[good]) < 1e-6 or np.std(x[good]) < 1e-6:
            return None
        return float(np.corrcoef(resid[good], x[good])[0, 1])
    same, opposite = corr(lag), corr(half)
    peaks, _ = find_peaks(scores)
    distant = [j for j in peaks if abs(allowed[j] - best) >= max(.3, .15 * best)]
    alternative = max(distant, key=lambda j: scores[j]) if distant else None
    competing = alternative is not None and scores[alternative] >= .9 * scores[k]
    boundary = k == 0 or k == len(allowed) - 1
    reasons = []
    if boundary:
        reasons.append('search_boundary')
    if competing:
        reasons.append('similar_distant_peak')
    if scores[k] < .2:
        reasons.append('weak_improvement_over_linear_trend')
    if same is None or same < .5 or opposite is None or opposite > -.2:
        reasons.append('repetition_not_supported_by_lag_comparison')
    base.update(candidate_period_s=period, candidate_cpm=best, score=float(scores[k]),
                lag_correlation=same, half_lag_correlation=opposite,
                alternative_peak_cpm=float(allowed[alternative]) if alternative is not None else None,
                status='exploratory_repetition_candidate' if not reasons else 'period_unclear',
                reason=';'.join(reasons) if reasons else 'exploratory_rules_passed_not_validated')
    return base, allowed, scores, trend, fitted
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/frequency.py ================

================ BEGIN FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/rigid_correction.py | git_blob_sha=dbd8ab1569062094f825db7566df2c62a97c4b2f ================
import cv2
import numpy as np
from cuda_sparse_lk import calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk


def rigid(a, b):
    x, y = a.mean(0), b.mean(0)
    u, _, vt = np.linalg.svd((a-x).T @ (b-y))
    fix = np.eye(2)
    fix[-1, -1] = np.linalg.det(u @ vt)
    rotation = u @ fix @ vt
    return np.column_stack((rotation.T, y-x @ rotation))

def step(a, b, wall_points):
    mask = np.uint8((a > 12) & (b > 12))*255
    mask = cv2.erode(mask, np.ones((17, 17), np.uint8))
    mask[:125] = 0
    mask[410:] = 0
    mask[:, :20] = 0
    mask[:, 520:] = 0
    region = np.zeros_like(mask)
    cv2.fillConvexPoly(region, cv2.convexHull(np.rint(wall_points).astype(np.int32)), 255)
    region = cv2.dilate(region, np.ones((31, 31), np.uint8))
    mask[region > 0] = 0
    p = cv2.goodFeaturesToTrack(a, maxCorners=180, qualityLevel=.015, minDistance=8,
                                mask=mask, blockSize=7)
    stats = dict(pose_valid=False, training_inliers=0, heldout_count=0,
                 heldout_raw_median_px=np.nan, heldout_corrected_median_px=np.nan)
    if p is None or len(p) < 18:
        return None, stats
    q, ok, _ = calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk(
        a, b, p, win_size=(25, 25), max_level=3, iterations=30)
    if q is None:
        return None, stats
    back, bok, _ = calc_optical_flow_pyr_lk(
        b, a, q, win_size=(25, 25), max_level=3, iterations=30)
    if back is None:
        return None, stats
    good = (ok.ravel().astype(bool) & bok.ravel().astype(bool) &
            (np.linalg.norm(back[:, 0]-p[:, 0], axis=1) < 1.))
    # Holdout assignment precedes fitting and has no dependency on residuals.
    heldout = np.arange(len(p)) % 3 == 0
    train = good & ~heldout
    test = good & heldout
    p, q = p[:, 0].astype(float), q[:, 0].astype(float)
    if train.sum() < 12:
        return None, stats
    _, inlier = cv2.estimateAffinePartial2D(p[train], q[train], method=cv2.RANSAC,
                                           ransacReprojThreshold=1.5, maxIters=1000)
    if inlier is None or inlier.sum() < 8:
        return None, stats
    select = inlier.ravel().astype(bool)
    matrix = rigid(p[train][select], q[train][select])
    predicted = p[test] @ matrix[:, :2].T + matrix[:, 2]
    stats.update(pose_valid=True, training_inliers=int(select.sum()), heldout_count=int(test.sum()))
    if test.any():
        stats.update(heldout_raw_median_px=float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(q[test]-p[test], axis=1))),
                     heldout_corrected_median_px=float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(q[test]-predicted, axis=1))))
    return matrix, stats
================ END FILE: 01_feature_extraction/frozen_runtime_20261126/r2_frozen/code/rigid_correction.py ================

"""Read-only evidence sidecar for the frozen V1 uterine-motion pipeline.

The script reuses the frozen tracker, rigid registration, regional support,
and frequency functions unchanged.  It adds descriptive evidence channels
for global motion, neighboring wall points, paired-wall distance, brightness,
and tracking quality.  It does not classify real physiology or modify tracks.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import numpy as np


HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PACKAGE = HERE.parent
CODE = PACKAGE / "code"
sys.path.insert(0, str(CODE))

from ab_block_tracking import patch as tracking_patch  # noqa: E402
from ab_block_tracking import track_step  # noqa: E402
from contrast import local_contrast  # noqa: E402
from rigid_correction import step as rigid_step  # noqa: E402
import validate_regional_v1_1 as frozen  # noqa: E402


RANDOM_SEED = 20261028
FROZEN_FILES = (
    "ab_block_tracking.py",
    "cuda_sparse_lk.py",
    "contrast.py",
    "rigid_correction.py",
    "frequency.py",
    "run_regional_frequency.py",
    "validate_regional_v1_1.py",
)


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def write_csv(path: Path, rows: list[dict]) -> None:
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError(f"No rows for {path.name}")
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as stream:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(rows)


def finite_mean(values: np.ndarray, axis=None):
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    if axis is None:
        selected = values[np.isfinite(values)]
        return float(selected.mean()) if len(selected) else math.nan
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
        return np.nanmean(values, axis=axis)


def safe_corr(left: np.ndarray, right: np.ndarray) -> float:
    left = np.asarray(left, float)
    right = np.asarray(right, float)
    mask = np.isfinite(left) & np.isfinite(right)
    if mask.sum() < 3:
        return math.nan
    x = left[mask] - left[mask].mean()
    y = right[mask] - right[mask].mean()
    scale = float(np.linalg.norm(x) * np.linalg.norm(y))
    return float(x @ y / scale) if scale > 1e-12 else math.nan


def finite_range(values: np.ndarray) -> float:
    values = np.asarray(values, float)
    selected = values[np.isfinite(values)]
    return float(np.ptp(selected)) if len(selected) else math.nan


def contiguous_runs(mask: np.ndarray, epoch: np.ndarray) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    mask = np.asarray(mask, bool)
    epoch = np.asarray(epoch)
    starts, stops = [], []
    active = False
    for index, good in enumerate(mask):
        continued = index > 0 and good and mask[index - 1] and epoch[index] == epoch[index - 1]
        if good and not continued:
            starts.append(index)
            active = True
        if active and (not good or index == len(mask) - 1):
            stops.append(index if good and index == len(mask) - 1 else index - 1)
            active = False
    return [(start, stop + 1) for start, stop in zip(starts, stops)]


def patch_median(frame: np.ndarray, point: np.ndarray) -> float:
    own = tracking_patch(frame, point)
    return float(np.median(own)) if own is not None else math.nan


def target_layout(
    holdout: dict,
    anterior_axes: np.ndarray,
    posterior_axes: np.ndarray,
) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    count = len(anterior_axes)
    regions = dict(zip(frozen.REGION_NAMES, np.array_split(np.arange(count), 3)))
    local = regions[holdout["target_region"]]
    columns = local if holdout["target_wall"] == "anterior" else local + count
    all_axes = np.vstack((anterior_axes, posterior_axes))
    return local.astype(int), columns.astype(int), all_axes


def track_with_evidence(
    gray: np.ndarray,
    initial: np.ndarray,
    local_vector: np.ndarray,
    local_envelope: np.ndarray,
    condition: str,
    frequency_cpm: float,
    duration_s: int,
    target_columns: np.ndarray,
) -> dict:
    """Frozen tracking path with additional read-only diagnostic capture."""
    time_s = np.arange(int(round(duration_s * frozen.FPS)) + 1, dtype=float) / frozen.FPS
    n, count = len(time_s), len(initial)
    positions = np.full((n, count, 2), np.nan, np.float32)
    raw_positions = np.full_like(positions, np.nan)
    valid = np.zeros((n, count), bool)
    raw_valid = np.zeros_like(valid)
    corrected = np.full((n, count, 2), np.nan, float)
    pose_valid = np.zeros(n, bool)
    epoch = np.zeros(n, int)
    poses = np.full((n, 3, 3), np.nan, float)
    target_ncc = np.full((n, count), np.nan, float)
    target_fb = np.full((n, count), np.nan, float)
    local_brightness = np.full(n, np.nan, float)
    global_brightness = np.full(n, np.nan, float)
    heldout_raw = np.full(n, np.nan, float)
    heldout_corrected = np.full(n, np.nan, float)
    training_inliers = np.zeros(n, int)
    heldout_count = np.zeros(n, int)

    yy, xx = np.indices(gray.shape, dtype=np.float32)
    fan_mask = gray > 12
    accumulator = np.eye(3)
    current_epoch = 0
    previous = frozen.make_frame(
        gray, xx, yy, local_vector, local_envelope,
        condition, 0.0, frequency_cpm,
    )
    processed_previous = local_contrast(previous)
    positions[0] = raw_positions[0] = initial
    valid[0] = raw_valid[0] = True
    corrected[0] = initial
    pose_valid[0] = True
    poses[0] = accumulator
    local_brightness[0] = finite_mean([
        patch_median(previous, initial[column]) for column in target_columns
    ])
    global_brightness[0] = float(np.median(previous[fan_mask]))

    for index in range(1, n):
        current = frozen.make_frame(
            gray, xx, yy, local_vector, local_envelope,
            condition, float(time_s[index]), frequency_cpm,
        )
        processed = local_contrast(current)
        live = np.flatnonzero(valid[index - 1])
        if len(live):
            found, good, scores, fb = track_step(
                processed_previous, processed, positions[index - 1, live])
            target_ncc[index, live] = scores
            target_fb[index, live] = fb
            kept = live[good]
            positions[index, kept] = found[good]
            valid[index, kept] = True

        raw_live = np.flatnonzero(raw_valid[index - 1])
        if len(raw_live):
            found, good, _, _ = track_step(
                previous, current, raw_positions[index - 1, raw_live])
            kept = raw_live[good]
            raw_positions[index, kept] = found[good]
            raw_valid[index, kept] = True

        cv2.setRNGSeed(RANDOM_SEED)
        matrix, stats = rigid_step(
            previous, current, raw_positions[index - 1, raw_live]
        ) if len(raw_live) >= 3 else (None, {})
        if matrix is None:
            accumulator = np.eye(3)
            current_epoch += 1
        else:
            accumulator = accumulator @ np.linalg.inv(
                np.vstack((matrix, [0.0, 0.0, 1.0])))
            pose_valid[index] = True
            poses[index] = accumulator
            corrected[index, valid[index]] = (
                positions[index, valid[index]] @ accumulator[:2, :2].T
                + accumulator[:2, 2]
            )
        epoch[index] = current_epoch
        heldout_raw[index] = stats.get("heldout_raw_median_px", math.nan)
        heldout_corrected[index] = stats.get("heldout_corrected_median_px", math.nan)
        training_inliers[index] = stats.get("training_inliers", 0)
        heldout_count[index] = stats.get("heldout_count", 0)
        target_points = [
            patch_median(current, positions[index, column])
            for column in target_columns if valid[index, column]
        ]
        local_brightness[index] = finite_mean(target_points)
        global_brightness[index] = float(np.median(current[fan_mask]))
        previous = current
        processed_previous = processed

    return dict(
        time_s=time_s,
        positions=positions,
        raw_positions=raw_positions,
        valid=valid,
        raw_valid=raw_valid,
        corrected=corrected,
        pose_valid=pose_valid,
        epoch=epoch,
        poses=poses,
        target_ncc=target_ncc,
        target_fb=target_fb,
        local_brightness=local_brightness,
        global_brightness=global_brightness,
        heldout_raw=heldout_raw,
        heldout_corrected=heldout_corrected,
        training_inliers=training_inliers,
        heldout_count=heldout_count,
    )


def coordinate_rmse(
    tracked: dict,
    initial: np.ndarray,
    local_vector: np.ndarray,
    target_columns: np.ndarray,
    condition: str,
    frequency_cpm: float,
) -> tuple[float, int]:
    point_vectors = []
    for point in initial:
        x = int(np.clip(round(float(point[0])), 0, local_vector.shape[1] - 1))
        y = int(np.clip(round(float(point[1])), 0, local_vector.shape[0] - 1))
        point_vectors.append(local_vector[y, x])
    point_vectors = np.asarray(point_vectors, float)
    amplitude = frozen.POSITIVE_CONDITIONS.get(condition, 0.0)
    phase = np.sin(2 * np.pi * frequency_cpm * tracked["time_s"] / 60)
    expected = (
        initial[None].astype(float)
        + amplitude * phase[:, None, None] * point_vectors[None]
    )
    mask = (
        tracked["pose_valid"][:, None]
        & tracked["valid"][:, target_columns]
        & np.isfinite(tracked["corrected"][:, target_columns]).all(axis=2)
    )
    delta = tracked["corrected"][:, target_columns] - expected[:, target_columns]
    errors = np.linalg.norm(delta, axis=2)[mask]
    return (
        float(np.sqrt(np.mean(errors * errors))) if len(errors) else math.nan,
        int(len(errors)),
    )


def build_evidence_arrays(
    tracked: dict,
    initial: np.ndarray,
    all_axes: np.ndarray,
    local_indices: np.ndarray,
    target_columns: np.ndarray,
    condition: str,
    frequency_cpm: float,
    local_vector: np.ndarray,
) -> dict:
    corrected_delta = tracked["corrected"] - initial[None]
    raw_delta = tracked["positions"].astype(float) - initial[None]
    corrected_point = np.einsum(
        "nki,ki->nk", corrected_delta[:, target_columns], all_axes[target_columns])
    raw_point = np.einsum(
        "nki,ki->nk", raw_delta[:, target_columns], all_axes[target_columns])
    target_valid = (
        tracked["pose_valid"]
        & tracked["valid"][:, target_columns].all(axis=1)
        & np.isfinite(corrected_point).all(axis=1)
        & np.isfinite(raw_point).all(axis=1)
    )
    corrected_mean = np.where(target_valid, corrected_point.mean(axis=1), np.nan)
    raw_mean = np.where(target_valid, raw_point.mean(axis=1), np.nan)
    neighbor_dispersion = np.where(
        target_valid, np.std(corrected_point, axis=1), np.nan)

    background_point = np.full_like(corrected_point, np.nan, float)
    for frame in np.flatnonzero(tracked["pose_valid"]):
        inverse = np.linalg.inv(tracked["poses"][frame])
        mapped = (
            initial[target_columns] @ inverse[:2, :2].T + inverse[:2, 2]
        )
        displacement = mapped - initial[target_columns]
        background_point[frame] = np.einsum(
            "ki,ki->k", displacement, all_axes[target_columns])
    background_mean = np.where(
        tracked["pose_valid"], np.nanmean(background_point, axis=1), np.nan)

    count = len(initial) // 2
    anterior_columns = local_indices
    posterior_columns = local_indices + count
    pair_valid = (
        tracked["pose_valid"]
        & tracked["valid"][:, anterior_columns].all(axis=1)
        & tracked["valid"][:, posterior_columns].all(axis=1)
        & np.isfinite(tracked["corrected"][:, anterior_columns]).all(axis=(1, 2))
        & np.isfinite(tracked["corrected"][:, posterior_columns]).all(axis=(1, 2))
    )
    initial_distance = np.linalg.norm(
        initial[posterior_columns] - initial[anterior_columns], axis=1)
    current_distance = np.linalg.norm(
        tracked["corrected"][:, posterior_columns]
        - tracked["corrected"][:, anterior_columns], axis=2)
    pair_strain = current_distance / initial_distance[None] - 1.0
    radial_strain = np.where(pair_valid, pair_strain.mean(axis=1), np.nan)

    ncc = tracked["target_ncc"][:, target_columns]
    fb = tracked["target_fb"][:, target_columns]
    mean_ncc = np.array([finite_mean(row) for row in ncc])
    max_fb = np.array([
        float(np.nanmax(row)) if np.isfinite(row).any() else math.nan for row in fb
    ])

    point_vectors = []
    for column in target_columns:
        point = initial[column]
        x = int(np.clip(round(float(point[0])), 0, local_vector.shape[1] - 1))
        y = int(np.clip(round(float(point[1])), 0, local_vector.shape[0] - 1))
        point_vectors.append(local_vector[y, x])
    point_vectors = np.asarray(point_vectors, float)
    projected_truth_scale = np.einsum(
        "ki,ki->k", point_vectors, all_axes[target_columns]).mean()
    amplitude = frozen.POSITIVE_CONDITIONS.get(condition, 0.0)
    phase = np.sin(2 * np.pi * frequency_cpm * tracked["time_s"] / 60)
    truth_mean = amplitude * projected_truth_scale * phase

    evidence_valid = (
        target_valid
        & pair_valid
        & np.isfinite(background_mean)
        & np.isfinite(radial_strain)
        & np.isfinite(tracked["local_brightness"])
        & np.isfinite(tracked["global_brightness"])
    )
    return dict(
        target_valid=target_valid,
        pair_valid=pair_valid,
        evidence_valid=evidence_valid,
        corrected_point=corrected_point,
        raw_point=raw_point,
        corrected_mean=corrected_mean,
        raw_mean=raw_mean,
        background_point=background_point,
        background_mean=background_mean,
        radial_strain=radial_strain,
        neighbor_dispersion=neighbor_dispersion,
        mean_ncc=mean_ncc,
        max_fb=max_fb,
        truth_mean=truth_mean,
    )


def pairwise_neighbor_correlation(values: np.ndarray) -> float:
    correlations = []
    for index in range(values.shape[1] - 1):
        correlations.append(safe_corr(values[:, index], values[:, index + 1]))
    selected = np.asarray(correlations, float)
    selected = selected[np.isfinite(selected)]
    return float(np.median(selected)) if len(selected) else math.nan


def segment_rows(
    holdout: dict,
    condition: str,
    tracked: dict,
    evidence: dict,
    frequency_row: dict,
) -> list[dict]:
    runs = contiguous_runs(evidence["evidence_valid"], tracked["epoch"])
    if not runs:
        return [dict(
            holdout_id=holdout["holdout_id"], condition=condition,
            segment="", primary=False, start_frame="", end_frame="",
            duration_s=0.0, frames=0, expected_class=expected_class(condition),
            frequency_status=frequency_row["status"],
            frequency_cpm=frequency_row["frequency_cpm"],
            diagnostic_best_cpm=frequency_row["diagnostic_best_cpm"],
            target_corrected_range_px="", target_raw_range_px="",
            background_range_px="", radial_strain_range="",
            local_brightness_range="", global_brightness_range="",
            raw_background_corr="", corrected_background_corr="",
            corrected_local_brightness_corr="",
            corrected_global_brightness_corr="",
            corrected_radial_strain_corr="", neighbor_adjacent_corr="",
            mean_neighbor_dispersion_px="", mean_target_ncc="",
            max_target_fb_px="", mean_heldout_residual_px="",
            evidence_valid_fraction=float(evidence["evidence_valid"].mean()),
        )]
    lengths = np.array([stop - start for start, stop in runs])
    primary_index = int(np.argmax(lengths))
    rows = []
    for number, (start, stop) in enumerate(runs):
        own = slice(start, stop)
        corrected = evidence["corrected_mean"][own]
        raw = evidence["raw_mean"][own]
        background = evidence["background_mean"][own]
        local_brightness = tracked["local_brightness"][own]
        global_brightness = tracked["global_brightness"][own]
        radial = evidence["radial_strain"][own]
        rows.append(dict(
            holdout_id=holdout["holdout_id"], condition=condition,
            segment=number, primary=number == primary_index,
            start_frame=start, end_frame=stop - 1,
            duration_s=float(tracked["time_s"][stop - 1] - tracked["time_s"][start]),
            frames=stop - start, expected_class=expected_class(condition),
            frequency_status=frequency_row["status"],
            frequency_cpm=frequency_row["frequency_cpm"],
            diagnostic_best_cpm=frequency_row["diagnostic_best_cpm"],
            target_corrected_range_px=finite_range(corrected),
            target_raw_range_px=finite_range(raw),
            background_range_px=finite_range(background),
            radial_strain_range=finite_range(radial),
            local_brightness_range=finite_range(local_brightness),
            global_brightness_range=finite_range(global_brightness),
            raw_background_corr=safe_corr(raw, background),
            corrected_background_corr=safe_corr(corrected, background),
            corrected_local_brightness_corr=safe_corr(corrected, local_brightness),
            corrected_global_brightness_corr=safe_corr(corrected, global_brightness),
            corrected_radial_strain_corr=safe_corr(corrected, radial),
            neighbor_adjacent_corr=pairwise_neighbor_correlation(
                evidence["corrected_point"][own]),
            mean_neighbor_dispersion_px=finite_mean(
                evidence["neighbor_dispersion"][own]),
            mean_target_ncc=finite_mean(evidence["mean_ncc"][own]),
            max_target_fb_px=float(np.nanmax(evidence["max_fb"][own]))
                if np.isfinite(evidence["max_fb"][own]).any() else math.nan,
            mean_heldout_residual_px=finite_mean(tracked["heldout_corrected"][own]),
            evidence_valid_fraction=float(evidence["evidence_valid"].mean()),
        ))
    return rows


def expected_class(condition: str) -> str:
    return {
        "no_motion": "no_motion",
        "global_translation": "global_motion_artifact",
        "global_brightness": "global_brightness_artifact",
        "local_brightness": "local_brightness_artifact",
        "local_motion_0p3": "true_local_motion",
        "local_motion_1p0": "true_local_motion",
        "mixed_local_0p3": "true_local_motion_with_global_and_brightness",
    }[condition]


def primary_frequency(
    holdout: dict,
    tracked: dict,
    anterior_axes: np.ndarray,
    posterior_axes: np.ndarray,
    duration_s: int,
) -> tuple[dict, list[dict]]:
    rows, _ = frozen.regional_rows(
        holdout["holdout_id"], tracked, anterior_axes, posterior_axes,
        holdout["mm_per_processed_pixel"], duration_s,
    )
    target = frozen.primary_for(
        rows, holdout["target_wall"], holdout["target_region"])
    primary_rows = [row for row in rows if row["primary"]]
    return target, primary_rows


def frame_rows(
    holdout: dict,
    condition: str,
    tracked: dict,
    evidence: dict,
) -> list[dict]:
    rows = []
    for index, seconds in enumerate(tracked["time_s"]):
        rows.append(dict(
            holdout_id=holdout["holdout_id"], condition=condition,
            frame=index, time_s=seconds, epoch=int(tracked["epoch"][index]),
            pose_valid=bool(tracked["pose_valid"][index]),
            target_region_valid=bool(evidence["target_valid"][index]),
            paired_wall_valid=bool(evidence["pair_valid"][index]),
            evidence_valid=bool(evidence["evidence_valid"][index]),
            target_corrected_mean_px=evidence["corrected_mean"][index],
            target_raw_mean_px=evidence["raw_mean"][index],
            background_projected_mean_px=evidence["background_mean"][index],
            radial_strain=evidence["radial_strain"][index],
            local_brightness_median=tracked["local_brightness"][index],
            global_brightness_median=tracked["global_brightness"][index],
            neighbor_dispersion_px=evidence["neighbor_dispersion"][index],
            mean_target_ncc=evidence["mean_ncc"][index],
            max_target_fb_px=evidence["max_fb"][index],
            heldout_raw_median_px=tracked["heldout_raw"][index],
            heldout_corrected_median_px=tracked["heldout_corrected"][index],
            training_inliers=int(tracked["training_inliers"][index]),
            heldout_count=int(tracked["heldout_count"][index]),
            truth_target_mean_px=evidence["truth_mean"][index],
        ))
    return rows


def process_case(
    holdout: dict,
    duration_s: int,
) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict], list[dict], list[dict], list[str]]:
    cv2.setNumThreads(1)
    gray, initial, anterior_axes, posterior_axes = frozen.load_background(holdout)
    local_indices, target_columns, all_axes = target_layout(
        holdout, anterior_axes, posterior_axes)
    local_vector, local_envelope = frozen.build_fields(
        gray.shape, initial, all_axes, target_columns)
    own_frames, own_segments, own_cases, own_frequency, logs = [], [], [], [], []
    for number, condition in enumerate(frozen.CONDITIONS, start=1):
        started = time.time()
        tracked = track_with_evidence(
            gray, initial, local_vector, local_envelope, condition,
            holdout["true_frequency_cpm"], duration_s, target_columns)
        frequency_row, all_primary = primary_frequency(
            holdout, tracked, anterior_axes, posterior_axes, duration_s)
        evidence = build_evidence_arrays(
            tracked, initial, all_axes, local_indices, target_columns,
            condition, holdout["true_frequency_cpm"], local_vector)
        rows = segment_rows(
            holdout, condition, tracked, evidence, frequency_row)
        primary = next((row for row in rows if row["primary"]), rows[0])
        rmse, samples = coordinate_rmse(
            tracked, initial, local_vector, target_columns,
            condition, holdout["true_frequency_cpm"])
        passed = frequency_row["status"] == "exploratory_repetition_candidate"
        positive = condition in frozen.POSITIVE_CONDITIONS
        agreement = (
            bool(passed and abs(float(frequency_row["frequency_cpm"])
                                - holdout["true_frequency_cpm"]) <= 0.25)
            if positive else ""
        )
        negative_false_pass = (
            sum(row["status"] == "exploratory_repetition_candidate"
                for row in all_primary) if not positive else 0)
        case_row = dict(
            holdout_id=holdout["holdout_id"], condition=condition,
            expected_class=expected_class(condition), duration_s=duration_s,
            target_wall=holdout["target_wall"],
            target_region=holdout["target_region"],
            true_frequency_cpm=holdout["true_frequency_cpm"],
            frequency_status=frequency_row["status"],
            frequency_cpm=frequency_row["frequency_cpm"],
            diagnostic_best_cpm=frequency_row["diagnostic_best_cpm"],
            target_frequency_agreement=agreement,
            negative_region_false_pass=negative_false_pass,
            negative_region_total=6 if not positive else 0,
            coordinate_rmse_px=rmse, coordinate_samples=samples,
            target_valid_fraction=float(evidence["target_valid"].mean()),
            pair_valid_fraction=float(evidence["pair_valid"].mean()),
            evidence_valid_fraction=float(evidence["evidence_valid"].mean()),
            pose_valid_fraction=float(tracked["pose_valid"].mean()),
            primary_segment=primary.get("segment", ""),
            target_corrected_range_px=primary.get("target_corrected_range_px", ""),
            target_raw_range_px=primary.get("target_raw_range_px", ""),
            background_range_px=primary.get("background_range_px", ""),
            radial_strain_range=primary.get("radial_strain_range", ""),
            local_brightness_range=primary.get("local_brightness_range", ""),
            global_brightness_range=primary.get("global_brightness_range", ""),
            raw_background_corr=primary.get("raw_background_corr", ""),
            corrected_background_corr=primary.get("corrected_background_corr", ""),
            corrected_local_brightness_corr=primary.get(
                "corrected_local_brightness_corr", ""),
            corrected_global_brightness_corr=primary.get(
                "corrected_global_brightness_corr", ""),
            corrected_radial_strain_corr=primary.get(
                "corrected_radial_strain_corr", ""),
            neighbor_adjacent_corr=primary.get("neighbor_adjacent_corr", ""),
            mean_neighbor_dispersion_px=primary.get(
                "mean_neighbor_dispersion_px", ""),
            mean_target_ncc=primary.get("mean_target_ncc", ""),
            max_target_fb_px=primary.get("max_target_fb_px", ""),
            mean_heldout_residual_px=primary.get(
                "mean_heldout_residual_px", ""),
        )
        own_frames.extend(frame_rows(holdout, condition, tracked, evidence))
        own_segments.extend(rows)
        own_cases.append(case_row)
        for row in all_primary:
            own_frequency.append(dict(
                holdout_id=holdout["holdout_id"], condition=condition,
                wall=row["wall"], region=row["region"],
                is_target=(row["wall"] == holdout["target_wall"]
                           and row["region"] == holdout["target_region"]),
                status=row["status"], reason=row["reason"],
                frequency_cpm=row["frequency_cpm"],
                diagnostic_best_cpm=row["diagnostic_best_cpm"],
                range_mm=row["range_mm"], duration_valid_s=row["duration_s"],
                support_tier=row["support_tier"],
            ))
        elapsed = time.time() - started
        message = (
            f"{holdout['holdout_id']} {condition} {number}/7 "
            f"completed {elapsed:.1f}s"
        )
        logs.append(message)
        print(message, flush=True)
    return own_frames, own_segments, own_cases, own_frequency, logs


def condition_summary(case_rows: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    metrics = (
        "target_corrected_range_px", "target_raw_range_px", "background_range_px",
        "radial_strain_range", "local_brightness_range", "global_brightness_range",
        "raw_background_corr", "corrected_background_corr",
        "corrected_local_brightness_corr", "corrected_global_brightness_corr",
        "corrected_radial_strain_corr", "neighbor_adjacent_corr",
        "mean_neighbor_dispersion_px", "mean_target_ncc", "max_target_fb_px",
        "mean_heldout_residual_px", "coordinate_rmse_px",
        "target_valid_fraction", "evidence_valid_fraction",
    )
    output = []
    for condition in frozen.CONDITIONS:
        rows = [row for row in case_rows if row["condition"] == condition]
        item = dict(
            condition=condition,
            expected_class=expected_class(condition), cases=len(rows),
            target_frequency_agreement=sum(
                row["target_frequency_agreement"] is True for row in rows),
            positive_target_total=sum(
                row["target_frequency_agreement"] != "" for row in rows),
            negative_region_false_pass=sum(
                int(row["negative_region_false_pass"]) for row in rows),
            negative_region_total=sum(
                int(row["negative_region_total"]) for row in rows),
        )
        for metric in metrics:
            values = np.asarray([float(row[metric]) for row in rows], float)
            values = values[np.isfinite(values)]
            item[f"median_{metric}"] = float(np.median(values)) if len(values) else ""
            item[f"min_{metric}"] = float(np.min(values)) if len(values) else ""
            item[f"max_{metric}"] = float(np.max(values)) if len(values) else ""
        output.append(item)
    return output


def plot_metrics(output: Path, summaries: list[dict]) -> None:
    conditions = [row["condition"] for row in summaries]
    labels = [
        "none", "global move", "global bright", "local bright",
        "local 0.3", "local 1.0", "mixed 0.3",
    ]
    specifications = (
        ("median_raw_background_corr", "raw vs background corr"),
        ("median_corrected_local_brightness_corr", "corrected vs local brightness corr"),
        ("median_radial_strain_range", "paired-wall strain range"),
        ("median_target_corrected_range_px", "corrected target range (px)"),
        ("median_coordinate_rmse_px", "coordinate RMSE (px)"),
        ("median_neighbor_adjacent_corr", "adjacent-point corr"),
    )
    figure, axes = plt.subplots(3, 2, figsize=(13, 11), constrained_layout=True)
    x = np.arange(len(conditions))
    for axis, (field, title) in zip(axes.ravel(), specifications):
        values = [float(row[field]) if row[field] != "" else math.nan for row in summaries]
        axis.bar(x, values, color="#3b82f6")
        axis.axhline(0, color="black", linewidth=0.8)
        axis.set_title(title)
        axis.set_xticks(x, labels, rotation=30, ha="right")
        axis.grid(axis="y", alpha=0.25)
    figure.suptitle("Context evidence pilot: median across H02-H04")
    figure.savefig(output / "condition_metric_profiles.png", dpi=160)
    plt.close(figure)


def write_report(
    output: Path,
    case_rows: list[dict],
    summaries: list[dict],
    duration_s: int,
) -> None:
    positives = [
        row for row in case_rows if row["condition"] in frozen.POSITIVE_CONDITIONS]
    positive_agreement = sum(row["target_frequency_agreement"] is True for row in positives)
    negative_rows = [
        row for row in case_rows if row["condition"] in frozen.NEGATIVE_CONDITIONS]
    false_pass = sum(int(row["negative_region_false_pass"]) for row in negative_rows)
    negative_total = sum(int(row["negative_region_total"]) for row in negative_rows)
    summary_by_condition = {row["condition"]: row for row in summaries}
    global_motion = summary_by_condition["global_translation"]
    global_brightness = summary_by_condition["global_brightness"]
    local_brightness = summary_by_condition["local_brightness"]
    local_motion = summary_by_condition["local_motion_1p0"]
    lines = [
        "# 目标—周围组织证据旁路：阶段A小样本结果",
        "",
        f"H02、H03、H04；每例{duration_s}秒；7种已知真值条件。冻结追踪、刚性校正、区域支持和频率规则未修改。",
        "",
        "## 冻结基线复核",
        "",
        f"- 三类已知局部运动目标频率一致：{positive_agreement}/{len(positives)}。",
        f"- 四类阴性条件区域误报：{false_pass}/{negative_total}。本旁路尚未设阈值，因此没有声称减少误报。",
        f"- 所有坐标由冻结流程产生；旁路只读取和比较，不改写轨迹。",
        "",
        "## 关键描述性证据（3例中位数）",
        "",
        f"- 整体平移：目标原始位移与背景姿态相关系数 {float(global_motion['median_raw_background_corr']):.3f}；校正后目标范围 {float(global_motion['median_target_corrected_range_px']):.4f} px。",
        f"- 整体亮度：校正位移与局部亮度相关系数 {float(global_brightness['median_corrected_local_brightness_corr']):.3f}；径向应变范围 {float(global_brightness['median_radial_strain_range']):.5f}。",
        f"- 局部亮度：校正位移与局部亮度相关系数 {float(local_brightness['median_corrected_local_brightness_corr']):.3f}；径向应变范围 {float(local_brightness['median_radial_strain_range']):.5f}。",
        f"- 1.0 px真实局部运动：径向应变范围 {float(local_motion['median_radial_strain_range']):.5f}；相邻点相关系数 {float(local_motion['median_neighbor_adjacent_corr']):.3f}。",
        "",
        "## 解释边界",
        "",
        "- 本轮只检查连续证据能否分离竞争解释，没有根据结果临时设置分类阈值。",
        "- 相关性不是因果证据，也不是单独判定规则；证据冲突时应标为暂不可判断。",
        "- 径向距离变化只有在前后壁候选点具有可接受对应关系时才可解释为探索性形变，不能直接称为生理收缩。",
        "- 相邻点一致只能支持ROI活动或局部形变；没有稳定的时空传播证据时，不报告严格传播方向、速度或CF/FC。",
        "- 本轮是合成已知真值开发试验，不等同于真实患者视频准确率。",
        "",
        "## 文件",
        "",
        "- `frame_evidence.csv`：逐帧证据和有效掩码。",
        "- `segment_evidence.csv`：每个连续共同有效片段的证据。",
        "- `case_summary.csv`：病例×条件汇总。",
        "- `condition_summary.csv`：三例条件中位数及范围。",
        "- `regional_frequency_primary.csv`：冻结规则的六区域主要段结果。",
        "- `evidence_plots/condition_metric_profiles.png`：必要的汇总图。",
        "- `protocol.json` 和 `实际运行日志.txt`：配置、哈希和运行记录。",
    ]
    (output / "结果说明_中文.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    if args.out.exists():
        raise ValueError("Output exists; refusing to overwrite")
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True)
    plots = args.out / "evidence_plots"
    plots.mkdir()

    protocol = json.loads(args.protocol.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    catalogue = frozen.catalogue(args.videos, args.existing_results)
    resolved = frozen.resolve_locked(protocol, catalogue)
    requested = tuple(part.strip() for part in args.cases.split(",") if part.strip())
    selected = [row for case_id in requested for row in resolved
                if row["holdout_id"] == case_id]
    if tuple(row["holdout_id"] for row in selected) != requested:
        raise ValueError("Requested anonymous cases were not resolved")
    if args.duration <= 0 or args.duration > frozen.MAX_SECONDS:
        raise ValueError(f"Duration must be within 1..{frozen.MAX_SECONDS} seconds")

    recorded_protocol = dict(
        experiment="V1.3 target-surrounding context evidence pilot",
        date="2026-10-28", stage="A_known_truth_descriptive",
        cases=list(requested), duration_s=args.duration,
        conditions=list(frozen.CONDITIONS), synthetic_fps=frozen.FPS,
        classification_thresholds={},
        evidence_mode="descriptive_only_no_outcome_reclassification",
        evidence_channels=[
            "global_pose_projection", "same_wall_neighbor_consistency",
            "paired_wall_distance_strain", "local_and_global_brightness",
            "tracking_and_registration_quality",
        ],
        missingness=(
            "failed frames remain empty; no interpolation; no joining across epoch changes"
        ),
        locked_protocol_sha256=sha256_file(args.protocol),
        frozen_source_hashes={name: sha256_file(CODE / name) for name in FROZEN_FILES},
        sidecar_script_sha256=sha256_file(Path(__file__)),
        privacy="only anonymous holdout IDs are written",
    )
    (args.out / "protocol.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(recorded_protocol, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    all_frames: list[dict] = []
    all_segments: list[dict] = []
    all_cases: list[dict] = []
    all_frequency: list[dict] = []
    log_lines = [
        f"started_local=2026-10-28",
        f"cases={','.join(requested)}",
        f"duration_s={args.duration}",
        "classification_thresholds=none",
    ]
    started = time.time()
    for holdout in selected:
        frames, segments, cases, frequency_rows, logs = process_case(
            holdout, args.duration)
        all_frames.extend(frames)
        all_segments.extend(segments)
        all_cases.extend(cases)
        all_frequency.extend(frequency_rows)
        log_lines.extend(logs)

    summaries = condition_summary(all_cases)
    write_csv(args.out / "frame_evidence.csv", all_frames)
    write_csv(args.out / "segment_evidence.csv", all_segments)
    write_csv(args.out / "case_summary.csv", all_cases)
    write_csv(args.out / "condition_summary.csv", summaries)
    write_csv(args.out / "regional_frequency_primary.csv", all_frequency)
    plot_metrics(plots, summaries)
    write_report(args.out, all_cases, summaries, args.duration)
    elapsed = time.time() - started
    log_lines.extend([
        f"completed_seconds={elapsed:.3f}",
        f"opencv_version={cv2.__version__}",
        f"numpy_version={np.__version__}",
        "frozen_sources_modified=false",
        "historical_results_overwritten=false",
        "patient_identifiers_written=false",
    ])
    (args.out / "实际运行日志.txt").write_text(
        "\n".join(log_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(summaries, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--videos", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--existing-results", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--protocol", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--cases", default="H02,H03,H04")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=int, default=60)
    run(parser.parse_args())
